================================================================

You are helping a product manager triage conference talks. The PM has 15+
years of experience spanning physical and software networking, network
security, and digital identity. Their core interests are: physical datacenter
networking (optics, switching, topology, RDMA/lossless fabrics, cabling,
DC/WAN interconnect), software and cloud-native networking (eBPF, Cilium,
Kubernetes networking, Gateway API, service mesh, IPAM, BGP/datapath
internals), network security, and identity/authentication (passkeys/FIDO,
OAuth, OIDC, SPIFFE/SPIRE, workload and agentic identity). Physical DC and WAN
systems work is CORE to this PM, not adjacent — do not down-rank a paper for
being about hardware, optics, or datacenter fabric.

Below is the program for Cisco Live Europe 2026 (Amsterdam) (vendor). Read it and produce a digest
of the talks/papers/sessions most worth their attention.

## How to read this program

This is a vendor / community program (e.g. a CNCF or vendor conference).
Separate genuine technical or strategic substance from product marketing.
Down-rank pure product pitches and sponsor slots. Up-rank end-user case studies
with real numbers (scale, latency, cost, incident detail), contributor-led
architecture and internals talks, and sessions that reveal where an open-source
project is actually heading. Be skeptical of "we adopted X and it was great"
talks with no metrics or failure discussion.

## Enriching from the source material (best effort)

The program text gives you a title, authors, and an abstract per item, and
often links (`page:`, `pdf:`, `slides:`, `video:`). The abstract alone is thin — the full
paper has the methodology, real results, and limitations that make the What /
How / Where-applicable sections accurate instead of guessed.

If you have a tool to fetch URLs, then for EACH selected item (Core, Adjacent,
and Wildcard) try to retrieve the fuller source before writing it up:

- Prefer a `pdf:` link; else fetch the `page:` link (often has the full text or
  a longer description); a `video:` link is a last resort, don't transcribe it.
- Use what you retrieve to ground What/How/Where-applicable in the paper's
  actual contributions, evaluation, and stated constraints.

This is strictly best-effort. Many links fail or are paywalled:

- If a fetch fails, is paywalled, returns only an abstract, or you have no fetch
  tool, just fall back to the abstract already in the program text. That is
  fine and expected — do not stall or skip the item.
- NEVER invent paper contents, numbers, or findings from a failed or partial
  fetch. If you only had the abstract, write from the abstract.
- Note when an item's write-up is based only on the abstract, e.g. end its
  What line with "(from abstract only)", so the reader knows the depth.
- Do not let enrichment change which items you select or their tier; selection
  is from the program. Enrichment only deepens the write-up.

## What to produce

Group selected items into three relevance tiers:

- Core: directly relevant to the PM's core interests as listed above
  (physical DC networking, cloud-native networking, network security, or
  identity) as they work on them today.
- Adjacent: relevant to the broader field; useful context or near-term
  influence.
- Wildcard: 2-4 items that are NOT a direct fit for the PM's core interests
  but are worth their attention anyway because the work is genuinely novel or
  signals where the field is heading (a new technique, a surprising result, an
  emerging subfield). A Wildcard should make the PM think "I wouldn't have
  searched for this, but I'm glad I saw it." Do not pad this tier — if there
  are only two true wildcards, list two.

For each selected item provide, in this structure:
- **Title**: the `###` heading is the item's title exactly as the program
  gives it, with nothing added. Speaker(s)/author(s) with affiliation go on
  the line below it.
- No links section: the pipeline puts a links row (abstract page, paper,
  slides, recording) under every heading after you finish, from the program's
  `page:`/`pdf:`/`slides:`/`video:` lines and from verified sources. Do not
  write one, and do not link the title.
- **Why**: why this matters — the problem it addresses and why it's worth the
  PM's time.
- **What**: what the work actually is — the core contribution or finding, in
  plain terms (not the marketing abstract).
- **How**: how it works — the key technical approach or mechanism.
- **Where applicable**: the environment this applies to, and any specifics that
  constrain it. Explicitly flag if it is org-specific (e.g. "Alibaba-specific
  production setup", "requires Broadcom Tomahawk silicon", "assumes a
  single-operator WAN") versus broadly applicable. If it's a general technique,
  say so.

Then a short "Themes" section (3-6 bullets) naming patterns across the
program: what the field is collectively paying attention to this cycle.

## Rules

- Prioritize ruthlessly. 15 sharp picks beat 60 hedged ones. A large program
  should still yield a tight list.
- Skip pure product pitches, sponsor slots with no substance, and
  beginner/101 sessions unless they signal a real shift.
- If the program text is incomplete or an abstract is missing, say so for that
  item rather than inventing content.
- Do not invent talks, authors, or affiliations. Use only what's in the
  program text below.

## Output format

The digest is read on GitHub (GitHub-Flavored Markdown viewed in the repo) and
on the project's Zensical site, which renders the same file. Use only syntax
both render reliably, and follow these rules:

- Headings with `#`/`##`/`###`; bold with `**...**`; italics with `*...*`.
- Bullet lists with `-`. Put a blank line before every list, before every
  heading, and between paragraphs, or they won't render correctly.
- Indent nested lists by four spaces per level; avoid deeper nesting.
- Any link you do write is `[text](url)` — never a bare URL — and uses a URL
  exactly as the program text gives it.
- No raw HTML except the metadata comment below. Do not use heading IDs,
  footnotes, definition lists, or other non-GFM extensions.
- You may use tables and `code spans` where they genuinely help, since GitHub
  supports them; don't force them.

Begin the output with this metadata, wrapped in an HTML comment so it does not
show in the rendered view but stays in the source:

## 2026 Amsterdam

### Energy Networking Systems: Supercharge Your Network with New Power & Cooling Technologies - CENGRN-2200
Speakers: Denise Lee (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770634660913001bi7V
Cisco is at the forefront of shaping the future of energy, playing a critical role in innovating and transforming the energy landscape. We believe we must sustainably design and future-proof energy grids, buildings, networks, data centers and technology for whatever energy requirements are around the corner - whether that’s AI or whatever comes next. Cisco understands the urgency of designing and future-proofing energy systems. As technology evolves, so too must our energy infrastructure, so that we can meet emerging demands efficiently and sustainably. In this session we’ll discuss how we’re future-proofing IT infrastructure to meet AI demands from the campus to the data center through deploying pioneering advancements in Fault Managed Power (FMP) technology systems and liquid cooling solutions to support AI workloads at the Edge.

### Powering Secure AI at the Edge with Cisco Unified Edge - PSOCOM-1203
Speakers: Eugene Kim (Cisco), Ronnie Chan (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242990654001vXqH
The AI revolution is moving to the edge, where massive data streams demand a new computing vision for real-time inferencing. Discover Cisco Unified Edge, our newly launched, innovative solution that unifies compute, networking, storage, and fused security for the edge. This edge-optimized, AI-ready, and SaaS-managed platform delivers a superior user experience with unprecedented visibility, consistency, and control across diverse edge use cases. We'll explore how simplified fleet management, centralized control, and automated orchestration streamline operations, ensuring robust protection for your critical AI workloads and data as you optimize deployments and drive innovation at the edge.

### Splunk Unlocks Insights with Generative and Agentic AI Like Never Before - BRKAI-2281
Speakers: Jeff Wiedemann (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242990261001vPXA
In this session, we'll explore Splunk's latest Generative and Agentic AI capabilities including new Splunk-hosted LLMs and foundational models like our Time Series Foundation Model, which brings anomaly detection, predictive analytics, and natural language summarization to even the messiest operational data. And sure, Splunk is awesome, but we know your world doesn't stop at our dashboards. What if your machine data could play nicely with the rest of your business apps, tools, and teams? With Splunk MCP Server, it's not only possible - it's easy. You'll leave with a clear understanding of these new capabilities, how to start using them, and how to tailor them to your unique use cases.

### Unleash AI Faster: Automated Cisco AI Pod Setup with Intersight - DEVNET-2488
Speakers: Marc Abu El Ghait (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242963155001vooN
Deploying complex AI infrastructure, such as Cisco AI Pods, often involves intricate, time-consuming manual configurations. This DevNet Tech Talk will demonstrate how to dramatically accelerate and standardize the deployment of AI platforms, starting from unconfigured devices to a fully operational AI environment. The session will showcase a powerful synergy between Cisco Intersight automation capabilities and readily available DevNet community scripts. Attendees will see how these tools can be leveraged to orchestrate the entire deployment lifecycle, from initial device setup, to the final configuration of a production-ready AI platform. This approach ensures consistency, reduces human error, and significantly cuts down deployment time. You'll leave this session with ideas to help you: Automate the end-to-end deployment of complex AI infrastructure Leverage Intersight's API for rapid, consistent AI platform setup Integrate automation scripts for enhanced efficiency This session is ideal for network architects, DevOps engineers, and IT professionals involved in deploying AI/ML infrastructure. A basic understanding of Intersight and automation concepts is beneficial. The session will feature live demonstrations of the automated deployment process.

### emetry in Splunk Observability - BRKOBS-1377
Speakers: Alec Chamberlain (Cisco), Dean Lewis (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242956312001vU4a
Cloud-native teams often see symptoms in application monitoring but lose time proving the network cause. With Isovalent Tetragon (eBPF) streaming kernel-level DNS/TCP/HTTP verdicts, Splunk Observability now correlates traces, metrics, logs and real network flows so attendees can move from "slow checkout" to the exact DNS latency, TCP drops, or policy deny behind it. Attendees will learn (1) how to collect and label Tetragon telemetry using the Splunk OpenTelemetry Collector, (2) how to pivot from a slow trace span to Tetragon flows and verdicts in dashboards/navigators, and (3) how to operationalize alerting and SLOs that include network context. The session includes a live demo that follows an SRE from APM to Network Explorer and down to the precise rule/external hop causing trouble. Audience: Platform engineers/SREs with Kubernetes and OpenTelemetry familiarity; basic Tetragon concepts helpful. You'll leave this session with ideas to help you accelerate root cause, standardize cross-stack runbooks, and plan rollout patterns for mixed K8s/VM estates.

### 123 URWB: A Wireless Network Engineer's Guide - BRKIOT-2027
Speakers: Alice Masini (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243075261001vT41
Bring reliability beyond the Wi-Fi boundaries with Cisco Ultra-Reliable Wireless Backhaul (URWB) technology. This technology has proven its value as a solution for wireless connectivity for mission-critical applications. In this session, we will explore the latest Cisco annoucements regarding URWB innovations within the Cisco Wireless portfolio, and attendees will learn how to leverage on URWB and Wi-Fi across the Cisco Wireless ecosystem to boost operational efficiency and productivity. Attendees will understand the architecture, the implementation, and the capabilities the Cisco Wireless Solution can provide - together with an overview of the major use cases and verticals, where the integrated solution becomes game changer for productivity and efficiency.

### 400G, 800G, and Terabit Pluggable Optics: What You Need to Know - BRKOPT-2699
Speakers: Mark Nowell (Cisco), Errol Roberts (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243062610001vhXB
Optical interfaces are fundamental in our networks, and the technology is always being pushed to higher speeds. New developments happening in this space will enable the next generation of higher-capacity networks. This session will provide attendees with insights into the current trends and the next development areas for high-speed optical interconnects, what is driving the development, and what the priorities are for success. The session will focus on these following areas: Current 400G pluggable optics technologies and their applications Next-generation pluggable form factors for 800G and 1.6T (including their breakout use cases) New 800 Gb/s and 1.6 Tb/s Ethernet standardization Next-generation optical co-packaging developments for integrating optical interfaces into ASICs The session will cover both coherent and noncoherent optics, and will target attendees with a working knowledge of deploying optical interfaces and modules. Finally, we will also highlight some of the implications to the systems and networks that incorporate these optical interfaces.

### 5 Ways to Deploy Scalable , Secure AND Cost Sensitive Industrial IoT Solutions for Customers using Cisco Catalyst SD-WAN - BRKIOT-2990
Speakers: Pete Kavanagh (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243075061001vtoW
Utility customers and Public Service (transport, mass transit) customers need a massively scaling network automation and security. How do we address the cost aspect of such solutions? How do we optimize for rising costs of data usage under cellular plans for IoT customers? How do we plan the network and topology for maximum connectivity and minimal cost and downtime? These issues have traditionally not been a concern for enterprise customers, and hence we want to tackle these for IoT customers specifically, through a technical breakout. The session will address new optimization in SD-WAN for reduced bandwidth usage, tackling network planning for optimizing which WAN circuits to use under certain conditions for IoT customers, and finally how to create and plan for the right network topology for optimal network management . At the end of this session, an IT or OT administrator should be able to deploy Industrial IoT routers at scale, efficiently and with minimal operational cost increases .

### 5G Security: Why and How? - BRKSEC-2763
Speakers: Oussama Naffati (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243082706001vt5K
There's a popular myth that 5G networks are secure by design, but is that really the case? Join this session to uncover the reality of 5G security, including real-world attacks and challenges facing 5G networks today. Understanding the challenges is just the first step. The real question is: how can we effectively secure these networks against modern threats? Whether you're a security engineer curious about how breaches occur in mobile networks, or a mobility engineer seeking insights into the security challenges and solutions for 5G, this session is for you. Let's dive into the "why" and "how" of 5G security together.

### 6 GHz and Wi-Fi 7 Crash Course: Why and How to Deploy Them... or Not - BRKEWN-2856
Speakers: Federico Ziliotto (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242966933001v9iT
During this breakout, we will take a look at the fundamental advantages of the new 6 GHz band and the more recent Wi-Fi 7 certification, as well as their sometime marketing and less realistic promises. We will also deep dive into how to migrate to 6 GHz and/or Wi-Fi 7 from an existing deployment and what gotchas we should keep in mind when addressing different use cases (enterprise users and machines, IoT, guests, etc.). You will leave this session with practical guidelines and configuration examples, both for Catalyst and Meraki, to successfully implement 6 GHz and/or Wi-Fi 7 in your network. Or not, of course, if you'd rather not ;-)

### 7 Steps: Master the Art of Unifying Multicloud Secure Connectivity and Design - Cisco SD-WAN + Multicloud Defense - BRKENT-2283
Speakers: Prashant Tripathi (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1771608134700001rTYu
Cisco SD-WAN seamlessly orchestrates connectivity, enabling access to workloads across multiple clouds. Through native integration with leading cloud service providers like AWS, Azure, and Google Cloud, as well as middle-mile providers such as Megaport and Equinix, it ensures secure inter-site/inter-region connectivity. The solution integrates with AWS Cloud WAN, leveraging Transit Gateway architecture, collaborates with Google NCC for Google Cloud connectivity, and utilizes Azure's vWAN and vHUBs. Inter-cloud SD-WAN connectivity between diverse cloud providers is facilitated by SD-WAN Gateways. With the powerful addition of Multicloud Defense, the solution delivers comprehensive security, unifying policies across AWS, Azure, and Google Cloud for on-demand protection of workloads and inter-cloud traffic. This paves the way for converged multi-cloud networking and security.

### A Beginner's Guide to Network as Code for ACI - DEVNET-1202
Speakers: Ezgi Agcagul (Cisco), Achintya Murali (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242993117001v6fJ
Infrastructure as Code is a game changer for network deployment, management, and operations.This beginner-level session is designed to guide attendees through the complete process of Network as Code with ACI, starting from an empty YAML file. Attendees will learn, step by step, how to automate Cisco ACI operations and management, with no prior automation experience assumed. While CI/CD pipelines offer great benefits to operating a modern network, they are often perceived as complex or difficult to get started with. Cisco's Network as Code approach, with the DevNet ecosystem, serves a starting point to this automation journey. All code in the session will be built using publicly available examples, allowing attendees to extend what is covered into their own environments. By the end of the session, attendees will understand how to structure Infrastructure as Code for Cisco ACI, how CI/CD integrates into day-to-day network operations, and how to leverage Network as Code with confidence.

### A Day in the Life of Marty OT Administrator: Securing, Automating, and Assuring OT Operations with Cisco Catalyst Center - BRKIOT-2876
Speakers: Kevin Wood (Cisco), Abubakar Maaroof (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242947138001vuED
Industrial networks are under pressure to deliver uptime, security, and agility while converging with enterprise IT standards. OT network administrators must manage rugged infrastructure, collaborate with IT, and keep pace with compliance requirements all without disrupting production. This session follows Marty, an OT network administrator, through a typical workday in a modern manufacturing plant. Attendees will see how Cisco Catalyst Center enables Marty to: Use site-based role-based access control (SRBAC) to securely share management access with his IT counterpart Onboard, automate, and secure the industrial network with simplified Day-0 and Day-N automation workflows Streamline software image management to keep devices current Rely on AI-assisted guided remediation for faster troubleshooting Configure Resilient Ethernet Protocol (REP) rings for industrial redundancy Maintain compliance with built-in reporting and audit capabilities This session shows how Catalyst Center bridges IT and OT, combining automation, assurance, and security visibility to meet Industry 4.0 demands. You'll leave this session with ideas to help you collaborate securely across IT and OT teams, automate industrial operations without downtime and achieve compliance while improving operational resilience.

### A Network Engineer's Blueprint for ACI Forwarding - BRKDCN-3900
Speakers: Joseph Young (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243045961001vpwh
As the Cisco Application Centric Infrastructure (ACI) list of features continues to expand and encompass more data center architectures, it is more important than ever to have a good understanding of how a packet is forwarded end to end. This session will provide a deep dive into how ACI forwards traffic. Important questions that will be covered include: How do the basic forwarding mechanisms in ACI work (VXLAN, TEP reachability, end point learning, etc.)? In what ways does ACI forwarding differ from legacy environments? What do the forwarding-related configuration options do? How can an engineer effectively prove what is happening to a packet? At the end of this session, attendees should be able to explain in detail, how ACI would handle a specific flow, as well as use available tools to debug and verify what is happening. Attendees should already have a deep understanding of routing and switching fundamentals in legacy environments and knowledge of basic ACI constructs (endpoint groups, contracts, bridge domains, VRFs).

### A New Era of Cross-domain Diagnostics with AI and Agentic Systems - BRKETI-2172
Speakers: Frank Brockners (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1771608137349001rkJ6
This session delves into the realm of troubleshooting and explores future potentials, asking: can AI diagnose problems like a human and assist with complex, cross-domain troubleshooting tasks? This exploration will proceed through three incremental steps: analyzing logs with Large Language Models (LLMs), analyzing streaming router telemetry with AI, and employing an agentic AI approach to diagnose complex, cross-domain/cross-vendor deployments involving applications, middleware, servers, and networks. Note: This session is an extended version of AI-2006 - "Applying AI/ML Methods to Diagnose Network Issues Using Telemetry Data"

### A Quantum Leap in Networking: Cisco's Blueprint for Tomorrow's Infrastructure - CENETI-1290
Speakers: Ramana Kompella (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242994138001v0op
Quantum networking is no longer a distant concept-it's a transformative technology reshaping the future of enterprise infrastructure. This session will explore how quantum networking, a vision we intend to realize in the next five years, can unlock unprecedented computational power and security capabilities. We'll dive into real-world advancements, including Cisco's groundbreaking Quantum Network Entanglement Chip and Network-Aware Quantum Compiler, which are paving the way for scalable, distributed quantum systems. Attendees will learn how quantum networking enhances classical systems today, from secure communications to advanced decision-making, while preparing enterprises for the quantum era. Join us to discover why understanding quantum networking is critical for staying ahead and how our partnerships with quantum leaders like IBM can deliver a strategic advantage. Don't miss this opportunity to understand the advantages and considerations your organization can take to harness the power of distributed quantum computing.

### A Short History of Time in Industrial Systems - Synchronizing Critical Infrastructure Elements with PTP - BRKIOT-2269
Speakers: Marcin Szreter (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242965934001vQwR
This session explores time synchronization in industrial networks, with a particular focus on the Precision Time Protocol (PTP). As the industry accelerates towards IEC 61850-based substation architectures, precise and reliable time distribution has become essential for safe and efficient operation. We begin with the fundamentals - introducing key time-synchronization technologies, PTP profiles, and their implementation across Cisco industrial platforms. We will then share best practices derived from validated designs and real-world customer deployments. Finally, we will discuss the latest developments aimed at ensuring time resilience, including strategies for maintaining accurate synchronization in scenarios where GPS/GNSS services are degraded or unavailable.

### A Song of ISE and Posture: Advanced Deployment and Troubleshooting - BRKSEC-3077
Speakers: Andrea Bertorello (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243090089001vggE
Endpoint Security is nowadays a pillar of all organizations, and increasing their compliance checks before connecting to the organization networks is a trend. Your endpoint security is your armor. This armor is not a single piece of steel but instead, it's a set of security components, where a single weakness in one of them may destroy the efficiency of the entire system. ISE posture services help, to ensure that your endpoints armor is always in good shape. During the session, attendees will have the opportunity to explore advanced posture scenarios and settings, such as load balancing, posture session management, MFA and bidirectional-posture. A demo will provide in-depth information on these topics. Furthermore, after the session, attendees will possess the knowledge and skills to troubleshoot any posture-related issues using log references, to solve the problem of now from where to start if something is not working.

### A to I: AI Workloads from Architecture to Implementation with Cisco Compute - BRKCOM-1578
Speakers: Riccardo Tortorici (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242960779001vwmv
Join us for a technical deep dive into how Cisco compute platforms - spanning dense GPU servers, rack mounts, and blades - support the full spectrum of AI workloads, from large-scale training to fine-tuning and real-time inference. Using three detailed user stories, we'll walk through best practices for architecting, deploying, and optimizing each use case, showcasing how to align workload requirements with the right platform. This session will equip you to confidently navigate the available platforms and make informed decisions for robust, production-ready AI environments

### ACI Layer 4-7 Policy-Based Redirect (PBR) Deep Dive and Tips - BRKDCN-3982
Speakers: Minako Higuchi (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243047886001vS5J
When designing network security in a data center, have you ever wondered where to enforce security policies and how to steer traffic to get firewalls to inspect the traffic? How would the use of Cisco ACI affect your Layer 4-7 services such as firewalls, IPSs, and load balancers? This session will provide a deep dive into ACI Layer 4-7 service integration capabilities, especially PBR service graphs. It will cover ACI contract security capabilities, how forwarding works to steer traffic, design options with common use cases, and multi-location data center design options. The basic assumption is that attendees will have a solid knowledge of Cisco ACI and its functional components, including EPGs, contracts, VRF, bridge domains, Multi-Pod, Multi-Site, etc.

### ACI Multi-Site Architecture and Deployment - BRKDCN-2980
Speakers: John Weston (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243045736001vNXK
This session provides an in-depth view of the Cisco ACI Multi-Site architecture, beginning with an overview of various multi-site use cases for interconnecting multiple ACI fabrics in both enterprise and service provider environments, with a particular focus on data center interconnect (DCI). It covers application, operational, and availability considerations for Cisco ACI Multi-Site. Key architectural topics include inter-site network (ISN) deployment considerations, Nexus Dashboard Orchestrator architecture, extending network and segmentation across multiple ACI fabrics encompassing both control and data planes, connectivity to external Layer 3 domains, and network services integration. The session has been updated to incorporate enhancements in Nexus Dashboard 4.1, including support for Endpoint Security Groups (ESGs) in Multi-Site scenarios. Attendees should have at least a basic understanding of ACI to gain the most from this session.

### ACI Objects: How to Avoid Getting Your Configuration Wires Crossed - BRKDCN-2647
Speakers: Davis Creemer (Cisco), Chris Merkel (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243046840001vhrW
This session is designed for engineers and architects who spend time designing and implementing ACI-based solutions. With a focus on best-practice and scalability, we will review common design challenges and how to avoid them. The ACI object model provides a powerful way to set policy once and reuse it as needed, but knowing how to use these objects can be challenging. It can also be easy to find yourself in an endless configuration sprawl. From the AAEP and pDom to the EPG and the layer 3 out, we will talk about many of the commonly used objects, their relationships, their purposes, and how to optimally reuse them for truly scalable solutions. Attendees should have a general understanding of ACI and some familiarity with its common objects.

### ACI Troubleshooting: Advanced Layer 3 Out Features - BRKDCN-3678
Speakers: Roland Ducomble (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243044191001vZJY
A layer 3 outside (L3Out) network configuration in Cisco Application Centric Infrastructure (ACI) controls the external connectivity toward the fabric. In this session, we will build on earlier Cisco Live L3Out sessions and dive deep into features such as route control and transit routing. We will cover this from both a single-pod and multi-pod perspective, and explore troubleshooting, best practices, and the inner workings.

### ACI: The Foundation of an Internal Private Cloud - BRKDCN-2984
Speakers: Steve Sharman (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243041469001vKDc
There are thousands of customers globally who have successfully deployed ACI fabrics and operate them as "just another network," but what if you could operate your ACI fabric as programmable private cloud infrastructure? In this session, we will look at how you can operate your ACI fabric as the foundation of an internal private cloud. We will look at how to migrate services onto an ACI fabric (network centric) and then implement segmentation (application centric). We will look at how to use endpoint security groups to wrap security around endpoints within a VRF. We will then see how we can block east/west traffic within a hypervisor, and finally, we'll dynamically add in firewalls to provide targeted layer 7 control.

### AI & Cyber Risk: Mastering Vulnerability Management at Scale - ITLGEN-2008
Speakers: David Worth (Cisco)

### AI and the Future of Compliance: Can You Audit an LLM? - AI-1646
Speakers: Vinay Saini (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242978823001vnVX
As organizations rapidly adopt Large Language Models (LLMs) to drive automation, customer engagement, and decision support, a critical question arises: can these AI systems be audited for compliance, security, and trustworthiness? Unlike traditional software, LLMs are non-deterministic, opaque, and often externally hosted - posing significant challenges to existing regulatory frameworks such as NIST, ISO/IEC, and sector-specific mandates like HIPAA or PCI DSS. This session explores the emerging compliance landscape around LLMs, highlighting gaps in current standards, real-world risk scenarios, and the evolving requirements from global regulators. We'll discuss what auditing an LLM truly entails - from tracing training data and monitoring inference logs to securing prompt access and testing for bias or model drift. Attendees will leave with a practical understanding of how to integrate auditability into their AI pipelines, prepare for upcoming AI regulations, and design future-proof AI governance strategies. Target Audience: CISOs, compliance leaders, AI/ML architects, security strategists, and enterprise risk teams.

### AI for an Inclusive Future: Transforming Trends into Impact - IF-1667
Speakers: Shadnaz Nia (Cisco)

Along the way, we will highlight how design choices around data, context, language accessibility, and human‑in‑the‑loop operations make the difference between AI that merely optimizes and AI that genuinely includes.​ Attendees will leave with a practical lens for evaluating their own AI initiatives: how to frame problems around the most vulnerable users, how to use telemetry and observability to detect inequitable outcomes early, and how to operationalize inclusive AI patterns using Cisco and Splunk solutions without needing a research lab or a massive data science team.

### AI-Powered Cisco: Accelerate your Managed Services - PSOMS-1146
Speakers: Rupal Hada (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1771608147723001rCw8
Artificial Intelligence (AI) is transforming how organizations operate delivering automation, insights, and security at unprecedented speed and scale. Cisco integrates AI deeply across its portfolio, from networking and collaboration to security and observability, enabling customers to simplify operations, improve experiences, and protect their digital ecosystems. This paper highlights key use cases demonstrating how Cisco's AI-powered solutions help businesses accelerate innovation and resilience. 1. AI-Driven Networking: Smarter, Self-Healing Infrastructure Cisco's AI-native networking capabilities in Catalyst Center, Meraki Cloud, and ThousandEyes bring predictive intelligence to the network edge. Use Cases: Predictive Insights and Root Cause Analysis: AI models analyze telemetry from millions of devices to predict failures and optimize performance before users are impacted. Automated Configuration and Troubleshooting: Cisco Catalyst Center uses AI-driven recommendations to detect anomalies, automate fixes, and improve network uptime. Wireless Experience Optimization: Meraki leverages machine learning to automatically adjust RF settings, balance client loads, and optimize coverage for dynamic environments. Business Impact: Reduced downtime, improved user experience, and up to 40% faster issue resolution. 2. AI-Powered Security: Faster Threat Detection and Response Cisco integrates AI into its Security Cloud, combining threat intelligence from Talos with machine learning to detect sophisticated attacks in real time. Use Cases: Anomaly and Behavior Analytics: AI models identify deviations in user or device behavior, flagging insider threats and zero-day attacks. Automated Response: Cisco XDR uses AI to correlate events across email, endpoints, and the network, accelerating incident response. Adaptive Policy Enforcement: Secure Access (SASE) solutions apply AI to dynamically adjust access controls based on risk context. Business Impact: Faster detection, reduced analyst workload, and stronger zero-trust enforcement. 3. AI-Enhanced Collaboration: Smarter, More Inclusive Meetings Cisco Webex AI Assistant brings contextual intelligence to collaboration. Use Cases: Real-Time Transcription and Translation: Webex automatically translates and summarizes meetings across 100+ languages. Meeting Summaries and Action Items: AI captures key takeaways and tasks, ensuring everyone stays aligned. Noise Removal and Visual Enhancement: AI filters distractions and enhances video quality for hybrid workers. Business Impact: Increased productivity, better meeting accessibility, and reduced cognitive load on teams. 4. AI in Observability: From Data Overload to Insight Cisco's Full-Stack Observability (FSO) platform uses AI and machine learning to correlate metrics, traces, and logs across multi-cloud environments. Use Cases: Proactive Performance Management: AI detects anomalies before they affect applications or end users. Business Transaction Insights: ML algorithms correlate technical performance with business KPIs for smarter prioritization. Dynamic Baselines: Continuous learning establishes normal behavior and flags deviations in real time. Business Impact: Accelerated root cause analysis, optimized digital experience, and improved ROI on IT investments. 5. AI for Sustainability and Operational Efficiency AI insights from Cisco Networking Cloud and Energy Management solutions help organizations meet sustainability goals. Use Cases: Power Optimization: AI identifies idle devices and automatically reduces energy consumption. Smart Building Management: Meraki sensors and AI analytics optimize HVAC, lighting, and occupancy patterns. Business Impact: Lower carbon footprint and reduced operational costs. Conclusion Cisco's AI strategy centers on trust, transparency, and responsible innovation empowering organizations to use AI confidently while maintaining privacy and compliance. Across networking, security, collaboration, and observability, Cisco is not just embedding AI; it's creating AI-native platforms that learn, adapt, and evolve with your business.

Join us to discover how this AI-first solution enhances threat detection and response, ensuring your SOC stays ahead in the rapidly evolving cybersecurity landscape.

### APIs vs MCP for Enhancing AI Workflows - DEVNET-1712
Speakers: Shereen Bellamy (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1771608141286001r8uQ
The rapid evolution of AI-driven workflows demands more efficient and standardized integration methods. Traditional APIs, while foundational, often present challenges such as inconsistency, high maintenance, and integration complexity. The emerging Model Context Protocol (MCP) offers a streamlined, uniform protocol designed to simplify AI agent interactions with diverse tools and services. This session explores the capabilities of both traditional APIs and MCP, highlighting their differences and how MCP addresses key limitations in AI workflows. Attendees will understand how MCP's client/server architecture enables seamless multi-tool orchestration, built-in security with OAuth enhancements, and reduced integration toil compared to conventional APIs. You'll leave this session with ideas to help you: Understand the architectural distinctions between APIs and MCP and their impact on AI agent integration. Leverage MCP's resource discovery, elicitation flows for human-in-the-loop interactions, and secure OAuth-based authentication to enhance AI workflows. Evaluate practical use cases across DevOps, NetOps, and SecOps where MCP simplifies automation, monitoring, and incident response compared to traditional API approaches. This session is designed for attendees with beginner to intermediate experience in API integration and AI workflows. Familiarity with REST APIs and basic AI agent concepts is recommended.

### Advanced Techniques for Successful Cellular Connectivity Implementation - BRKIOT-3624
Speakers: Emmanuel Tychon (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243026099001vS13
Cellular connectivity has evolved from a backup solution to a primary network access method for enterprise and Industrial IoT deployments. With 5G proliferation and decreasing data costs, organizations are increasingly relying on cellular networks for remote locations and mobile scenarios where traditional connectivity isn't viable. However, the path from cellular promise to production reality is fraught with challenges. Module selection, bandwidth optimization, frequency band compatibility, and configuration complexity create a perfect storm for deployment failures. Add service provider nuances, SIM card complications, firmware bugs, and configuration errors, and it's no wonder that cellular projects often stall or generate endless support tickets. This comprehensive session cuts through the complexity with battle-tested best practices for successful cellular deployments. You'll discover how to avoid common pitfalls that derail projects, master systematic troubleshooting methodologies, and implement proper logging strategies that turn mysterious connectivity issues into solvable problems. Whether you're planning your first cellular deployment, or struggling with existing implementations, this session provides the practical knowledge and proven frameworks that transform cellular connectivity from a source of frustration into a reliable foundation for your critical applications. What you'll learn: Strategic module and service provider selection criteria Configuration best practices that prevent common deployment failures Systematic troubleshooting workflows for rapid issue resolution Advanced logging and monitoring techniques for proactive maintenance

### Agentic AI and Observability: Let the System Work for You - BRKOBS-1164
Speakers: Luca Relandini (Cisco), Leila Reyhani (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242997273001vOgm
In today's complex digital environments, traditional observability tools struggle to keep pace with the volume and complexity of data, leading to longer mean time to detect (MTTD) and mean time to resolve (MTTR) incidents. This session explores how Agentic AI revolutionizes observability by autonomously orchestrating data analysis and troubleshooting workflows, enabling systems to work proactively for you. Discover how Splunk Observability uses agents to accelerate incident detection, root cause analysis, and remediation. See how Cisco AI Canvas, leveraging agentic AI, will automate the entire troubleshooting lifecycle - reducing operational complexity, minimizing alert fatigue, and democratizing observability for all users. Join us to learn how agentic AI empowers site reliability engineers, architects, and IT leaders to transform observability practices with faster, smarter, and cross-domains insights. See examples demonstrating dramatic productivity gains, including reducing triage times by up to 50%, and how AI assistants embedded across Cisco and Splunk products enable seamless, natural language interactions to surface critical insights and guided recommendations. Let the system work for you - embrace agentic AI to transform your observability practice and accelerate your digital resilience journey.

### Agentic AI for Networkers - BRKOPS-1327
Speakers: Frank Brockners (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242986894001vDNE
As Yuval Noah Harari notes, humanity's unique ability to collaborate in large groups is what sets us apart from all other species. Today, generative AI is enabling a similar leap - enabling software to collaborate with software in natural language - similar to humans: Agentic AI. Agentic AI refers to systems composed of multiple AI agents that can autonomously reason, plan, collaborate, and act in pursuit of complex goals, with minimal or no human supervision. This session explores the past, present, and future of agentic AI, with a strong emphasis on practical, demo-driven insights into how these systems support and enhance networking use cases. We begin with a review of current AI assistants in networking, then transition to a deeper dive into how agents can coordinate, reflect, leverage tools, and work together to solve problems. Demos will showcase agentic AI automating workflows, customizing user interactions (e.g., AgenticOps with AI Canvas), and even reasoning across unfamiliar data schemas in cross-domain and cross-organizational troubleshooting scenarios. The session concludes by exploring distributed agentic AI - what some call the Internet of Agents. We'll discuss both the infrastructure and semantic layers: how agents are discovered, connected, secured, and observed using protocols like MCP or A2A, and frameworks like AGNTCY.ORG; and how they simulate human-like cognition through structured reasoning, group memory, goal alignment, and interaction patterns. Attendees will leave with a clear understanding of the current landscape of agentic AI, its real-world applications in networking, and where this transformative technology is headed next.

### Agentic AI in Action: Redefining Business Practices - ITLGEN-2001
Speakers: Carlos Pereira (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243032700001vyHm

### Agentic Observability and Evaluation: from Monitoring to Predictability and Control for Agentic Enterprise Applications - BRKOBS-2522
Speakers: Giovanna Carofiglio (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242996877001vlX4
As enterprises increasingly adopt agentic AI in their applications in the form of MAS (multi-agent systems) with distributed and interconnected agents (IoA, Internt of Agents), the need for robust, interoperable observability and evaluation solutions becomes critical. This session presents the latest advancements from an industry-leading collaboration, including Cisco and Splunk, aimed at defining an open, unified standard for agentic observability developed in the open source Agncty collective (agcnty.org) and actively discussed for standardisation in OpenTelemetry GenAI forums. The session will also cover critical aspects of explainability, evaluation, predictability and control tailored to enterprise agentic environments. It will introduce a new charter for agentic agentic APM (Application Performance Monitoring) with features such as agentic quality/cost tracking, impact assessment, and anomaly detection specifically designed for agentic applications. Special attention will be devoted to evaluation approaches whether leveraging LLM-as-a-Judge or active testing and evaluation to optimize agent performance across diverse deployment scenarios. The session will review the current landscape of observability and evaluation-spanning both open-source and commercial solutions-and culminate in a live demo showcasing end-to-end agentic observability and evaluation using Agncty open-source components. Attendees will gain actionable insights into building enterprise-grade, future-proof monitoring and control for next-generation agentic applications.

### AgenticOps for Cisco Secure Firewall : Autonomous, Self-Healing Security with a Digital Workforce - BRKSEC-2509

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243017239001vy6i

Cisco's AIOps for Firewalls has begun to ease this burden with predictive insights and guided remediations, but the next leap is here. Cisco's AgenticOps for Firewalls is the next evolution of AIOps. This introduces intelligent, self-healing agents that go beyond recommendations to autonomously provisioning configurations such as VPNs, apply them, and help troubleshoot issues end-to-end. In this session, attendees will learn how AIOps transforms firewall health management, how guided remediations evolve into self-healing actions, and how AgenticOps establishes a digital workforce capable of handling end to end tasks, thereby reducing MTTD/MTTR and cutting operational overhead. Through real-world use cases, attendees will see how these innovations simplify complexity, strengthen security posture, and free teams to focus on higher-value priorities.

### All You Need To Know About App Hosting - BRKOPS-2490

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243094864001vLVS

### All You Need to Know about Forwarding on Cisco 8000 Series Secure Routers - BRKARC-2886
Speakers: David Roten (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1771608126733001r7zj
Cisco 8000 Series Secure routers are the latest evolution of Cisco's enterprise routing platforms. Cisco 8000 Series Secure routers build on the strength of the previous Catalyst 8000, Catalyst 8500L, ASR 1000, ISR 4000 and ISR1000 platforms. Upper end Cisco 8500 Secure Router platforms still take advantage of the QFP ASIC. Using the QFP platforms as a foundation, this session will cover the x86 based data forwarding plane of the Cisco 8400, 8300, 8200 and 8100 Secure Routers. The session will be a deep dive into the specific behaviors and troubleshooting techniques in high utilization scenarios of the Cisco 8000 Secure Routers using QFP and x86. We will go into the details of the load-based and flow-based architectures that are used on all the Cisco 8000 Secure Router platforms. This deep dive will help attendees understand which platforms are optimal for their network, and how to best take advantage of layer 3 and layer 2 forwarding features. Aspects of previous Catalyst 8000 Series Routers will also be addressed for historical reference and completeness.

### An Introduction to eBPF for Network Security Engineers - DEVNET-2927
Speakers: Kyle Winters (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243055571001vC3X
In today's rapidly evolving cybersecurity landscape, staying ahead of threats requires cutting-edge tools and techniques. Extended Berkeley Packet Filter (eBPF) is transforming how network security engineers monitor and protect systems in real-time. This session will introduce eBPF's powerful capabilities for network security, offering live examples tailored for security professionals. Attendees will learn how to leverage eBPF to monitor network traffic and detect suspicious patterns and potential attacks, trace system calls to identify unauthorized access attempts, and block malicious connections based on predefined security policies. You'll leave this session with ideas to help you enhance your security monitoring, detect anomalies, and respond to threats more effectively. Understanding of basic network protocols and Linux system internals is recommended. The session includes live demonstrations of eBPF in action, showcasing practical use cases and real-time analysis techniques. You will also learn how eBPF is leveraged by Cisco Hypershield, a groundbreaking security architecture designed to defend modern, AI-scale data centers. Join us to explore how eBPF can be a game-changer in your security toolkit and empower you to safeguard your network with advanced, real-time monitoring capabilities.

### Architecting Layer 4-7 Network Services in a Multi-Tenant Data Center with VXLAN EVPN - BRKDCN-2974
Speakers: Matthias Wessendorf (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243048296001vbBZ
With VXLAN BGP EVPN data center fabrics, we now embrace integrated multi-tenant capabilites. With this, not only is the requirement for tenant separation present, but also interconnectivity and attachment of layer 4-7 services. This session specifically covers requirements and approaches for deploying multi-tenant data center fabric, with a focus on how to archive multi-tenancy within data plane and control plane around VxLAN with BGP EVPN. Further, the session will cover integration of layer 4-7 services to satisfy the needs of tenant interconnectivity, security appliance, and load balancers attachement. We will define the notion of multi-tenancy and how it relates to small, medium, and large enterprises, as well as discuss hardware and software technologies as they map to multi-tenant data centers. Subsequently, the integrating of layer 4-7 services into VxLAN with BGP EVPN is approached. This session will focus exclusivly on data center fabric deployments with VXLAN BGP EVPN including EVPN multi-site.

### Are You Prepared for the Next Typhoon? - BRKGEN-2499
Speakers: Paul Giralt (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243096256001vCJN
Global threat actors are increasingly targeting network infrastructure through sophisticated, coordinated attacks. Come learn how the Salt Typhoon threat actors targeted major telecommunications companies, and what you can do to protect your own networks from the same tactics, techniques, and procedures. While these attacks focused on service providers, the lessons apply to networks of all types. Also learn what changes Cisco is making in upcoming software releases to make the network more resilient to these types of attacks. This session begins with insights from Cisco Talos, highlighting the nature the Salt Typhoon attacks to deepen your understanding of the attack surface and the need for urgent action to protect your infrastructure. We will then introduce Cisco's commitment to Resilient Infrastructure which plans to implement a variety of changes over the coming year that will make Cisco's devices more secure by design. We will discuss specific changes being introduced across the product portfolio including IOS XE, IOS XR, NX OS, Identity Services Engine (ISE), and ASA / FTD as well as controllers like Cisco Catalyst Center, Meraki Dashboard, and Catalyst SD-WAN. Learn how to prepare for these changes by implementing hardening best practices and understanding the operational implications of software that embodies secure-by-design principles.

### Are You Ready for AgenticOps? AI Skills for Networking and Cybersecurity - CISCOU-2324
Speakers: Giuseppe Cinque (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242982527001v33c
AgenticOps is rapidly emerging as AI copilots and autonomous agents move from concept to reality, transforming the daily work of security and infrastructure teams. But which roles are changing, what new skills are essential, and how can professionals prepare? Drawing on two years of research done inside the AI Workforce Consortium, this session explores the real-world impact of AgenticOps. Using current job-market data and practitioner insights, we map the shifting landscape of cybersecurity and networking careers and offer practical guidance to help you stay ahead. The AI Workforce consortium, is an industry wide initiative created by Cisco, includes Accenture, Cornerstone, Google, IBM, Intel, Indeed, Eightfold, Microsoft and SAP.

### Atomic Config Replace on Catalyst: A New Era in Network Management - BRKDEV-2188

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243053940001vA7y
Join us to modernize your Catalyst device configuration automation with the new Atomic Config Replace (ACR) feature. We will cover ACR's newest capabilities including config diff, rollback, and more. We will also discuss what ACR is and demonstrate its functionality. Key topics include config management and validation, resolving replace and merge conflicts, and config rollback options. The ACR approach ensures full configuration application, preventing partial-config states. Attend this session to become an expert in automating, configuring, and managing your Catalyst devices.

### Automate Cisco Catalyst Center with Cisco Workflows - BRKOPS-2379
Speakers: Keith Baldwin (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243068184001vCHI
Significantly reduce network deployment times and enhance accuracy through automation with Cisco Workflows. Cisco Workflows transforms network automation and orchestration. This innovative solution seamlessly integrates Cisco and third-party automation controllers using REST-API, PowerShell, and SSH. Cisco Workflows enables users to create comprehensive workflows for end-to-end orchestration across various products, such as automating a branch SD-WAN router, Catalyst switching, and Meraki APs, all within a unified network interface. This session provides an in-depth overview of the platform, demonstrating its capabilities with on-prem management platforms and showcasing complete network automation. Additionally, it highlights the benefits of a collaborative DevOps approach in overcoming deployment challenges and boosting operational efficiency. The session also showcases the integration of Cisco products with low-code/no-code solutions to streamline IT processes and enable seamless project deployment.

### Automate Your Catalyst SD-WAN with Network as Code - BRKENT-2115
Speakers: Tomasz Zarski (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1771608133489001rOXk
Network as Code enables organizations to automate network management and achieve greater agility, significantly reducing manual effort and errors. This session demonstrates how to apply the Network as Code approach to automate Catalyst SD-WAN, accelerating migration and change implementation by up to 90%. Attendees will learn how to leverage automation to define data models, perform automated pre-change validation, deploy configurations using Terraform, and manage network changes through a full CI/CD pipeline. Understanding of basic Catalyst SD-WAN concepts is expected.

### Automate the Deployment of a Wireless Network with the Help of Cisco Catalyst Center - BRKOPS-2402
Speakers: Christian Gauer (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243070445001vGJg
Join us in this session as we delve into the practicalities of deploying a Wi-Fi 6/6E network using Catalyst 9800 controllers and Catalyst access points, facilitated by Cisco Catalyst Center (formerly Cisco DNA Center). Our discussion will guide you through the process of designing for Software-Defined Networking (SDN) and executing an effective network deployment. Learn how to harness plug-and-play for effortless onboarding of wireless controllers and access points. We'll also explore how to position access points using maps from Ekahau imports. Finally, we'll focus on operating your network, taking full advantage of the monitoring capabilities offered by Catalyst Center, and efficiently managing software updates.

### Automate your NX-OS Data Center fabrics using Ansible, NETCONF and YANG - DEVNET-2143
Speakers: Gerard Sheehan (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243051511001vpAw
NETCONF and YANG are quickly becoming the industry standard transport and encoding, to manage network fabrics. Come and see how you can manage your NX-OS fabric, from Day 1-N with NETCONF and YANG, using DIY in-house tools, or leveraging open-source tools such as Ansible.

### Automating ACI, SD-WAN, and Campus (SDA and IOS-XE) with Service-as-Code: Simplifying Cross-Domain Integration and Policy Consistency - BRKXAR-3022
Speakers: Zig Zsiga (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243071521001vB0y
Enterprises run ACI in the data center, SD-WAN in the WAN, and a mix of SDA and standalone IOS-XE at the campus edge. Integrating these domains consistently across the data, control, and policy planes remains a major challenge. This session presents a prescriptive architecture for AC SD-WAN Campus integration and introduces Service-as-Code (SAC) as the automation model that operationalizes it. Service-as-Code enables declarative configuration across controller-based (ACI, SD-WAN, SDA) and direct-to-device (IOS-XE) environments using Cisco-managed data models. It integrates with Git-based CI/CD workflows for incremental change and applies automated pre and post validation to enforce compliance. The framework also delivers cross-domain orchestration from a single interface to ensure consistent policy, secure segmentation, and operational efficiency across the enterprise network. Attendees will learn how to design a cohesive multidomain architecture and automate it end to end using Service-as-Code to achieve unified policy enforcement and simplified operations at scale.

### Automating Cisco NSO Deployments with Kubernetes Operator - DEVNET-2928
Speakers: Cassandra Barreto Villegas (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243007689001vXDh
What if you could describe exactly how you want NSO to run and deploy it with a single command? Cisco Network Services Orchestrator (NSO) simplifies managing complex, multivendor service provider networks. Traditionally deployed as virtual machines or bare metal, NSO setups can be complex and time-consuming. NSO has recently evolved to support native container deployment, moving closer to a fully cloud-native application and enabling greater scalability and integration. This session demonstrates how Kubernetes Operators enhance NSO deployments and lifecycle management. Attendees will learn to use Kubernetes APIs to streamline NSO provisioning, configuration, and scaling, reducing manual effort and operational complexity. By the end of this session, attendees will know how to automate NSO deployments in cloud-native environments using Kubernetes Operators, simplify multivendor network orchestration with containerized NSO, and improve operational efficiency by integrating NSO automation with Kubernetes. This session is designed for attendees comfortable with Cisco NSO, Docker, and Kubernetes at an intermediate level. The source code used during the session can be found at the following link for hands-on practice: https://github.com/carlosgrillet/nso-operator.

### Automating Cisco UCS Unified Edge Infrastructure with Ansible - DEVNET-2290
Speakers: Michael Maurer (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243009120001vZOl
This session will provide an overview of managing Cisco UCS Unified Edge infrastructure, focusing on network and compute resource automation using the Red Hat Ansible Automation Platform. Attendees will learn how to leverage Cisco UCS, Cisco Intersight, and Ansible Certified Content Collections to streamline Day 0, Day 1, and Day 2 operations, enabling scalable, secure, and efficient infrastructure management at the edge.

### Automating Configuration on Hybrid Mesh Firewall - DEVNET-2406
Speakers: Karmanya Dadhich (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243008292001vN9M
Cisco Hybrid Mesh Firewall (HMF) secures hybrid environments by integrating physical, virtual, cloud, container, and IoT workloads under a unified management system. This session explores how HMF combines Firewall, Multicloud Defense, and Secure Workload within Cisco Security Cloud Control, simplifying cybersecurity management with Zero Trust and comprehensive security for cloud and on-premises workloads, including Kubernetes, VMs, and bare metals. Focused on automating HMF using Terraform, the session includes a live demo of Secure Workload, Firewall, and Multicloud Defense automation. The bigger aim is to demonstrate how Cisco HMF makes security management easy, and how automation can further enhance this ease and efficiency. Attendees will gain insights into streamlining cybersecurity processes with Cisco HMF automation.

### Automating Conversations and Customer Experience with AI Agent - DEVNET-1240
Speakers: David Veevers (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243048904001vvqE

You'll also gain knowledge in how to go about setting up and creating your first LLM or Conversational Bots to power any of your digital channels OR your contact center. Transform your customer interactions with cutting-edge AI.

### Automating Micro-Segmentation and Deploying Layer 4-7 Services in VXLAN EVPN Fabrics using Group Policy Option (GPO) and Nexus Dashboard - BRKDCN-2909
Speakers: Alessandro De Prato (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243041247001vS8A
Macro and Micro Segmentation are key design strategies for securing any data center. This session describes the new segmentation capabilities introduced in NXOS VXLAN EVPN fabrics to enhance the security posture inside the perimeter of the data center. You will learn about security groups, security group ACLs, and how they leverage VXLAN Group Policy model to classify endpoints dynamically. Attendees will learn how contracts can be used to allow or deny undesired traffic, and to implement a zero-trust model where endpoints can only communicate with other endpoints as per allowed policies. This session will cover the various use cases and techniques network operators can leverage to divide the data center into distinct security zones and enable workloads to communicate regardless of where these applications reside within the network. This session will also contain live demos to highlight the automation and management functionalities offered by Nexus Dashboard to enable micro-segmentation traffic enforcement in the DC. Prior knowledge of VXLAN EVPN and Nexus Dashboard will be beneficial. Those interested in the underlying architecture of GPO, including an in depth understanding on how the control and data planes operate, should consider attending BRKDCN-2633 before this session.

### Automating Network Compliance: Leveraging Cisco NSO for Compliance Auditing, Reporting, and Remediation - DEVNET-2144
Speakers: Jorge Mira (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243052718001vIoc

Attendees will discover how to leverage Cisco Network Services Orchestrator (NSO) compliance reporting to audit network configuration using compliance templates. The session dives into the evolution of auditing to automating network configuration baselines for standardized network deployments. Attendees will understand how these tools facilitate continuous compliance checks and remediation of non-compliant configurations. By exploring real-world examples to illustrate the implementation of these strategies, attendees can gain valuable insights into increasing security posture and meeting compliance needs for networks.

### Automating Operations with Nexus Dashboard and Kafka: From Real-Time Anomaly Streaming to Network Compliance - DEVNET-2603
Speakers: Amit Kumar (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243007890001v36H
Modern networks continuously generate anomalies and insights, but turning those alerts into immediate, consistent actions is what drives operational excellence. In this session, attendees will learn how to integrate Cisco Nexus Dashboard with a Kafka broker to stream anomaly data in real time and automate downstream actions using Python scripts. The live demo will show how anomalies detected by Nexus Dashboard, can be streamed in real time to Webex chat spaces, used to create tickets in an ITSM platform, and even push configuration corrections back to the network when non-compliant changes are detected. This practical example of event-driven automation demonstrates how telemetry, APIs, and integrations can create a self-compliant network that minimizes manual effort and audit risk. Attendees will leave with reusable ideas and sample workflows to help them build similar automated pipelines in their own environments.

### Automating Your AI Journey - CENDEV-1997
Speakers: Shannon McFarland (Cisco), Sujith Joseph (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242944561001v4rM

It's an infrastructure and operations challenge that demands a shift to an "AI-first ops" mindset. In this panel, leaders from real-world operations teams share how they use automation frameworks to deploy the full AI stack: from cloud native AI Agents, MCP servers, RAG pipelines, and related software services to the Cisco infrastructure that supports these services.

### Automation Advocacy: Bridging the Gap from Ops to DevOps - DEVNET-2237
Speakers: Allison Estes-Smargiassi (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243052917001v4z7
You're all in on automation - you've learned every tool you can get your hands on and have started automating some of your own tasks. Now what? Maybe you have big plans - new tools to implement, scripts to write, pipelines to build - but now you need buy-in from the rest of your team. Going from small one-off automation to automations at scale takes lots of time and effort, which can be a tough sell in the fast pace environments that many teams face, where it's easier to keep doing things the way they've always been done. In this session, we'll be sharing the automation journey of DevNet Sandbox over the past three years with a specific focus on how we got the ball rolling. We'll talk about the paradigm shift from Ops to DevOps, as well as some tips and tricks we've found to ease into that transition.

### Automation After the Honeymoon Phase - BRKOPS-2849
Speakers: Joerg Schultz (Cisco), Julia Clarke (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243068782001v2MH
Automation is essential for scaling network operations, but what happens when your tools reach Day 2 maturity? Beyond the initial benefits, maintaining and evolving automation to meet changing business needs presents unique challenges - challenges often overlooked in the rush to implement. This talk dives into the realities of Day 2 automation. We'll explore real-world examples of issues like skills gaps, technical debt, and tightly coupled scripts that can hinder progress. Learn how to adapt and future-proof your automation efforts by fostering collaboration between engineers and business stakeholders. You'll gain insights into making your tools more durable, flexible, and aligned with evolving business priorities. Discover why the key to long-term automation success lies less in the tools - like Python, Ansible, and Terraform - and more in the people driving their use. If you're a network engineer with experience in automation and an operations focus, this session will give you actionable strategies to thrive in a Day 2 world.

### Automation and In-Depth Troubleshooting of Cisco Catalyst 8000, ASR 1000, ISR, and SD-WAN Edge - BRKTRS-3475

We will demonstrate problem isolation and root cause analysis on a live, feature-rich network such as routing, QoS, IPsec (FlexVPN and SD-WAN), and NAT.

### Automation in Action: Lessons Learned from Dozens of Customer Deployments - DEVNET-1425
Speakers: Ed Moore (Cisco), Chris Adams (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242944969001vDks
As we advance on our automation journeys, building support for new ways of working can be challenging. Drawing from real experiences with dozens of customers, this session shares field-tested patterns and strategies that drive successful automation adoption. We'll dive into three key deployment patterns: Expanding As-Code Deployments Across Multiple Domains: Learn how organizations are extending Infrastructure-as-Code principles beyond single environments to create unified, scalable automation frameworks Building confidence with Automated Testing and Compliance: Discover best practices for embedding automated testing and compliance into your automation workflows, improving confidence, and catching issues before they impact production Self-Service Operations: See how self-service models empower teams, accelerate delivery, and reduce operational friction-turning automation into a business enabler rather than a technical hurdle. Through real-life customer stories and practical examples, you'll gain actionable insights into what works, what doesn't, and the key factors that drive automation success. Whether you're just getting started or looking to scale, you'll leave with actionable ideas and a clearer path to successful automation in your own organisation.

### Automation, AI and Autonomy: Navigating the Future of Networks - PARAI-1426
Speakers: Morgan Spriggs (Computacenter)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242976811001vfqf
'Future networks will be fully autonomous -- configuring, managing, monitoring, and optimising without human intervention. Sounds great. But what is the reality behind this statement and how do organisations navigate the journey from manual to automated to fully autonomous networking? In this session, we cut through the hype to show a clear path towards more autonomous, self-improving networks including: • The emerging balance between automation and accountability and how human expertise and AI intelligence are coming together to define the next generation of network operations • How AI is already delivering measurable value in real customer environments enhancing operations, strengthening security, and elevating network performance • The evolving and elevated role of the network engineer Join us to discover what an AI-enabled network truly looks like and why the future of networking isn't about choosing between humans or machines, but combining both to build the most reliable, predictive networks ever.

### Backend to Frontline: Enhancing Customer Experience with Webex Connect Integrations - BRKCCT-2397

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242971139001vW7n
As customer expectations for self-service and instant support continue to rise, organisations need to bridge the gap between robust backend systems and seamless customer-facing experiences. ### Beginner's Guide to ThousandEyes - BRKENT-1656
Speakers: Primoz Kolman (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1771608132797001rGrI
Your application serves customers worldwide . Your organization relies on diverse connectivity options to unify your network across all locations. Your IT and networking teams are implementing SD-WAN and SASE but face challenges with incident management , spending excessive unproductive time in war rooms , avoiding accountability , and struggling with inefficient tools . This session highlights how Cisco ThousandEyes plays a central role in quickly identifying the source of critical performance degradation incidents . What you will learn: How ThousandEyes Assurance enhances your Digital Resiliency by gathering and visualizing data across application and network layers How to leverage ThousandEyes to rapidly pinpoint the root cause of critical incidents affecting your applications and networks How ThousandEyes helps reduce incident mean-time-to-resolution ( MTTR ) or mean-time-to-innocence ( MTTI ) and improves operational efficiency for IT and networking teams Audience Level: Beginners. ### Benchmarking AIML Infrastructure at Scale: AIML Architectures, AI Workloads and Reproducibility for High Performance Data Centers - BRKDCN-2651
Speakers: Rakesh Kumar (Cisco), Ram Gandikota (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242961375001v37e
As AIML infrastructure scales rapidly, the need for repeatable, transparent, and architecture-aware benchmarking has become critical. In this session, we dive deep into current AIML benchmarking methodologies we used - highlighting the challenges for benchmarking to align and keep up with fast-evolving AI training and inferencing needs, and showcasing strategies to bring reproducibility closer to reality to form standards. This session presents a comprehensive roadmap for benchmarking AIML infrastructure at scale, focusing on alignment with open standards and real-world reproducibility. We will examine lessons learned from deploying modern benchmarking methodologies, including insights from large-scale cluster engagements and case studies that highlight critical factors such as network behavior, application-level KPIs (like NCCL collectives), and the challenges of network tuning. Attendees will discover how recent innovations - including packet trimming and UEC-based feature alignment - are transforming benchmarking frameworks and shaping the future of Ethernet standards for AIML clusters. Attendees will leave with practical strategies for harmonizing benchmarking processes with production deployments, reducing test drift, and driving reliable performance validation for next-generation AIML platforms.

### Best Practices and Recommendations for Migrating Cisco Catalyst Center from 2.3.7 to 3.1 - BRKOPS-2438
Speakers: Faisal Mehmood (Cisco), Sean Sivak (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242974009001v5l5
Upgrading from Cisco Catalyst Center 2.3.7 to version 3.x requires a new approach to planning and migration compared to previous releases. With Catalyst Center 3.1, Cisco introduces significant enhancements in performance and security, as well as highly anticipated features like Site-Based Role-Based Access Control. These advancements offer robust capabilities for managing and monitoring even the most demanding network environments. However, the enhancements in 3.1 - especially those related to the underlying operating system - mean that a direct upgrade from 2.3.7 to 3.1 is not supported. To address this, Cisco has developed the Platform Refresh application, which streamlines the migration process by verifying prerequisites, converting existing 2.3.7 backups, installing Catalyst Center 3.1, and restoring data from the converted backup.

Our team of engineers utilized Nexus 9000 Series switches provisioned and managed by Nexus Dashboard to train and fine-tune frontier large language models and run inference workloads across distributed clusters of GPUs. Automating routine network and security tasks with AI-driven platforms. Building new service offerings around AI-powered observability and AIOps. Showcasing a live demo of a simulated network issue being solved autonomously by an AI-powered managed service.

### Building AI-Ready APIs for Your Network: MCP and A2A Patterns - BRKAI-2843

Attendees will learn design patterns for building network APIs that AI agents can consume naturally and reliably, from defining schemas and capabilities to ensuring secure, deterministic responses. Practical examples will show how MCP and A2A enable multi-domain agent coordination without brittle, one-off integrations.
