### Building Europe's Secure Digital Future at the Speed of Innovation - CENIF-1004
Speakers: Agostino Santoni (Cisco), Guy Diedrich (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242982727001v0br
Europe stands at a defining moment-positioned to lead the next era of digital transformation, but only if we move with both velocity and security. In this session, Guy Diedrich, Cisco's Global Innovation Officer, and Agostino Santoni, SVP of Cisco South Europe, will share how Cisco is partnering with European nations to architect a digital future that doesn't force a choice between speed and safety. The reality is stark: AI is compressing innovation cycles from decades to months, fundamentally rewriting how economies compete. Yet this acceleration creates profound global vulnerabilities-from cybersecurity threats that reached -8.7 trillion in 2024, to a skills crisis where 90% of ICT roles are experiencing moderate to high AI-driven transformation right now. Europe cannot afford to digitize without securing, or to innovate without the human capacity to lead that innovation. The stakes are existential: nations that can't upskill their people at the speed AI demands risk outsourcing not just jobs, but their capacity to shape their own digital future. Join Guy and Agostino for a conversation about how Europe can and must move at the speed of innovation without compromising the trust and security that define European values. Because in the AI era, trust isn't just good policy. It's the infrastructure that makes everything else possible.

### Circuit-Style Segment Routing and Service Emulation - BRKMPL-2133
Speakers: Thomas Peiyao Wang (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243064820001v2O1
This session will demonstrate how to enable time-division multiplexing (TDM) and private-line emulation services over bandwidth-guaranteed circuit-style segment routing. Attendees will also see service emulation use cases over the resilient circuit-style segment routing transport. Traditional circuits over SONET/SDH transport are becoming outdated, even at the end of life, and costs are skyrocketing. Segment routing has been well adopted by the market. Circuit-style segment routing provides an alternative solution to offering reliable transport, but much cheaper than SONET/SDH. The centralized SR-PCE helps to compute the network resources and ensure the bandwidth-guaranteed CS-SR data path with protection.

### Cisco CX: Unlocking Network Insights with Multimodal AI - AI-2596
Speakers: Mahesh Viswanathan (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243029099001vNHk
Cisco Customer Experience (CX) engagements generate a wealth of visually and semantically rich data, including network topology diagrams, configuration files, detailed test plans, performance graphs, log files, TAC case notes, image/audio attachments, and customer environment data. These mixed-mode data sources and data streams carry critical content embedded across text, tables, figures, and visual representations. Generative AI, specifically multimodal AI, can analyze this complex information to unlock deep insights hidden within images, architecture diagrams, logs, and text. Imagine CX engineers being able to instantly find and answer questions such as what is the optimal convergence time for this routing protocol in a replicated customer environment?, which historical defects are most relevant to my planned software upgrade given my specific hardware and configuration?, or how many similar network incidents have been resolved with this specific workaround across our customer base? hidden across diverse data sources like log files, network diagrams, and bug databases. Multimodal AI can process and comprehend both visual text and visual objects, and the intricate interplay between them, providing unprecedented clarity. Join us to understand how CX services will leverage this transformative technology to enhance proactive de-risking, optimize services engagements, and build new value-added services for our customers. We will walk through examples of how CX applies this technology, discuss suitable LLMs, and explore common CX use cases. You will understand how it opens new search and retrieval capabilities for your visually rich topological and operational network data across various industry scenarios. Finally, gain a deep understanding of the challenges for its adoption and how to mitigate them. This session will be an intermediate level and is for an audience that has some experience using generative AI for text-based applications.

### Cisco SD-WAN, Hidden Complexity Revealed: How Cisco TAC Addresses Really Tricky Problems - BRKTRS-3050
Speakers: Denis Kodentsev (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1771608138262001rGXD
The key benefit of Cisco SD-WAN is providing an easy and scalable way to implement and manage extremely complex WAN networks. This comes with a cost: the complexity does not go anywhere and becomes hidden. It's fine for normal network operations, as most of the outages could be easily addressed by leveraging the vManage GUI or by CLI in case of tricky issues. There's also a well-developed set of troubleshooting tools used by Cisco TAC also available for customers. But what if something goes wrong and the issue requires a much deeper understanding of the "moving parts" working in the SD-WAN background? These moving parts are essentially logically separated processes operating on a WAN edge router or SD-WAN controllers continuously interacting with each other. Such interactions could be really intensive while also insightful for understanding how Cisco SD-WAN works. These processes mostly do not reveal themselves as they deliver low-level logic of the Cisco SD-WAN technology and could be considered as "hidden" to the end user. The session will explore key aspects of the hidden complexity of Cisco SD-WAN technology so customers and partners can leverage the knowledge to navigate across very complex issues and situations, while troubleshooting and designing their Cisco SD-WAN networks. We'll start with the troubleshooting tools available today and how Cisco TAC utilizes them. What are a tool's scope, applicability, limitations, and best practices? Next, to translate the tool's output into something meaningful, we'll dig deep into control-plane and data-plane processes running on an SD-WAN edge router and how they interact with each other. What is the expected behavior and what are the major dependencies out there? Finally, we'll check some techniques we're using in TAC to navigate around the most troublesome issues - intermittent issues which are really hard to chase and even harder to isolate.

### Cisco SD-WAN: Better NetOps for Better DevOps - DEVNET-1318
Speakers: Alberto Rodriguez-Natal (Cisco), Vipin Varghese (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243054547001vgc7
Modern applications, typically made of many on-prem, cloud, and SaaS components, are more distributed than ever, and require a solid WAN foundation that provides optimal connectivity services. This challenges the Network Operations teams (NetOps) to provide WAN services to a very agile community of Development Operations teams (DevOps) who use agile application infrastructure (e.g. Cloud, Kubernetes, etc) to dynamically deploy and configure those distributed application components. This session will showcase some Cisco Catalyst SD-WAN capabilities, that enable seamless network alignment with the application infrastructure, to help reduce the friction between NetOps and DevOps teams. Attendees will learn about integrations that enable automated discovery of Kubernetes clusters and Kubernetes applications, Cloud OnRamp features that help with cloud deployments and SaaS traffic optimization, and different APIs that allow to configure all this programmatically and see it in action with a live demo. Some understanding of SD-WAN, Kubernetes, and Cloud is helpful, but not required.

### Cisco Security Cloud Control - Bringing the Cisco Security Portfolio Together - BRKSEC-1656
Speakers: Adam Bragg (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243087525001veei

### Complete Provisioning of Cisco Switches using Cisco Catalyst Center Templates - BRKOPS-2566
Speakers: Jaromir Pilar (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243070802001vMdT
Automation of device configurations and checking configuration compliance, is one of the key tasks which has high priority for many customers. Cisco Catalyst Center offers multiple options to solve this task, like automation, using predefined workflows for certain solutions like SD Access or using APIs for "network-as-a-code" type of provisioning. One of the most common and most flexible options is using CLI templates. In combination with plug-and-play functionality, it allows users to configure devices from unpacking it from the box to full participation in the network with desired configuration, without touching the device console, and without a terminal session to the device. In this session, attendees will learn how to onboard a device into a network via plug-and-play process, providing basic configuration via an onboarding template ,and how to provide full configuration via "workflow like" composite template configuring VLANs, interfaces, 802.1x authentication, Cisco TrustSec and many more functions. Examples of templates in Apache Velocity Language and Jinja will be shown including cycles, conditions, methods to work with data objects, template variables, links to Catalyst Center inventory etc. At the end of the session, a recorded demo of the entire process will be shown.

### Connecting the Unconnected with Cisco Validated Solution - BRKMSI-1000
Speakers: Shahid Ajmeri (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243064418001vUPH
Low Earth Orbit (LEO) satellite-based Non-Terrestrial Networks (NTNs) offer a high-throughput, low-latency, and scalable solution for extending wireless connectivity to remote and underserved regions. As NTNs become a strategic complement to terrestrial infrastructure, they are enabling key use cases such as 5G backhaul using SRv6 and EVPN overlays, SD-WAN for enterprise connectivity, secure communications for defence and national infrastructure, and future-ready support for quantum-resilient transport. Cisco is developing a validated, cloud-native architecture to enable seamless deployment of 5G Xhaul, SD-WAN, and IoT services over integrated terrestrial and non-terrestrial domains. The architecture is designed with built-in resiliency, leveraging segment routing and advanced telemetry to deliver dynamic path optimization, redundancy, and high availability, even in the face of link instability or constrained satellite bandwidth. The session will also showcase Cisco's Provider Connectivity Assurance (PCA) solution, which plays a critical role in delivering end-to-end service assurance. PCA provides real-time visibility, deep analytics, and SLA monitoring across both terrestrial and satellite transport, ensuring consistent performance, rapid fault isolation, and operational simplicity in complex hybrid network environments.

### DHCP Snooping and Option 82: Friends or Foes? - CISCOU-2336
Speakers: Peter Paluch (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242970125001v48v
If you ever deployed DHCP Snooping, you most probably faced issues related to the DHCP Option 82 added by the snooping switches: Client DHCP messages not reaching the DHCP server, or the server not responding to them. The usual quick fix is to disable the Option 82 insertion. However, this practice - though widespread - is wrong. Doing so cripples some of the DHCP Snooping protections, and in SD-Access deployments, it breaks the DHCP service entirely. This session brings the ultimate explanations and answers. We will look at the original purpose of DHCP Option 82, then describe the operation of DHCP Snooping in depth and explain the role of Option 82 in it, and finally discuss potential caveats and the proper ways of resolving them.

### Dashboards on Demand: Practical Network Visibility with RADKit and Grafana - DEVNET-2847
Speakers: Alfonso Sandoval Rosas (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242952335001vedy
As network environments grow in complexity, the need for flexible and scalable data collection and visualization becomes increasingly urgent. RADKit is a comprehensive framework that provides seamless connectivity, management, and automation for network devices. This session demonstrates a practical use case of the RADKit framework and its Python software development kit (SDK) to create a containerized system that collects, transforms, and displays device statistics in Grafana dashboards. In this session, you will learn how to integrate Python middleware, Genie parsers, InfluxDB, and Grafana, supporting both on-demand and time-series data analysis. The solution features interactive picklists and dynamic charts, all within a fully customizable container-based environment that you can easlity adjust to your specific needs. At the end of this session, you will leave with ideas to help you: Harness the RADKit Python SDK and open-source tools for advanced, customizable data collection and visualization across diverse devices Adopt a container-based approach for rapid deployment and easy scalability of monitoring solutions Visualize network trends and identify optimization opportunities through actionable dashboards Understanding of RADKit operation and container technologies is required. Familiarity with Python scripting is recommended. The session includes live demonstrations, and demo code will be publicly available.

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243005481001vDX4

This session demonstrates how a new engagement approach, together with Customer Journey Data Services (CJDS) in Webex Contact Center, consolidates these signals and activates real-time, rules-based triggers across channels. Attendees will learn how to define rule sets on key data points, and apply different capabilities such as artificial intelligence (AI) to initiate proactive outreach, recover abandoned journeys, redirect low-value repeat contacts to automation or self-help, and surface unvoiced dissatisfaction reducing churn and increasing conversions. Designed for contact center professionals and solution architects with an understanding of customer experience operations, the session features live demonstrations and real-world use cases that showcase measurable outcomes from data-driven, proactive engagement.

### Day 0 to Day N with Meraki Dashboard - DEVNET-1220

In Part 2 of the session, we will learn how APIs can be used to confidently and repeatedly deploy changes across multiple sites using different tools of automation such as Cisco Workflows, Python scripts, IaC tools like Ansible or Terraform. ### De-Risk Your Network using CML, Splunk and MCP server - BRKOPS-2585

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242976250001vb0C

### Demystifying AI Infrastructure for a Network Engineer - BRKDCN-2971
Speakers: Paresh Gupta (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242961568001vbki
If you are a network engineer with no prior background in AI Infrastructure, this session is for you. Learn how GPUs communication with each other during distributed AI jobs, their impact on the network, and how this differs from a typical data center. This session covers the fundamentals of the key networking technologies: RDMA over Converged Ethernet (RoCE), Priority-based Flow Control (PFC), and Data Center Quantized Congestion Notification (DC QCN) Inter-GPU collective operations using NVIDIA Collective Communication Library (NCCL), AMD ROCm Communication Collectives Library (RCCL), and Intel Habana Collective Communication Library (HCCL) NVIDIA Spectrum-X problem statement and solution with Cisco Nexus and Hyperfabric for AI, and UCS Ultra Ethernet introduction. No prior knowledge is necessary This session starts from the basics and makes you ready to design and operate networks of an AI Infrastructure.

### Demystifying the Impact of AI on Security: Opportunities and Risks - BRKAI-1941
Speakers: Munib Shah (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1771608125228001roaR
Artificial Intelligence is reshaping how networks are built, secured, and managed. For security and network engineers, AI is not just a new tool it's a fundamental shift in how threats are detected, analyzed, and contained. AI-driven technologies can enrich threat intelligence through deep learning and accelerate the contentment of malicious activity. But the same capabilities that empower defenders also equip adversaries, creating an urgent need for more advanced security controls. This session explores both sides of the equation: how AI can enhance your security posture and how to defend against AI-powered threats. Join us to see how Cisco security solutions - including Secure Firewall, Identity Services Engine (ISE), and our AI-powered security framework, AI Defense - can be leveraged to: Enhance threat detection and response with AI and deep learning Automate security operations and reduce false positives Protect environments where AI is used to perform business-critical tasks Build proactive defenses against AI-enabled attacks Attendees will walk away with a clear understanding of how to integrate AI-driven security capabilities into their infrastructure, and how to safeguard against the next wave of AI-powered threats

### Demystifying the Role of AI in Your Cisco Wireless Deployments - BRKEWN-3007
Speakers: Vishal Desai (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243067402001vcxl
Whether your wireless network runs on simplified IT management through Meraki Cloud or leverages the richness of wireless features with on-premises Cisco Catalyst Center, your wireless network is only as good as your ability to optimize it. In today's world, filled with many AI/ML applications for essential and ubiquitous services like Wi-Fi, it only raises the question of how AI/ML can really help deliver stable and yet best-in-class experiences to meet capacity needs, and maintain SLAs for your 5GHz and 6E wireless clients. But models generated by AI/ML are only as good as your data lake. With the right data and the right approach, it can multiply tenfold the efficiency of your network operations, your management and troubleshooting, but if you don't know how to use it, it is just like a laser saber without a battery. In this session, you will learn about under-the-hood details of powerful Applied AI-based applications available for both cloud-managed and on-premises Cisco wireless platforms. You will also learn how applied AI is enabling closed-loop optimizations that transform your wireless networks, maximizing client experiences, and making network operations more efficient by leveraging AIOps to automatically tune your wireless networks with tailor-made config recommendations based on the unique RF signatures, all of this done without compromising your privacy.

### Deploying Distributed AI/ML Infrastructure with Model Context Protocol (MCP) for Advanced AI Use Cases Across Cisco Platforms - BRKCLD-2944
Speakers: Jeff Teeter (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243092124001vNx2
In today's digital landscape, integrating AI and ML into enterprise operations is essential for staying competitive and driving innovation. This session explores deploying distributed AI/ML infrastructure using the Model Context Protocol (MCP) to enable advanced AI use cases across Cisco platforms. Attendees will discover how Cisco's AI-ready platforms can leverage MCP to unify access to Cisco platforms and 10,000+ functions/tools through intelligent natural language interfaces, demonstrating Cisco's commitment to enterprise AI innovation. This comprehensive session offers a deep dive into the architecture of autonomous AI agents that can query, configure, and orchestrate Cisco platforms including Meraki, Catalyst Center, SD-WAN, Intersight, and more - all through a single, intelligent gateway. The session covers the complete architecture from rapid platform scaffolding (integrating new platforms in seconds) to advanced customization, vector database indexing, and comprehensive observability with built-in telemetry. ### Deploying IPv6 Routing Protocols: Specifics and Considerations - BRKIPV-2418
Speakers: Peter Paluch (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243097475001v0q6
Even though all major IPv6 routing protocols are rooted in their well-known IPv4 counterparts, they all come with their own unique set of idiosyncrasies. And when faced with the task of deploying an IPv6 routing protocol along with its IPv4 variant, there are often multiple ways of doing it - but the best choice is not always obvious. The goal of this session is to discuss the specifics of IPv6 support in OSPFv3, IS-IS, EIGRP, and BGP in IOS XE. For each of these protocols, we will do a quick IPv6-focused refresher, and then focus on the protocol's unique IPv6 traits (and quirks). We will also look at how the protocol supports the co-existence of IPv4 and IPv6, and if there are multiple options to choose from, we will discuss the pros and cons of each of them. To benefit the most from this session, it is highly recommended to have a working knowledge of routing protocols in general.

### Deploying Nexus Dashboard in Your Organization - BRKDCN-2914
Speakers: Matthias Wessendorf (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243039442001vu9O
This technical breakout will provide an end-to-end view of deploying and operating Cisco Nexus Dashboard, which provides unprecedented simplicity by integrating operational services to manage customers on-premises and in cloud and hybrid fabrics through a single dashboard. You'll learn that by using the Cisco Nexus Dashboard, IT operations teams can seamlessly navigate all aspects of infrastructure lifecycle tasks from initial configuration and capacity planning, to running and troubleshooting the entire IT infrastructure. Understanding how an infrastructure is deployed for usage of new tools and methodologies in your organizations is one side, but effectively operating the infrastructure is the other side. This session will review some common tasks and activities for deploying and operating Cisco Nexus Dashboard for day-to-day operations.

### Deploying VPNs over Segment Routed Networks Made Easy using Crosswork - BRKMPL-2131
Speakers: Krishnan Thirukonda (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243062410001vf0Q
With segment routing, a software-defined network (SDN) approach to program paths for transport services such as layer 3 and layer 2 VPN becomes possible, enabling new approaches for managing transport networks based on segment routing. In this session, a review of the requirements and capabilities for an SDN approach for transport networks is discussed. This is followed by a description of the Cisco solution for multivendor SDN controller for transport networks to deploy and manage VPN services over SR-TE using MPLS or SRv6, using the Cisco Crosswork Network Controller (CNC). The main use cases covered by CNC will be described and a walkthrough of UI- and API-based workflows to create VPNs, SR-TE/SRv6 policies, and bind layer 2/layer 3 VPNs to these policies will be shown. Additionally, advance capabilities to do tactical bandwidth management and monitor service health will be discussed. The goal of this session is to understand how to bring an SDN approach to managing transport networks and understanding the Cisco solution for it.

### Deploying the Webex App to your Organization - BRKCOL-2198

We will focus on enabling calling, messaging and meetings workloads within the Webex App for your users. ### Deployment of Micro-Segmentation in Cisco NX-OS VXLAN EVPN Fabrics with VXLAN Group Policy Option (GPO) - BRKDCN-2633
Speakers: Alessandro De Prato (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243043245001vwFy
In traditional data center environments, security enforcement for protecting applications/workloads is typically implemented using perimeter firewalls and other security inspection devices. However, this approach is not effective against the advanced nature of modern attacks, which have an attack surface that spans the entire data center, affecting both east-west and north-south traffic flows. This session introduces the use of micro-segmentation with security groups and security group ACLs in Cisco NX-OS VXLAN EVPN-based data center fabrics. This feature, also known as VXLAN Group Policy Option (GPO) (IETF draft: draft-smith-vxlan-group-policy-05), can provide application-specific policies to tightly control the communication of application workloads regardless of where they reside within the network. The first part of the session covers the functional component of the Cisco GPO implementation, both for a control and data plane perspective. The second part discusses the most common use cases for the deployment of this micro-segmentation option, including service chaining improvement and simplification.

### Deployment of VXLAN EVPN Gateways with Cisco ACI for the Interconnection of Heterogeneous Data Center Fabrics - BRKDCN-2634
Speakers: Takuya Kishida (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243043493001vpDM
In recent years, the use of the VXLAN EVPN technology has become the de-facto standard for the deployment of data center fabrics. Cisco leverages this technology for its two flavors of fabric (and multi-fabric) architectures: Cisco ACI and Cisco NX-OS VXLAN EVPN. As a consequence, customers are very often challenged with the requirement of interconnecting those heterogeneous fabrics, both for co-existence or migration purposes. This session introduces the use of a VXLAN EVPN gateway functionality in a Cisco ACI fabric to establish native Layer 2 and Layer 3 connectivity with standard-based VXLAN BGP EVPN fabrics. The session covers the functional components and the control and data plane mechanisms, required to implement the VXLAN EVPN gateway functionality in an ACI fabric. It will also discuss how to interconnect pre-existing (brownfield) heterogeneous fabrics, a functionality usually referred to as "namespace normalization" and how to provide end-to-end micro-segmentation support between ACI and VXLAN EVPN fabrics through the integration of VXLAN EVPN gateways with the VXLAN Group Policy Option (GPO) technology. This session is a companion of breakout BRKDCN-2933, and the expectation is that attendees already have at least a basic understanding of Cisco NX-OS VXLAN EVPN technology in order to get the best out of this session. Also, it is highly recommended to attend BRKDCN-2602 before coming to this session.

### Design, Automate, and Simplifying VXLAN EVPN Multi-Site Architectures with Nexus Dashboard Federation - BRKDCN-2988
Speakers: Parth Patel (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243047047001v9JJ
With the exponential growth of Applications, Enterprises and Service Providers must create Scalable Data Centers. This has led to the deployment of Data Centers, large and small, spread across geographies, with IT requirements to interconnect them for reasons such as High availability, Disaster recovery, and Scale. VXLAN BGP EVPN Multi-Site allows inter-connecting loosely coupled VXLAN sites to build hierarchal overlays to provide secure Multi-Tenancy and Mobility at Scale. In this session, attendees will first learn different design options and automation workflows of VXLAN EVPN Single-Site and Multi-Site using Nexus Dashboard. Next, we will introduce Nexus Dashboard Federation, powered by One-Manage, which provides a unified platform to join, automate, and manage multiple Nexus Dashboard clusters. This centralized control enables seamless VXLAN EVPN Multi-Site deployments across Multi-Cluster fabrics, offering a scalable and automated solution to extend Layer-2 and Layer-3 services.

### Designing Access and Core Switching Architectures with Meraki - BRKENS-1402

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1771608127455001rM1A

### Designing Highly Available Networks using Catalyst 9000 Series Switches - BRKENS-2095
Speakers: Minhaj Uddin (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1771608128951001ropk
This session will cover new and existing high availability features introduced in IOS-XE on Catalyst 9000 Series switching platforms. We will discuss how you can achieve high availability in your network through various stages in the design lifecycle. During the initial design, we will discuss the benefits of features like StackPower, power resiliency modes, StackWise-480, and StackWise Virtual. After the design phase, we will discuss features like GIR, extended fast software upgrade (xFSU), patchability, and ISSU that will help maintain resiliency during the operational phase. We will then conclude by covering how you can efficiently leverage all the high-availability features in different network designs and achieve maximum uptime and resiliency.

Speakers: Krithika Moorthy (Cisco), Jiri Chaloupka (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243009711001vm1L
Modern data-center (DC) and WAN networks must handle unprecedented scale, complexity, and performance demands, particularly in hybrid and multi-cloud environments. This session explores the Neo-Cloud reference architecture - a proven design blueprint that uses IOS XR for backbone roles and SONiC for data center functions, delivering unified operations across both DC and WAN domains. You'll leave this session with insight into: Proven architectural patterns for scalable and resilient Neo-Cloud DC and WAN deployments Understand strategies to monitor, manage, and optimize distributed networks effectively Practical examples showing improved agility, efficiency, and adaptability in Neo-cloud networks

### Designing Regular and Irregular Meeting Rooms - BRKCOL-2176
Speakers: Bobby McGonigle (Cisco), Svein Terje Steffensen (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243034443001vJMq
Ever tried to set up a room with out of the ordinary requirements? This session will help you create the best experiences, both for regular and irregular meeting rooms, either in Microsoft Teams Room or RoomOS mode. Cisco is often approached with questions on how to design and set up specific types of rooms, be it a board room, a campfire layout, a divisible room, a training room or a court room. The common theme is they all have needs beyond a regular meeting room. This session will demystify Cinematic meetings and describe how to design to solve for various needs, both using the capabilities within the Cisco Room Devices, and when integrating with external equipment. ### Designing Routed Optical Networks: IP/MPLS Considerations - BRKSPG-2029
Speakers: Emerson Moura (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243065840001vfWL
Routed optical networking (RON) is a new IP/DWDM network architecture vision that greatly improves the economics and efficiency of networking as well as enables a new application paradigm. This session will specifically focus on the IP routing layer of RON and how this simpler, multi-service IP-centric architecture reduces operator costs, while at the same time improves network asset utilization and resiliency, as well as enables optimization of the network for distributed applications, content, and internet peering. A comparison of a traditional IP/DWDM architecture vs. RON architecture will also be presented.

### Designing Zero Trust Data Center Segmentation Architecture - BRKDCN-2930
Speakers: Cesar Obediente (Cisco), Brenden Buresh (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243047474001vNXj
Data center security has historically been dominated by leveraging firewall appliances to provide L4-L7 policy based stateful traffic controls. Introducing data center segmentation to sub-divide the network into smaller, more controlled domains offers additional protections by reducing attack surface diameter to defend against vulnerabilities. This session will investigate how data center segmentation can be systematically deployed in a layered fashion to provide the best aggregate security defense posture and create zero trust architecture. Utilizing a Nexus 9K VXLAN EVPN fabric baseline, first we'll explore macro-segmentation with VRFs and micro-segmentation with VXLAN GPO leveraging the network to provide L4 stateless policy controls. Next, we'll examine adding the DPU SmartSwitch platform and Isovalent host-based agents to strengthen security with L4 stateful controls. Finally, we'll expand to the hybrid mesh firewall concept combining network, host, and appliance enforcement points with a single central security controller to author and distribute policy.

### Detect, Investigate, and Respond Faster, Hands-On SOC Automation with Splunk Enterprise Security - CISCOU-2229

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242959226001vc0E

Attendees will explore real-world cyber incidents, build dynamic dashboards, and orchestrate automated workflows to strengthen SOC efficiency. Through guided exercises, attendees will learn how to transform data into actionable intelligence, enabling faster detection, deeper insights, and proactive defense against evolving threats.

### Deutsche Bank's Journey to a Modern Private Cloud Powered by Cisco Automation and Future Ready for AI - CSSDCN-2160
Speakers: Ahmet Guel (Cisco), Umar Ahmed (Deutsche Bank)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242951934001vnEI
Join us for an inspiring session showcasing Deutsche Bank's visionary initiative to transform their global data center landscape. Deutsche Bank is making a significant investment in Next Generation Data Center technology to build a modern, agile private cloud fabric architecture based on EVPN/ VxLAN. This strategic commitment highlights the bank's strong partnership with Cisco for its data center automation strategy. Rather than a traditional hardware lifecycle refresh, Deutsche Bank is adopting a service-oriented model, offering VM-as-a-Service, Container-as-a-Service, and Bare Metal-as-a-Service to its business units. Cisco CX LCS is actively supporting this migration to EVPN , leveraging the Nexus 9300 Series with 400G, Nexus Dashboard, and Catalyst 9500 Series with 400G to facilitate this transformation. Automation with Service-as-Code for VXLAN is accelerating deployment, enhancing DevOps time to market, and improving NetOps operational efficiency. This modernization drives critical business outcomes, agility, and cost savings, while aligning with the bank's cloud strategy by balancing workloads between hyperscalers and their private cloud. The architecture is AI-ready, built upon the Cisco AI Ready Data Center foundation and advanced automation capabilities. Furthermore, an Enterprise Agreement covering Data Center Switching and Campus Automation solidifies the long-term Cisco partnership and ensures predictable pricing. This session will share invaluable insights into the technical transformation, the substantial business value achieved, and key lessons learned from this powerful collaboration.

### Developing and Integrating with Cisco XDR - DEVNET-2236
Speakers: Matt Vander Horst (Cisco), Christopher Van Der Made (Cisco)

### Digital Sovereignty: Finding the Balance Between Flexibility and Control - IF-1147
Speakers: Nathan Jokel (Cisco), Chris Gow (Cisco), Lorena Marciano (Cisco Systems, Inc.)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242983761001vJqC
In an era of geopolitical competition, the calls for digital sovereignty grow louder particularly in Europe. But what is the right path? How can governments and customers balance greater control over data and infrastructure with the innovation of global cloud and AI architectures? Learn from executives and experts as they go beyond the headlines to tackle this critical question. They will discuss trends and developments in government policy on digital sovereignty, evolving customer expectations, and how to navigate the fine line between sovereign control and global architectures. Discover how Cisco's Sovereign Critical Infrastructure Portfolio and our experience as a trusted provider can seize business opportunities in cloud and AI while providing customers flexible choices that reflect their digital priorities.

### Digital Twin: A Solid Foundation for AIOps and Network Autonomy - AI-1109
Speakers: Julia Palomino (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242977206001v2Uy
In the world of AI/ML, having a solid network model is super important for AIOps. In this session, we're going to dive into the best practices for crafting an accurate and faithful model representation of the real network, which we call the Digital Twin of the Network. We'll check out how the Network Digital Twin can be used to model big, complex, and diverse multi-vendor and multi-domain networks. This way, network operators can confidently explore different "what-if" scenarios to design, plan, upgrade, and optimize the network - without risking any damage to the physical network. Network Digital Twins are crucial for helping network operators boost their network autonomy, where precise and reliable decision-making is key. We'll show you how it works with a couple of case studies featuring Cisco Crosswork Automation applications that leverage Network Digital Twin and AI-powered tools: Full-mesh RSVP-TE to Segment Routing migration Self-healing of local congested interfaces Can't wait to see you there.

### Driving the Future: SRv6 as the Backbone of Mission-Critical Utility and Transportation Networks - BRKMSI-2386
Speakers: Zubair Khan (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242964559001vre5
Utilities, transportation, and industrial IoT networks have long relied on siloed transport architectures - using legacy OTN and MPLS-TP technologies for mission-critical operational applications such as tele protection, GOOSE messaging, and WAMS in utility networks, or SCADA, level crossing and signaling in transportation. While these technologies have ensured deterministic performance, they are aging and increasingly complex to operate. Segment Routing is emerging as a transformative transport technology that modernizes networks while maintaining reliability, symmetry, and deterministic behavior. In this session, we'll explore how Cisco helps customers build next-generation WAN transport solutions supporting both Layer 2 and Layer 3 VPN services for their most critical applications. We will showcase how SR/EVPN simplifies operations and enhances network agility. Attendees will see circuit-style Segment Routing paths in action, providing deterministic, bidirectional, and latency-optimized connectivity for time-sensitive services. The session will also highlight how these paths enable private line emulation, supporting seamless migration of legacy services.

### Dynamic Network Defence: Automating Traffic Engineering based on ThousandEyes Insights - BRKOPS-1009
Speakers: Kemal Sanjta (Cisco), Mike Hicks (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243094007001vhU8
DDoS attacks and BGP hijacks have long plagued our networks, causing operational chaos, monetary losses, and reputational damage for affected organisations. Despite their prevalence, mitigating these threats often involves reactive, time-intensive processes that rely on manual, usually delayed, execution of relevant methods of procedure (MOPs). Even routine network performance issues, like packet loss or latency, frequently take hours to troubleshoot and resolve, leaving users and stakeholders frustrated. In this talk, we will demonstrate how to leverage the power of ThousandEyes insights to automate the resolution of common operational issues. This includes switching to alternative providers upon detecting detrimental network conditions like packet loss and latency spikes, handling the unpredictable nature of DDoS attacks, and automating responses to BGP hijacks. All of that within the minutes, rather than hours as it was traditionally the case. Our demo-heavy talk will show how to reduce Mean Time to Resolution (MTTR) from hours to minutes, empowering your team to maintain service continuity and to deliver operational excellence.

### EVPN Campus Fabric: Overlay Network Architecture Design Profiles - BRKENS-2838
Speakers: Dmytro Vishchuk (Cisco), Ramchander Nadipally (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242965737001vmj7
To achieve digital transformation and satisfy requirements for agile, scalable, and securely segmented networking, enterprise customers are quickly embracing fabric technologies within their campus networks. A standards-based BGP EVPN fabric delivers crucial agility, scalability, and secure segmentation. However, campus networks introduce unique design complexities, such as non-standard underlay topologies, wireless integration needs, varied switching platforms, and specific traffic steering demands (e.g., for firewalls, guest portals, etc.), which are not typically found in traditional Data Center (DC) CLOS architectures. This session will tackle these issues by presenting four distinct Campus EVPN fabric design profiles, derived from common customer use cases. Each profile will include its design prerequisites, a thorough explanation, and relevant configuration snippets.

### EVPN Campus: Design and Implementation - BRKENS-2041
Speakers: Sergey Nasonov (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242945958001vJfD
Ethernet VPN (EVPN) has become the de facto standard in the Service Provider and Data Center domains and has recently begun making inroads into campus networks, replacing MPLS VPN and traditional networking approaches. This session provides an in-depth exploration of the architecture and design choices involved in deploying an EVPN fabric in campus environments, along with step-by-step implementation guidance. The session focuses on delivering a fully functional EVPN campus configuration in IOS-XE, incorporating lessons learned from real-world EVPN deployments. The session will cover: • BGP EVPN protocol operation • Campus design choices • Implementation guidance using IOS-XE CLI • DHCP, Group-Policy, ESI Multihoming and Multicast operation in the EVPN overlays A baseline knowledge of networking (at the CCNP level) is expected from the audience.

### EVPN Troubleshooting and Debugging - BRKSPG-2473
Speakers: Davide Pacifico (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243066036001vcNM
EVPN is a technology initially designed for Ethernet multipoint services to provide advanced multi-homing capabilities. By using BGP for distributing MAC address reachability information over the MPLS network, EVPN brought the same operational and scale characteristics of IP-based VPNs to layer 2 VPNs. Today, beyond DCI and E-LAN applications, the EVPN solution family provides a common foundation for all Ethernet service types, including E-LINE and E-TREE, as well as data center routing and bridging scenarios. EVPN also provides options to combine layer 2 and layer 3 services into the same instance. This session is a continuation of the EVPN troubleshooting and debugging series presented in earlier Cisco Live conferences. This time, we will look into specific EVPN use cases related to EVPN convergence like Mobility, Fast-Reroute, IRB Gateway and more.

### Efficacy 101: How to Detect, Protect, and Remediate against Email-based Threats - BRKSEC-2245
Speakers: Sergio Pinto (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243089257001vDpy
Attackers are amongst the most sneaky and creative people out there. They spend their entire days figuring out the best way to trick and lure end-users. Fortunately, so do we. Cisco Secure Email Solution uses many technologies, solutions, and techniques to understand attacks, making sure we only allow legitimate emails through and stopping anything suspicious or potentially malicious. Being able to detect BEC types of attacks, phishing, scamming, ransomware, and other attacks, require different techniques to detect, prevent, protect, and remediate against. Email Security is a cornerstone of any Enterprise Security strategy, being a leading vector of attack for an overwhelming 90%+ of all security exploits. In this session, you'll understand the latest techniques we use to detect potentially malicious content, namely how we detect, protect, and remediate against malicious URLs, malicious files, user and brand impersonation, phishing, and many other types of attacks.

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243009314001vZmN
This session will delve into the diverse operational experiences offered by Cisco Nexus Dashboard, Cisco's unified data center networking operations platform. It will showcase the platform's comprehensive automation and programmability capabilities, including its robust REST API, deep integration with Infrastructure as Code (IaC) tools like Terraform and Ansible, and the strategic power of Inventory-Driven Automation. Furthermore, we will learn how Nexus Dashboard enables seamless integration with enterprise IT Service Management (ITSM) platforms for automated service catalog delivery, and facilitates intelligent, real-time responses to critical network events through event-driven automation. This exploration will demonstrate how Nexus Dashboard empowers an organization's teams to streamline operations, enhance agility, and build a truly automated data center network.

### Engineering the Agentic Future: Cisco's Journey to Next-Gen AI Systems - DEVNET-2322
Speakers: Hasith Kalpage (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243032924001v835
Autonomous agents and AI assistants are redefining the landscape of product development. Join us as we share Cisco's ongoing transformation toward an agentic future, highlighting real-world engineering challenges, practical solutions, and open-source innovations. In this session,you'll get an inside look at our Agentic Platform Engineering initiatives-including the community-driven CAIPE.io project and an overview of the architecture and core components of AGNTCY.org. We'll also spotlight emerging open-source efforts like MCP and A2A, discussing how these frameworks interoperate and accelerate multi-agent development. Beyond technical architectures, we'll explore how observability and governance enable safe, scalable deployment of agentic systems in production environments. Whether you're just starting with multi-agent design or already building intelligent systems, you'll leave with actionable insights to begin or advance your own journey into the future of AI-powered product engineering.

### Engineering the Future: Reshape Your Workforce with AI - IF-1247
Speakers: Scott Herpolsheimer (Cisco), Gianpaolo Barozzi (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1771608144974001r026
Today, AI isn’t just reshaping technology — it’s reshaping our workforce. To compete, companies need to rethink how work is designed, how roles are defined, and how people can deliver greater value. In this session, learn how Cisco is using AI to augment, evolve, and create jobs for the technical workforce. We'll peel back the layers on our unique methodology, “Working with AI," detailing how we systematically identified, assessed, and planned for high-value AI use cases. Discover how our data model serves as the technical backbone, mapping AI's impact on specific technical roles and activities, quantifying productivity gains, and ensuring feasibility for practical implementation. You’ll leave ready to use AI to empower your teams to work smarter, adapt faster, and deliver better results.

### Enhancing Cloud-Native Security: Isovalent's CNI Innovations in Kubernetes - BRKSEC-2891
Speakers: Dirk Stoeckmann (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243083712001vYft
In this presentation, we introduce Isovalent's products Cilium, Tetragon, and the underlying eBPF technology. We explain how these tools address the network and security challenges of modern cloud-native applications. Cilium revolutionizes networking in Kubernetes clusters, while Tetragon provides deep insights into the security and behavior of applications. Both are based on eBPF technology, which allows dynamic code insertion into the Linux kernel without the need for recompilation. We will also provide an outlook on how Cisco Hypershield will utilize these products as part of a decentralized security architecture in the future.

### Enhancing Network Automation with Event-Driven Architecture - DEVNET-1640
Speakers: Kareem Iskander (Cisco), Akhila Pamukuntla (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243056796001vA9O
In today's fast-paced network environments, it's crucial to respond quickly and automatically to keep things running smoothly and securely. In this session, we will explore how to implement an event-driven architecture that seamlessly integrates NetBox for network management and documentation, Splunk for real-time data analysis and alerting, and Cisco Cloud Networking for robust network functionality. You will discover how to harness the power of these tools to create an agile network management system where events in NetBox trigger automated actions and alerts in Splunk, boosting overall network efficiency and reliability. We will: Explore the key motivations behind adopting event-driven architectures for network automation Delve into the inner workings of NetBox Events Demonstrate the practical application of Splunk, highlighting Cisco Meraki Dashboard and NetBox Events in action

### Enhancing Splunk with OT context using Cisco Cyber Vision - BRKIOT-2846
Speakers: Kartik Kamra (Cisco), Andrew McPhee (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243019061001vAHV
An operational technology (OT) security strategy is not complete without coverage across both IT and OT networks. There does not necessarily have to be a convergence of these worlds, but context sharing and a unified view across the entire network is critical for protecting operations. This session will highlight the powerful combination of Cisco's Cyber Vision and Splunk working better together to create a defensible architecture and mature cybersecurity program. Cisco Cyber Vision, with a brand new alerts framework, provides comprehensive visibility, monitoring, threat detection, and vulnerability management in industrial environments. This data is seamlessly sent to Splunk for further analysis. Splunk's ability to ingest data from any system empowers organizations to achieve comprehensive security across both IT and OT environments. This capability also empowers organizations to correlate and integrate diverse data sources, enhancing the resilience of industrial environments with complete visibility.

### Enhancing Trust and Security in Service Provider Networks - BRKSPG-2868
Speakers: Rakesh Kandula (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243064018001vJb7
In today's rapidly evolving threat landscape, networks face increasingly sophisticated attacks, making it essential to establish trust from the ground up and maintain it throughout a device's lifecycle. This session explores the foundational trust mechanisms underpinning Cisco routers and outlines security best practices designed to defend against advanced threats. Considering recent high-profile incidents such as Salt Typhoon, operational security hardening has become more critical than ever. While the session primarily focuses on features available in IOS-XR routers, these principles are broadly applicable across Cisco's product portfolio. Additionally, the session will address emerging risks to network security posed by quantum computing, as well as the post-quantum cryptography (PQC) transition options available today.

### Enhancing Wildlife and Environmental Conservation with IoT and Connectivity Solutions - CSSIOT-2033
Speakers: Sophie Maxwell (Connected Conservation)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242952733001vm8B
The Connected Conservation Foundation (CCF) is transforming wildlife and environmental protection through IoT and digital infrastructure, supporting 46 protected areas across 13 countries. This session will showcase how wide-area connectivity, sensors, and high-resolution satellite imagery are forming a scalable technology blueprint for conservation. By creating early warning systems, wildlife tracking and ecosystem monitoring, CCF equips conservation teams with a real-time, 360-degree view of vast and remote landscapes. Cisco technologies have been central to this impact - helping protect 40+ threatened species from poaching, conflict and climate-driven pressures while 'strengthening local community resilience. Notable successes include supporting Kenya's rhino population recovery, where secure parks now safely host 67 reintroduced rhinos, some tagged with LoRa trackers, and maintaining a 700+ days poaching-free record at Sabi Sand Nature Reserve. CCF and Cisco are also investing in people - launching a free global Protected Area Technician Training Program with Cisco Networking Academy and partners, while connecting education campuses in South Africa and Uganda. These initiatives build local skills, provide reliable digital infrastructure and create new opportunities for communities living alongside protected areas.

### Enterprise Architecture: Insights from Principal Architects Across Verticals - CISCOU-2334
Speakers: Dave Lucas (Cisco), Zig Zsiga (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242984165001vKMG

This session covers common themes in embedding AI into operations, lessons learned deploying AI infrastructure in data centers, scalable architectures, AI-ready networking, and Services as Code. The product-agnostic discussion focuses on enterprise architecture best practices, addressing challenges in integrating AI workloads while ensuring security, observability, and agility. Gain insights to design AI-ready environments that drive innovation and business outcomes across industries.

### Enterprise-Ready Agentic Workflows: Hardening MCP with Policy-as-Code - DEVNET-2685
Speakers: Lori Jakab (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242992480001vsg9
Agentic workflows are moving beyond proof-of-concept stages, with Model Context Protocol (MCP) servers increasingly available for Cisco infrastructure and network automation platforms. However, the frantic pace of innovation in the AI space has once again left security considerations as an afterthought: MCP specifications lack enterprise security frameworks, hindering adoption in production environments. This session shows how to harden today's wide-open MCP implementations by adding Policy-as-Code controls. You'll discover why current MCP authentication mechanisms fall short of enterprise requirements and explore implementing unified security policies spanning both MCP-based agentic workflows and existing API-based automation. Beyond traditional API payloads, Open Policy Agent (OPA) and Rego are applied to JSON-RPC messages between MCP clients and servers, creating consistent access controls across your entire automation ecosystem - ensuring policy consistency from legacy scripts to cutting-edge AI agents. You'll leave this session with ideas to help you implement unified Policy-as-Code frameworks spanning agentic and traditional automation, design consistent access controls using OPA and Rego, and build scalable policy architectures that future-proof your Cisco automation investments. Basic understanding of agentic concepts and intermediate automation knowledge recommended. The session includes demonstrations of unified policy implementation and real-world Cisco deployment scenarios.

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243032477001vnX2
Network infrastructure practitioner session based on a Cisco-on-Cisco story explaining the best practices to be followed on preparing and building a non-blocking ethernet fabric for AI workloads. This session will cover the technology stack used, key configuration considered, challenges faced while building the network infrastructure to deliver a 256 GPU compute form for AI workloads.

What will it take for Ethernet to succeed in this emerging enormous market? What's happening in the Ethernet Industry to address these needs?

### Evaluating and Improving Defenses With MITRE ATT&CK - BRKSEC-2227
Speakers: Mike McPhee (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243088836001vf9z
Security architectures can pose complexity and cast doubt. How can we be certain that the solutions we have acquired are adequate? How can we know they are configured properly to cover the many vectors of attack? Cisco, and much of the industry, have taken to using frameworks like MITRE's Adversarial Tactics, Techniques, and Common Knowledge Database (ATT&CK) to help objectively assess those solutions and their coverage of the attackers that an environment is most likely to encounter. In this session, attendees will follow along as we follow an example threat from identification to gap analysis and validation of mitigations. Together we'll: Discover the benefits of learning from the mistakes of others Learn and see how to use MITRE ATT&CK (and similar frameworks) to evaluate their blind spots See demonstrations of Parsing Cyber Threat Intelligence, Mapping Coverage, Gap Analysis, and Validating your progress with MITRE Caldera

Learn how Splunk ITSI applies AI and machine learning to help teams find and fix issues faster, and what exciting innovations and integrations, the team is planning for the future.

### Everything You Ever Wanted to Know about Power in Routers and Switches (and Quite a Bit More) - PSOGRN-2161
Speakers: LJ Wobker (Cisco), Lane Wigley (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242974994001vIpj
This session provides the foundation for a deep understanding of power usage in networking equipment. It discusses all aspects of power in routers and switches. Topics include provisioning vs. typical vs. peak power; power usage by component; how traffic, temperature, and other factors impact power usage; optimizing power supply efficiency; optics power; conversion loss; power redundancy models; thermal challenges in high power systems; modeling power consumption; and what does and doesn't move the needle on power savings during off-peak times. The session will also provide examples from system testing to support the concepts covered in this breakout.

### Evolution of the Transport Network Architecture in the Age of 5G, 6G and AI - BRKSPG-2133
Speakers: Alexander Preusche (Cisco), Shahid Ajmeri (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243061406001vt8c
The rapid expansion and inorganic growth, technological convergence, network densification, and the exponential growth in connected devices are making it increasingly difficult to manage and operate networks with traditional methods. Future networks must be intelligent and agile, leveraging Artificial Intelligence (AI) to autonomously make decisions while providing clear rationale and explanations to enable true zero-touch operations. AI is now a critical enabler of business value, driving improvements in performance, efficiency, customer experience, and unlocking new revenue opportunities. Once primarily a differentiator for hyper-scalers, AI is now being widely adopted across enterprises worldwide. As AI deployments grow and become more distributed, telecom service providers play a pivotal role in delivering the necessary connectivity and edge infrastructure to support these workloads. However, to support the evolving network topologies and traffic patterns, telecom networks must adopt a significant transformation. This session will discuss how Cisco Agile Services Networking solution is helping service providers to transform their network architecture, optimizing it for flexible service deployment and ensuring a resilient, high-quality user experience.

### Evolving VPN Services with Cisco Agile Services Networking - BRKMSI-1865

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243011097001vmfg

### Expedite your Troubleshooting with SD-WAN Manager Tools - BRKTRS-2595
Speakers: Uma Mohanty (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1771608138033001rqWZ
Cisco Catalyst SD-WAN Solution represents an evolution of networking from an older, hardware-based model to a secure, software-based, virtual IP fabric. With Legacy network it was very difficult to represent the network on the GUI and also take further troubleshooting steps based on some of the scenarios encountered on the network. SD-WAN solution being a evolved one, we not only have the GUI but also have a bag of tools that comes along with the SD-WAN Manager that can help us troubleshoot the network further and expedite the resolution to the issues. This session will focus mostly on the bag of tools that comes along with SD-WAN Manager which can be used to troubleshoot the issues further. We will discuss some of the tricky issues faced in the customers network,and how to utilize these tools in order to expedite the troubleshooting, and hence reduce the Mean time to resolution of the faults. This will be a fun session where we bring in some of the issues that have already been dealt in the past by the customers, and we will see how the SD-WAN Manager tools set helps us to identify and resolve the issues timely. The highlight of this session would be to showcase how well we can utilize the API docs on SD-WAN Manager and gather some quick data points that can be used in out troubleshooting workflow.

### Exploring Generative AI: A Beginner's Guide to Concepts and Practical Applications? - BRKAI-1031

### Exploring the Paradigm Shift in Security: AI and Quantum Cryptography's Influence on Zero Trust - BRKXAR-1009
Speakers: Saskia Laura Schröer (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243067610001v7uA
This session explores the transformative impact of AI and Post-Quantum Cryptography on Zero Trust principles. As organizations increasingly adopt Zero Trust principles, this session will guide attendees through the next steps in advancing Zero Trust within their organizations amid the paradigm shifts introduced by AI and Quantum technologies. The focus will be on foundational principles and frameworks - not products - to help you navigate this rapidly evolving security landscape. The session will lay the groundwork for you to go back to your organizations and provide strategic guidance on the paradigm shift in security, and specifically, on AI. Key Takeaways: Understanding Zero Trust, AI, and Post-Quantum Cryptography Theory: A brief overview of Post-Quantum Cryptography, AI, and Zero Trust principles, focusing on their intersections and importance in today's security landscape. Frameworks and Practical Guidelines: We will provide an overview of current frameworks and guidelines, that help you transition from theory to practice, including strategies for beginning or enhancing Zero Trust principles within your organization, focusing particularly on AI use cases. Real-World Examples: Learn from real-world scenarios illustrating successful implementations and common pitfalls to avoid. Why Attend? Broaden Your Perspective: Gain a comprehensive understanding of Zero Trust principles and their critical role in modern security strategies, involving AI and Post-Quantum Cryptography. Actionable Strategies: Leave with a roadmap for implementing Zero Trust principles based on your organization's unique needs related to the paradigm shift in security.

### Fabric Networks for OT: The History (IP MPLS), Today (SD-Access) and Emerging Trends (SRv6 and BGP EVPN) - BRKIOT-2115
Speakers: Pete Kavanagh (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242966323001vcQY
Network fabric techniques have been used for many years in large OT WANs, however increasingly OT LAN is being fabric-enabled, and customers are looking at an end-to-end fabric strategy across OT WAN and LAN. MPLS-over-IP is commonly deployed on the OT WAN, SD-Access is being deployed on the OT LAN, and a lot of customers are asking about SRv6 and BGP EVPN. As well as reviewing the different fabric technologies, this session will cover some real customer scenarios, and we'll discuss the various design elements to consider; expect an engaging, hands-on discussion with architecture walkthroughs, design examples, and live audience interaction.

### Federated Search: Bringing Splunk's Value to Your Distributed Data Estate - BRKENS-2279

### Finding Real Anomalies in Meraki Telemetry with the Dashboard API - DEVNET-2882
Speakers: Joe Tansey (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1771608143357001r6es
Most "anomalies" are just noisy spikes. This session shows how to use the Meraki Dashboard API and an unsupervised machine learning model (Isolation Forest/Sklearn) to learn 'your' normal and flag what actually departs from it. We'll walk step-by-step from extracting time series (client counts, WAN usage, switch port stats) to training a baseline on six months of data, then enriching alerts with 24-hour context so humans can act fast. No labels required, no re-platforming - just a small Python job beside the tools you already run. We'll cover seasonality, drift, thresholds that won't page you at 3 a.m., and the simple review loop that keeps false positives low.

### From CLI to the Cloud: Transforming Cisco Networking device management with Cloud Native IOS XE - BRKENS-2357

Moving from a box-by-box approach to a cloud-operated network, enables engineers to shift their focus from time-consuming configuration tasks to delivering true value through network services for end users. The advent of Cloud Native IOS XE, a single operating system for switching, wireless, and routing, introduces a range of new possibilities for managing and monitoring networking devices using the Cisco Meraki Cloud management platform, including centralized management, assurance, and software lifecycle management. In this session, attendees will be guided through a series of step-by-step demos on how to onboard Cisco Catalyst switches and access points to the Meraki dashboard. page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242987090001vll2
Operational data is exploding just as agentic AI is ready to transform how work gets done, but fragmented metrics, events, logs, and traces are too costly to move, too risky to ignore, and too inconsistent for AI to truly trust. In this session, you'll see how the Cisco Data Fabric architecture evolves Splunk into a unified, AI ready analytics fabric that keeps data where it lives while making it readily accessible by assistants and autonomous agents. We'll explore how a governed Machine Data Lake, federated search, and an AI powered metadata catalog and knowledge graph turn raw machine data into consistent, contextual "truth" that AI can safely act on shrinking investigation times and unlocking efficiencies with automation. You'll also learn how to align spend to business outcomes with tiered storage and "promote what matters" strategies, all while preserving existing Splunk investments and avoiding disruptive migrations or vendor lock in.

### From Chaos to Clarity: How Cisco Cloud Control Uses AI to Simplify Multi-Domain Operations - AI-1536
Speakers: Manu Anand (Cisco), Richard Jang (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243000981001vVHO
Managing Cisco's networking, security, collaboration, and observability tools once meant juggling dashboards, alerts, and silos. Cisco Cloud Control changes that by becoming the AI-native front door to your entire Cisco estate. In this session, see how purpose built agentic AI, embedded directly into the platform-not bolted on-works across your topology, asset inventory, and operations to transform complexity into clarity. We'll show how Cloud Control's unified Mission Control Homepage surfaces the work AI Agents are already doing for you, from automatically identifying FTD firewalls nearing end-of-support to correlating Splunk and SD-WAN events to find the root cause of an outage. You'll also experience AI-driven investigations inside Unified Inventory and Unified Topology where a simple prompt like "show all congested WAN paths in Europe" triggers guided troubleshooting and safe, reversible actions. Walk away seeing how Cisco Cloud Control's purpose-built intelligence reduces toil, speeds resolution, and lets teams focus on what matters most: delivering reliable, secure experiences across domains.

### From Chaos to Control: Practical Segmentation Strategies for Industrial Networks - BRKIOT-2588
Speakers: Anders C Pedersen (Cisco), Inigo Alonso (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242967727001v7dr
Industrial networks are becoming more complex and vital to operational technology (OT) environments, making robust segmentation essential for security and operational continuity. This session offers a practical guide to implementing segmentation in both existing and new industrial networks, focusing on designing, deploying, and managing strategies that align with ISA/IEC 62443 standards. Attendees will gain insights into: Core segmentation concepts, including the creation of zones and conduits to control communication flows and limit lateral threat movement Deploying network-based solutions to automate and enforce segmentation policies dynamically, empowering OT teams to enhance security with reduced reliance on IT Applying a range of segmentation methods, from VLAN-based logical segmentation to micro-segmentation using scalable groups, and integrating Zero Trust principles tailored for industrial environments After attending this session, you will be able to: Identify and address common challenges such as complex firewall rule management, building, maintaining segmentation in OT environments, and minimizing risks of misconfiguration Establish visibility and asset identification as foundations for effective segmentation Implement best practices for planning, vendor collaboration, and scalable policy enforcement to ensure sustainable segmentation aligned with operational needs Audience expectations: This session is ideal for cybersecurity and network professionals, as well as OT managers, who have a foundational understanding of industrial network concepts. Familiarity with network security and basic segmentation principles is recommended. Real-world examples, practical approaches, and actionable guidance will be featured to help you apply these strategies in your organization immediately.

Learn how AI Defense secures your AI operations, with an emphasis on new capabilities like MCP scanning and securing agent-to-agent (A2A) communications. Attend this session to see how Cisco AI Defense enables your organization to harness the power of agentic AI, securely and confidently.

### From Gen-AI Paradox to Enterprise Transformation: JARVIS Agentic AI at Scale - AI-1645
Speakers: Steffen Preuss (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1771608123999001rfNk
Modern organizations face a critical choice: continue relying on isolated digital silos that stifle innovation, or embrace collaborative, agentic AI architectures that enable transformative impact. The core challenge as the Gen? AI paradox: although generative AI is widely deployed, it has limits to drive meaningful business results. Agentic AI is the proposed solution, capable of automating complex workflows across systems, planning proactively, and executing with autonomy and memory. Such integration converts generative AI from a passive assistant to a strategic collaborator. This session introduces JARVIS - Outshift by Cisco, built on the open?source AI platform engineering framework AGNTCY, as a strategic solution to that dilemma. ### From IPv4 to IPv6: AI-Validated Standalone Cisco Deployments with MCP and pyATS - BRKIPV-2033

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242950207001vHVB
IPv6 adoption is accelerating across the industry, but many networks still rely on standalone routers and switches without centralized controllers and thus making migration a manual, time-consuming, and error-prone process. This session demonstrates how AI-powered integration of Model Context Protocol (MCP) and Cisco pyATS can bridge that gap. Attendees will follow a step-by-step workflow where pyATS automatically discovers devices and extracts their IPv4 configurations, MCP uses AI to analyze and translate them into IPv6-ready configurations aligned with Cisco configuration guides, and policy validation ensures readiness before rollout. This approach enables consistent, compliant, and scalable IPv6 implementation in non-controller-managed environments. You'll leave this session with ideas to help you: Integrate MCP and pyATS to enable AI-assisted IPv6 adoption in standalone Cisco networks Automate configuration collection, normalization, and translation using proven workflows Validate generated IPv6 configurations against Cisco best practices before deployment Recommended Audience: Network engineers, architects, and automation specialists familiar with IPv4 configuration who are looking to streamline IPv6 adoption in environments without centralized controllers.

### From Rack to Fabric: Blueprinting AI-Ready Infrastructure - BRKOPS-2427
Speakers: Christopher Liljenstolpe (Cisco), Zubair Khan (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242968542001vGi6
As the number of AI workloads surges and their computational requirements explode across industries, designing a future-ready data center capable of supporting high-throughput training and inference at scale has become a strategic imperative. This immersive session will walk you through the complete lifecycle of building an AI-native data center from the ground up - starting with physical infrastructure, all the way to cloud-integrated service orchestration. We'll begin with the physical layer: optimal rack layouts, high-density cabling, liquid cooling technologies including multiple bus bar topologies to deliver the necessary power, and RDHx, CDUs, and manifolds designed for next-gen thermal efficiency to reject the heat. Then, we'll dive deep into fabric architecture, focusing on AMD as well as NVIDIA GPU-based compute nodes connected over a high-performance RoCEv2 backend. You'll learn how to architect for scale using Cisco's end-to-end Ethernet fabric, including front-end networking, ingress/egress connectivity, Meet-Me rooms, and Internet Gateway (IGW) integrations. We'll also explore how SRv6 can be used to seamlessly extend your data center infrastructure across locations. Security is foundational in AI deployments. The session will showcase an AI data center security blueprint using Cisco Integrated Rack Designs, NGFW, XDR, Isovalent Cilium, Splunk, and Hypershield - complemented by the Security Trust Dashboard for continuous risk visibility, and the ability to leverage Cisco CX's knowledge and capabilities to integrate these new infrastructures in the the rest of your (or your customers) IT estate .Crucially, we'll demonstrate how GPU services can be orchestrated with intent-based provisioning and visibility, ensuring agile service delivery across compute, fabric, and storage layers. Attendees will get to explore and learn: How to design and scale AMD and NVIDIA GPU-powered AI data centers using RoCEv2 fabric Best practices for integrating cooling, power, and rack infrastructure tailored for AI workloads Ingress and egress design principles, including SRv6-based extension for hybrid AI clusters Strategies to orchestrate GPU-as-a-Service using a unified policy and provisioning framework Security architecture for AI data centers with Cisco's integrated SecOps stack Real-time visibility tools to ensure trust, compliance, and risk assurance

### From YAML to Fabric: Automating VXLAN EVPN with NetAsCode and NDFC - DEVNET-2509
Speakers: Nour Nada (Cisco)

Join this session to see how Cisco is leveraging the circular economy to reduce energy use and e-waste, drive cost savings, lower greenhouse gas emissions associated with the product life cycle, improve supply chain resiliency, and respond to government regulations - and how this helps your organization address its business and sustainability goals. Get hands-on with Cisco's services, like Cisco Refresh and the Product Takeback and Reuse Program. You will leave with: Insights into how AI is reducing product lifecycles and how circularity can reduce e-waste and support longevity Knowledge on how to leverage Cisco's circular solutions, including Cisco Refresh and the Product Takeback and Reuse Program, to help meet your needs Strategies to leverage Cisco's Circular Design Principles to meet new EU regulations on sustainability and product design Insights on how to incorporate certified remanufactured products into your stack to reduce emissions and accelerate project timelines Tools for life cycle assessments and product carbon footprints to make data-driven decisions and meet compliance and sustainability reporting requirements
