### Future-Proofing Secure WAN: Fabric Security and Navigating the Quantum Threat with PQC - BRKENT-2915
Speakers: Ahmet Uncu (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1771608136398001r7Of
This session will first delve into the Cisco SD-WAN fabric and how its inherent security features deliver robust fabric security. Following this, we will address the approaching threat of quantum computing to current encryption standards and outline strategies for future-proofing your Secure WAN. We'll explain the "Harvest Now, Decrypt Later" risk, introduce Post-Quantum Cryptography (PQC) concepts, and discuss Cisco's approach to quantum-safe networking. Learn how to build a crypto-agile SD-WAN fabric that can adapt to evolving cryptographic standards, ensuring long-term data confidentiality and integrity against quantum adversaries.

### Future-Ready Connectivity: Cisco Mobility Services with AI Powered IoT and Edge Computing - PSOMSI-2234

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243009911001vpmT
Telecom providers face unprecedented pressure to deliver high bandwidth, low-latency services, monetize 5G, and manage the data surge from billions of connected devices. Cisco's Mobility Services Platform offers a cloud-native converged core that simplifies operations, lowers investment risk, and accelerates deployment of managed 5G across enterprise verticals. Building on this foundation, Cisco is advancing key innovations in IoT Control Center and an AI-native mobile core. We'll cover embedded AI for IoT for real-time device intelligence, distributed edge compute for localized workloads, and eSIM Orchestration to enable seamless global connectivity and dynamic service provisioning. At the distributed edge, these capabilities bring compute, routing, and security closer to users, delivering faster response, improved workload orchestration, and stronger data protection. Attendees will learn how these innovations help mobile operators and enterprises unlock new revenue streams-- spanning IoT, private 5G, and next-gen applications, while creating a scalable, future-ready infrastructure for the era of intelligent connectivity.

### Gain Scalable, Deep Visibility into Network Traffic with Cisco Nexus Dashboard Data Broker - BRKDCN-1614
Speakers: Richard Licon (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243042386001vUKs
Visibility into network and application traffic has become increasingly important in our modern data centers. Network visibility is integral to network security, compliance, troubleshooting, and capacity planning. Custom monitoring solutions cannot cope with the explosive growth of data center networks. In order to match the port density and port capabilities of the data center, monitoring networks must also leverage those same switches used in the production network. Cisco Nexus Dashboard Data Broker (NDDB) is an automated, intelligent packet brokering solution that can scale with your data center. NDDB provides a software-defined, programmable solution that can aggregate, filter, and replicate network traffic using SPAN or optical TAPs for network monitoring and visibility. This solution offers a simple, scalable, and cost-effective solution that is well suited to customers who have a need for monitoring large amounts of traffic or business-critical traffic. This session will cover the following: Introduction to Nexus Dashboard Data Broker TAP and SPAN aggregation use case In-line monitoring use case Integration with other Cisco products Demonstration of the NDDB interface Product Demo

### GenAI Your Way: Reimagine Network Troubleshooting - BRKOPS-3822
Speakers: Gabi Zapodeanu (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243066783001vQyV
Join us as we reimagine network troubleshooting with Generative AI (GenAI). Traditional troubleshooting can be a time-consuming and complex process, requiring expertise and manual effort. What if you could simplify the process by building your own AI-powered solution using open-source models? In this session, we'll explore how GenAI will transform network troubleshooting, freeing you to focus more on on issue-solving. It will reduce the time spent on data collection and analysis of the network configuration, operational state, and logs. You'll discover how to leverage GenAI to gain real-time insights into network issues, extracting with ease valuable details from large datasets, for a faster issue resolution. We'll review the core concepts to build your AI platform, including Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG), vector representations and databases, Large Language Models (LLMs), Function and Tool Calling. Prior experience with GenAI and LLMs is beneficial, but not required. Demos and complete open-source GenAI solution sample code will be included. Don't miss this opportunity to reimagine network troubleshooting - GenAI Your Way.

### Generative AI Demystified: How Cisco is Empowering the Future with LLMs - BRKXAR-2019

We will explore how Cisco is unlocking the potential of GenAI and LLMs across our portfolio, from Cisco Security Cloud, to Enterprise Network systems, to Webex.

### Get Out of the 90s: Cross-domain Troubleshooting with Cisco APIs and Splunk - BRKOPS-2556

Are you fed up with your users blaming the network every single day? Do you waste vast amounts of time chasing down outages and security events across too many platforms? Your Cisco infrastructure is full of real-time data, telemetry, alerts and APIs. Yet your monitoring is stuck in 1999, with a classic mix of SNMP interface graphs, a pile of logs, and emails nobody reads. Is anybody even catching SNMP traps anymore? Become the hero of your network's story. In this session, you will learn how to utilize the APIs already available in your network and security infrastructure to access troubleshooting data and telemetry from multiple sources, combine them in a meaningful way, and leverage AI to take better action faster. ### Global Regulations On AI: Insights Before Action - AI-1380
Speakers: Vardaan Singh (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1771608123393001rg58
Artificial Intelligence (AI) is the buzzword of this century with countless applications across diverse domains. The field is rapidly evolving with vendors swiftly competing to utilize its capabilities. However, governments and authorities worldwide recognize both the potential and risks of AI if mismanaged. As a result, it has attracted significant global scrutiny as well. With the recently enacted landmark EU (European Union) legislation on classifying and supervising AI, it's crucial for Cisco's engineers and management professionals to stay updated on the global regulations and sentiments towards AI ensuring their products and strategies are compliant minimizing legal and administrative roadblocks. This session aims to: Compare and contrast the existing domestic AI laws and trends in EMEA, AMER, and APJC regions Discuss the impacts and sentiments of the AI Seoul Summit and the Paris AI Action Summit Conduct an ideation session to identify potential socio-cultural and political risks of AI and develop strategies for effective compliance and innovation By attending, attendees will gain valuable insights into navigating the complex AI landscape further futureproofing their innovations.

### Help Yourself. Building a Network Info Bot with GenAI and MCP - BRKOPS-2164
Speakers: Joe Clarke (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242973402001v2gs
In my role as a core engineer at the Cisco Live Europe Network Operations Center (NOC), I frequently encountered a persistent need for a self-service interface to streamline workflows. NOC engineers often needed to quickly locate devices, reserve DHCP leases, or gain insights into network operations. My initial attempt to solve this problem involved creating a chatbot for Webex. While it worked, the solution quickly spiraled into a spaghetti mess of regular expressions struggling to parse IP addresses, MAC addresses, and usernames effectively. Fast forward to 2025: with the explosion of generative AI capabilities and the GPUs we deployed for Cisco Live Amsterdam, I saw an opportunity to reimagine this tool. Leveraging GenAI on top of Ollama and incorporating Model Context Protocol (MCP) for dynamic context-switching, I developed a next-generation "Info Bot". This AI-powered agent integrates with network operations systems and uses MCP to intelligently manage context, ensuring more relevant and accurate responses in complex environments. In this session, I'll guide you through: Setting up your own instance of Ollama as the foundation of a GenAI-powered tool Developing an MCP server to improve your bot's ability to manage dynamic, multi-tool interactions and context switching Building an AI-powered Info Bot that integrates with NetOps systems to deliver actionable network insights while maintaining operational safety and security

### Hijacking Trust: Weaponizing Stolen Cookies and Tokens - BRKSEC-2132
Speakers: Stefan Duernberger (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242947331001vpW4
In a world where digital trust is constantly under siege, attackers are increasingly targeting session cookies and tokens to hijack user identities and bypass security controls. This session opens with a deep dive into real-world session hijacking techniques - demonstrating how attackers leverage advanced proxy tools such as Evilginx and EvilnoVNC to steal authentication cookies and compromise user sessions. We will then explore OAuth 2.0 and OIDC, examining how these protocols facilitate secure and seamless user authentication across platforms. Attendees will gain insights into Google Device-Bound Session Credentials (DBSC) and Cisco Duo Passport, highlighting their innovative approaches to identity security. Finally, we will conclude with an overview of Shared Signals and Events, emphasizing their importance in maintaining a secure and responsive authentication environment. This session is tailored for Security Architects and Administrators looking to understand both the offensive and defensive sides of authentication, and seeking actionable insights for Zero Trust architectures. Walk away with the knowledge to recognize and counter advanced threats against trust in your authentication flows.

### How AIDA Cruises Built Continuous Remote Access Across an Entire Fleet with Smart Out of Band Management - PARENS-1708

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242953095001veaN
Ensuring network access across a modern cruise fleet is a unique challenge. Ships operate as mobile data centers and every guest-facing service depends on connectivity. AIDA Cruises needed a reliable way to troubleshoot and manage over 60,000 onboard network devices, even when primary links were unavailable. In this session, Diogo Almeida, Head of Network, Connectivity and Infrastructure at AIDA Cruises, will share how the company introduced Smart Out of Band management using Opengear across eleven ships and onshore environments, enabling remote configuration, firmware updates, and troubleshooting without dispatching technicians. Diogo will cover: How a dedicated maintenance network was built across the fleet Key requirements for continuous remote access at sea How console access accelerated recovery and reduced operational overhead Measurable operational impact, including faster troubleshooting and ROI in under nine months Attendees will walk away with practical insights applicable to distributed networks, critical locations, and remote operational teams.

### How to Network LLMs. Splitting Large Language Models Running on GPUs Across a Network - BRKOPS-2931
Speakers: Dave Zacks (Cisco), Patrick leMaistre (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243092340001vR9F
We all know that LLMs can do some amazing things. But as capable as they are, they often don't fit in a single GPU or even a single server with multiple GPUs. Enter the network - split the AI models across multiple hosts and use the network to link them - to address this gap. Come to this session to learn how a properly designed network can distribute your AI model across multiple systems and use the network to link them - on-premises with private, local data sets. This session is delivered by the dynamic duo of Patrick LeMaistre and Dave Zacks, both distinguished Cisco Live speakers. Come to this session for a fun, engaging deep dive on how the networks are critical in hosting large AI models - all within 60 minutes from start to finish.

### How to Optimize SaaS Applications using Cisco Catalyst SD-WAN - BRKENT-2630
Speakers: Lars Granberg (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1771608135404001rsut

Catalyst SD-WAN offers Cloud OnRamp for SaaS to optimize SaaS applications and provides a seamless way to use internet circuits for SaaS applications. Cloud OnRamp for SaaS helps mitigate SaaS user experience challenges with unreliable circuit performance changes, and Optimize SaaS user experience, by leveraging path probing and failover to alternate best paths when circuit performance changes. In this session, we are going to cover the latest innovations around Cloud OnRamp for SaaS and how we can further enhance SaaS optimization for Microsoft 365, Webex, etc. and Custom Enterprise SaaS applications using the Cloud OnRamp for SaaS and cover how to Optimize SaaS applications when going via SSE.

### IP Measurements (IPM) Solution Blueprint for AI-Ready SP Networks - BRKMSI-2463
Speakers: Amish Servansing (Cisco), Ianik Semco (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242964367001vbCe
The explosive growth of AI workloads is fundamentally transforming network demands, necessitating ultra-low latency, high throughput, and deterministic performance. This session will equip attendees with a solution blueprint that leverages Cisco's Agile Services architecture, including advanced hardware and observability capabilities. Service provider network engineering teams will gain the confidence and tools needed to identify, mitigate, and optimize network resources for critical AI workloads. Attendees will also discover, through a real production service provider deployment, how to: Achieve Scalable Overlay Monitoring: Implement highly accurate, active SLA measurements from any edge to any edge, leveraging Silicon One's 14 million probes across all ECMP paths Optimize Paths with Underlay Correlation: Correlate active measurements with underlay routing for intelligent path optimization and robust SLA validation Drive Proactive AI Assurance: Utilize AI-driven performance baselining, anomaly detection, and proactive remediation to safeguard AI workload performance Gain End-to-End Cross-Layer Visibility: Correlate IP measurements with application-layer performance (e.g., LLM metrics) for comprehensive network and AI application insight

### IPv6 Security in the Local Area with First Hop Security - BRKENT-3002
Speakers: Eric Vyncke (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1771608136632001rNe4
A big difference in the security between IPv4 and IPv6 are the layer-2/layer-3 interactions, as DHCP is optional in IPv6 and ARP is replaced by Neighbor Discovery Protocol (NDP). Legacy IPv4 attacks such as ARP or DHCP spoofing have their equivalent in IPv6. Cisco has developed techniques for many years to secure this interaction in the local area (WLAN, LAN, SD-Access, Meraki, ACI, etc). This session explains the attacks, and how Cisco can help protect your networks with short demonstrations. A good knowledge of IPv6 is required, especially NDP.

### IPv6 Transition Technologies - IPv4aaS - BRKIPV-2282
Speakers: Anthony Belcastro (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242985396001vP7H
As the world rapidly exhausts IPv4 addresses, service providers are increasingly deploying IPv6-only networks. This shift presents a significant challenge for IT teams needing to maintain connectivity for legacy IPv4 applications and services, while embracing the future of networking. This session is your guide to navigating this complex landscape using the intuitive power of the Cisco Meraki platform. This session will explore the critical IPv6 transition mechanisms now available in the Cisco Meraki dashboard. Attendees will gain a comprehensive understanding of how technologies like Network Address Translation 64 (NAT64), 464XLAT, Dual-Stack Lite (DS-Lite), and Mapping of Address and Port with Encapsulation/Translation (MAP-E/MAP-T) function to ensure seamless connectivity. You will also learn how to select and configure the appropriate transition strategy for your specific network environment, simplifying what has traditionally been a complex undertaking and ensuring you are IPv6 ready. This is an intermediate-level session. A solid understanding of fundamental networking concepts, including IP families, addressing and routing is recommended. Prior experience with the Cisco dashboard is beneficial but not required.

### IPv6:: It's Happening - BRKIPV-2191
Speakers: Nathan Sherrard (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243097053001vhTf
IPv6 has had a long and winding history, with many organizations ignoring it. Yet in recent years, its use has exploded, and we are now at a point where everyone needs to take it seriously and get ready for an IPv6-only future. If you're looking at the steps toward enabling IPv6, and are wondering where and how to start, this is the session for you. We will look at recent adoption trends and government mandates that are forcing the issue, do a walkthrough on the protocol itself, and finally explore some best practices around addressing plans and transition technologies. This session is targeted for enterprise, commercial, and public sector customers who are only just beginning their IPv6 adoption. We will not be discussing issues relating to service provider and mobile network providers. No IPv6 knowledge is required to attend, though basic familiarity with IPv4 networking is assumed. If you attended the TECIPV Technical Seminars, you do not need to attend this session.

### ISE APIs in Practice - Better Endpoint Profiling with Network Packet Analyzers - DEVNET-2292
Speakers: Taylor Cook (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243055976001vtHj
Cisco ISE has long supported integrations with 3rd party systems to ingest additional contextual data for endpoints to enhance Network Access Control policy decisions. These integrations can be built using either ISE's Platform Exchange Grid (pxGrid) or the growing library of ISE OpenAPIs. This session will provide an overview of an ISE integration built using open-source packet analyzers to inspect network traffic and gain additional context for profiling endpoint devices. You will receive an overview of ISE APIs and existing publicly available toolsets, as well as learn how to use analyze live traffic across various protocols to learn more about devices on the network. This session will then showcase how to combine information learned from traffic analysis with the ISE platform to improve network policies. Attendees are encouraged, but not required, to have a basic understanding of Python, REST APIs, Wireshark, and network packet structure (OSI model).

### ISE Profiling Architecture and Best Practices - BRKSEC-3519
Speakers: Alex Bubnov (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243027497001v9Km
This is an advanced, deep-dive session focusing on ISE Profiling component. Drawing on extensive experience from the TAC escalation teams, the main goal of this session is to demonstrate ISE Profiling architecture, configuration best practices, and advanced troubleshooting methodologies. The session also includes performance impact assessment for ISE deployments handling the Profiler service, as well as load-balancing use cases, affecting Endpoint Profiling processing on ISE. This session features real-world troubleshooting scenarios, such as parallel processing of endpoint attributes received by ISE from multiple sources like RADIUS, DHCP, Multi-Factor Classification (MFC) profiler, pxGrid Probe, etc. - based on cases observed in the field. The target audience for this session includes network architects, consultants, engineers, and network designers who are seeking a deeper understanding of the Profiling component on ISE, as well as the knowledge to avoid common mistakes in their day-to-day operations, design, and deployment activities.

### Identity Intelligence Demystified - BRKSEC-2162
Speakers: Aaron Woland (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243085089001vah2
"Why hack in when I can log in?" The market is abuzz with the talk of Cisco Identity Intelligence (CII), which comes to Cisco via the acquisition of Oort. What is identity security all about? What types of attacks and risks are we seeing in the wild? What we see in the real world has shown us that organizations have more than one Identity Providers (IdP) like Okta, Micrsoft EntraID and Duo SSO, but the security team isn't getting a complete or holistic view across the different IdPs. Come join Distinguished Engineer, Aaron Woland, as he talks all about identity threats and Cisco Identity Intelligence. How it is part of Cisco XDR and Duo Security, integrations, how it relates to the Identity Services Engine (ISE) and most importantly how to improve your Security Posture by getting a hold of the identity related threats.

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243062206001vE6h
Artificial Intelligence (AI) is set to revolutionize every industry and the way users interact with the digital world. It's still early phases of AI adoption, and still unclear what are the deployment models and AI technics that will become more prevalent. However, it's already apparent that AI traffic patterns are different from traditional internet traffic, and that the use of AI frameworks such as RAG (Retrieval-Augmented Generation), grounding, AI agents and AI distribution to the edge, will have an impact on transport network design considerations. This session includes an analysis of the AI traffic characteristics for a set of AI use cases, and their impact on WAN and DCI design requirements.

### Infrastructure as Code for NXOS and NDFC with Ansible - BRKDCN-2946
Speakers: Matt Tarkington (Cisco), Mike Wiebe (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243040560001vdrX
In this session, we'll explore how Ansible and NXOS can be used to provide various automation and orchestration capabilities to operate your VXLAN EVPN data center fabric using Infrastructure of Code (IaC) principles. Ansible is widely adopted and used across the industry as a foundation for IaC, which uses established best practices for software development and applies them to your network. Network configuration is stored in a structured manner using a source control management system like GitLab, and becomes the source of truth for making network changes. In the session, we will walk through getting started with a development environment for Ansible and the Ansible NXOS collection. Next, we will define an Ansible playbook structure using available NXOS modules. We will then show you how you can store your Ansible playbook(s) in GitLab as a source of truth and build a CI/CD pipeline to push network configuration changes. The focus will then transition into how you use the same Ansible environment and IaC concepts to manage your VXLAN EVPN fabric using Nexus Dashboard Fabric Controller (NDFC) and corresponding Ansible collection. NDFC is the management controller for all NXOS-enabled deployments and offers a robust northbound-REST API for Ansible modules to leverage. Finally, we will build upon our GitLab CI/CD pipeline to make network changes to our NDFC-managed fabric.

### Infrastructure as Context with AI - DEVNET-2338
Speakers: Shreyas Srish (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242971538001v0kU
Artificial Intelligence (AI) is rapidly transforming how complex systems are managed, opening new avenues for automating, optimizing, and orchestrating operations. This session brings Infrastructure as Context to life by leveraging Large Language Models (LLMs) alongside code-as-core logic to translate natural-language prompts into Application Centric Infrastructure (ACI) configurations and push them directly to the fabric. By keeping core logic in code and delegating the language-centric work to the model, this approach delivers both robustness and conversational intelligence, getting the best of both worlds. In the context of ACI, this session will: Equip you with a clear understanding of the Infrastructure as Context paradigm Demonstrate a live application that uses natural language to generate and apply ACI configurations Show you how to combine LLMs with deterministic code for robust, conversational automation Recommended prerequisites: Basic familiarity with LLM and ACI fundamentals.

### Introduction to Segment Routing - BRKSP-2551
Speakers: Alberto Donzelli (Cisco), Alexey Gorovoy (NEBIUS)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243064222001v9Sq
This session provides an introduction to Segment Routing and its use cases. As a modern routing paradigm, Segment Routing delivers exceptional operational simplicity, scalability, and flexibility - making it the preferred technology for implementing IP transport networks across Service Providers, Enterprises, and Critical Infrastructures. Attendees will gain a solid understanding of the fundamental concepts behind Segment Routing across both MPLS and IPv6 forwarding planes. The session will cover its broad applicability, ranging from automated protection, Traffic Engineering and Network Slicing along with the enhanced performance and analytics capabilities that are essential for advancing toward autonomous networking. The session will also feature a Segment routing implementation from Nebius, showcasing their current backbone interconnect with one of their big-tech customers. It will also describe Nebius' design case study and roadmap on migrating their frontend AI DC architecture to Segment Routing IPv6 by replacing traditionl VXLAN and MPLS protocols stacks, leveraging simple yet powerful solution, including service chaining and traffic engineering techniques.

### Introduction to VXLAN: The Future Path of Your Data Center - BRKDCN-1621
Speakers: Richard Licon (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243042828001vSi4
Learn more about VXLAN and delve deeper into it. This session will cover what VXLAN is, how it works and the problems it solves. The need for flexibility in the data center opens the door to network overlays. Several standards have been proposed and implemented to enable virtualized networks to be layered over a physical network. The data center network needs to be flexible enough to support workloads that can move from one host to another, and for new services to be deployed rapidly. VXLAN is one such technology that provides massively scalable virtual network overlays on top of existing IP infrastructures. This session will include 101s and packet walks as we cover the insides of VXLAN. No prior knowledge of VXLAN is required. A basic understanding of unicast and multicast routing protcol is needed, along with an understanding of MP BGP.

### Introduction to eBPF - BRKSEC-2169
Speakers: Liz Rice (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243084108001vpus
eBPF is an industry-changing technology that is enabling a new generation of security, networking and observability tools. This session provides an in-depth exploration of its origins, rewards, and challenges, with live demos to give you concrete explanations of what eBPF is, and how it enables advanced, high performance capabilities. You'll leave this talk understanding why eBPF has been called "Superpowers for Linux"

### Keep Your Cisco Catalyst Wireless Network Healthy - Wireless Config Analyzer Express and Other Advanced Tools - BRKEWN-3006
Speakers: Javier Contreras (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243080777001vtBz

Wireless config analyzer express (WCAE) is the evolution of the well-known WLCCA tool, now adapted to work on the cloud, supporting 9800/AireOS and offering multi-platform access. The tool will perform close to 500 total configuration checks, all coming from the collective experience and learning of worldwide Cisco Wireless Technical Assistance Center (TAC) and escalation teams, with a large list of RF analysis features developed for internal use, now in your hands.

### Keeping Up with Zero Trust - BRKSEC-2176
Speakers: Jatin Sachdeva (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243088597001vdVy
Enforcing Zero Trust least privilege access has failed to delivered a simple user experience. Come see the updated Zero Trust requirements that will help you get Zero Trust back on track. This session will cover the top most commonly deployed use cases (i.e. ZTNA) that spans from user to workload/data/application(north-south traffic) and workload/data/application to workload/data/application (east-west traffic). Demos of each use case showing the user access experience and the administrator's experience will be provided. Attendees will walk away understanding that the Zero Trust journey must include holistic access policies that span end-to-end.

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242970936001v6si

### Kubernetes (K8s) Infrastructure Connectivity for the Modern Data Center - BRKDCN-2663
Speakers: Camillo Rossi (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243043725001vglD
Kubernetes platforms serve as the cornerstone of cloud-native applications, with the process of bootstrapping a Kubernetes cluster now simplified to just a few commands. However, selecting and configuring an appropriate Container Network Interface (CNI) remains a complex yet critical task for establishing a scalable and reliable Kubernetes infrastructure. With a variety of CNI solutions available, determining which ones integrate most effectively with Cisco NX-OS and ACI is essential for making informed design choices. In this session, we will delve into the capabilities of Cilium and explore strategies for seamless integration with Cisco ACI and NX-OS based fabrics. We will discuss methods for implementing network segmentation, Service LoadBalancing and demonstrate how to enhance visibility across container and network environments. This exploration aims to empower attendees with the knowledge to optimize their Kubernetes networking configurations, ensuring robust and efficient infrastructure.

### Kubernetes Networking - Using the Defaults vs Optimizing Your Setup - DEVNET-2485
Speakers: Conor Murphy (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242972804001vll1
New to Kubernetes networking and wanting to find out more? This is the session for you. It will first provide an overview of the default behaviour of Kubernetes networking, answering questions like "What is a CNI ?" and "How are Kubernetes Services implemented?". We then look at options to tune the environment for performance, visibility, and security including replacing IPTables with eBPF and Cilium as well as native routing design to a network fabric such as ACI.

### Lab Up For Success With Cisco Modeling Labs - BRKCRT-2059
Speakers: Francois Caen (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243031407001vCK0
No matter what Cisco certification you're preparing for, you know you need to do more than just read the book to be successful. You need to practice. You can scour the internet looking for used gear, but when you get it, you still need the space to rack it, not to mention the power and cooling. Cisco Modeling Labs (CML) is a tool that provides you the virtual gear you need to practice for several Cisco certification exams with almost limitless topology possibilities. What's more, you can "plug" packet sniffers in with just a few clicks, introduce link problems, and even connect to physical kit you do have. In this session, we will explore how you can use CML to setup your own home labs. We'll dive into features such as traffic generation, packet capture, and external connectivity. Example topologies will be shared for a few scenarios to kick-start your studying and set you up for success.

### Leading Through Crisis: How Cisco Crisis Response Leverages Tech and Talent - IF-1167

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242960582001vmpv
Celebrate two decades of Cisco Crisis Response (CCR) a global leader in delivering rapid, pro-bono communications and IT infrastructure in times of disaster and gain unique insight into how CCR has become an integral part of Cisco's efforts, working alongside agencies and NGOs around the world. Speakers: Chintan Patel (Cisco), Nathan Jokel (Cisco), Nicole Isaac (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243091715001vZu2

We'll share how Cisco's strategy and approach to global policy uniquely positions us to help our customers in EMEA and beyond navigate the opportunities and challenges of today's environment.

### Lessons From the Agentic Frontier: How the SOC is Winning in the AI Era - CENSEC-1203
Speakers: David Dalling (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242979374001vdJw
Agentic AI is rapidly pushing security teams into uncharted territory - where the lines between defenders and machines are getting blurred, where the mission is shifting, and where a new generation of threats threatens to overwhelm traditional defenses. To survive and thrive in the AI era, organizations are moving to the Agentic SOC with Splunk's industry-leading SecOps platform, Enterprise Security. In this new operating model, defenders are evolving from manual alert responders into proactive orchestrators of intelligent, automated ecosystems, increasingly leveraging and guiding systems that learn, adapt, and scale. Splunk has been working side by side with our own SOC and thousands of global teams on the front lines of the Agentic transformation. We'll discuss what we've learned so far: the most compelling insights, surprising lessons, and practical strategies for building a modern Agentic SOC that is human led, machine accelerated, and ready for what comes next.

### Lessons Navigating from Proof of Concept to Production with IP/Optical Convergence: How DT is Evolving their Transport Network with Cisco Routed Optical Networking - CSSENS-2717
Speakers: Nils Warnke (Deutsche Telekom), Silke Bauten (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242953872001vHGs
A strategic component of the DT Horizontal Digital Architecture network evolution project has been to integrate their IP and optical domains through the implementation of Cisco Routed Optical Networking. The key benefits to this approach include optimized Capex across IP and optical platforms and lower power consumption with reduced hardware needs. By converging standalone optical components into coherent pluggable optics deployed in routed ports, DT is projecting over 85% in energy savings. But how does an organization move from adopting a technology vision to operational execution in production environments? In this session you will hear from Nils Warnke, DT Lead Architect, on the lessons learned from integrating roles and responsibilities across IP and optical domains. You will learn how DT approached production readiness across four key areas: Hardware qualification of pluggable modules in Cisco and 3rd party host platforms Process automation to design integrated workflows that accelerate the lead-time to bring service Operations and assurance for identifying and correlating alarms and ensuring continuous operations Field readiness to ensure capacity planning that supports expanded rollouts

### Let Cisco Catalyst Center be your Guide to a Zero-Trust Workplace - BRKOPS-2683
Speakers: Felix Meixner (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243094211001vSDF
SecOps teams today are hyper-aware of the threats that face their network as the landscape becomes more complex and difficult to predict. Cisco's Zero Trust for workplace solution provides a security architecture based on key pillars of endpoint visibility, network segmentation and continuous trust monitoring. Cisco Catalyst Center (formerly Cisco DNA Center) provides critical insights into endpoint type and traffic patterns, automated management of configuration policies and continuous trust assessment provide NetOps and SecOps teams immense value. This session targets everyone interested to raise the level of security and trust in the campus network in a simplified and automated way, and explains the value of multiple product capabilities in Cisco Catalyst Center. The session helps to explore the various paths that you can take to achieve a Zero Trust Workplace. Explore what a system could look like with various network connectivity and services like Catalyst 9000 (C9K), Identity Services Engine (ISE) and SD-Access. Discover your optimal path based on where your network is currently and explore the benefits of each added step on your Zero Trust journey. For a more detailed coverage of the topic, we recommend visiting the four hour Technical Seminar TECOPS-2002 on Monday.

### Let's Deploy Cisco Catalyst Center Global Manager (CCGM): Single Pane of Glass for Multiple Catalyst Centers - BRKOPS-2492
Speakers: Shai Silberman (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243094660001vMKb
Are you managing multiple Cisco Catalyst Centers deployments across global sites? Then you must be facing complexities in managing multiple controllers, user logins, roles, and different workflows in UX/UI of all the controllers. The Catalyst Center Global Manager (CCGM) offers network administrators a single pane of glass view to simplify management and troubleshooting of devices and endpoints throughout the network. In this session, you will learn step-by-step how to: Deploy CCGM on VMware ESXi Integrate multiple Catalyst Center controllers into CCGM for a unified Site and Device Inventory view Monitor the health of networks, clients, devices, and SD-Access, including P1/P2/P3 issues, using CCGM Seamlessly cross-launch into Catalyst Center from CCGM without additional authentication Utilize global search to locate clients or devices by IP address, serial number, MAC address, software version, and other metadata The session will also feature live demonstrations of all these steps.

### Leveraging Tools to Successfully Migrate from UCM to Webex Calling Multi-Tenant - BRKCOL-2079
Speakers: Steven Fly (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243035070001vqzY
Moving your UCM calling platform to Webex Calling can feel like a big undertaking. However, if you follow a methodical process and leverage Webex and Cisco partner tools, you can reduce some of the work, effort and stress. In this session, we will review a 3-phase approach to move your calling from UCM to Webex Calling MT. We will highlight key activities and how you can reduce the effort or improve the process to complete them by using existing Webex tools. We will also highlight some of the key partner tools that you can use as well. ### Life of a Packet in Kubernetes: A Network Engineer's Guide - BRKCLD-3734
Speakers: Nico Vibert (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243028083001vmAe
Kubernetes networking can be difficult to reason about, especially for those used to traditional infrastructure. Traffic is routed through layers of abstraction such as services, overlays, policies and proxies, making visibility and control a challenge. This advanced session maps the full journey of a packet through a Kubernetes cluster, across nodes, and out via egress. Using Isovalent Cilium as the dataplane, we will unpack how traffic flows through technologies such as VXLAN and Geneve overlays, Gateway API, Egress Gateway, BGP routing, IPsec encryption, Direct Server Return (DSR), and multi-cluster networking with Cluster Mesh. We will also explore performance-focused features including BIG TCP and topology-aware traffic engineering. Expect live demos. Attendees will learn how to correlate packet behavior with Kubernetes constructs using Hubble, Cilium CLI, and Wireshark, gaining the insight needed to troubleshoot and optimize real-world networks. You'll leave this session with ideas to help you: trace Kubernetes traffic through encapsulation, routing, policy, and encryption layers understand the impact of topology-aware routing and load balancing decisions capture and analyze real traffic to resolve complex service connectivity issues This is an advanced session intended for network engineers, platform teams, and SREs with experience in IP networking and Kubernetes. Familiarity with routing, overlays, and load balancing is expected.

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242983962001vNzw

Translate ? Empower framework you can apply from discovery to rollout; examples of turning we should use AI here into scoped, testable changes in your existing stack; strategies to surface underheard voices so AI removes barriers; and ideas to start AI conversations grounded in friction, outcomes, and inclusion, not just features.

### MCP for DevOps: Unleashing the Power of AI and Model Context Protocol in Dev/Net/SecOps - DEVNET-3047

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243049903001vEk4

### MCP-Powered Network Guardians: Building Self-Healing Networks with Agentic AI - DEVNET-2093
Speakers: Kareem Iskander (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242944768001vQOU
What if your network could detect unauthorized changes and fix them before you even knew? In this session, see how AI agents use MCP to integrate monitoring, analysis, and automated remediation across your stack. We'll show how MCP connects systems like Splunk, Meraki APIs, NetBox, and configuration management tools into a single autonomous workflow that detects anomalies, validates against your source of truth, and applies intent-based recovery to reverse configuration drift. You'll Learn How To: Deploy AI agents that unify monitoring across Splunk, Meraki APIs, NetBox, and more Use the Protocol Bridge to create seamless automation between disparate tools Implement intent-based recovery that remediates unauthorized changes automatically

This introduction-level session will showcase how SD-WAN operations are made easier and more efficient with the introduction of new features and an enhanced user experience starting from 20.12 Release.They address customer challenges in the areas of onboarding, configuration, user interface, troubleshooting, visibility, and AI. ### Making Private Mobile Networks Mobile: Adopting Non-Terrestrial Networking with Private 5G to Create a Next-Generation Infrastructure - BRKMSI-2211
Speakers: Derick Linegar (Cisco), Matthias Falkner (Cisco), Filipe Rodrigues (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242964164001vlPB
The accelerating demand for ubiquitous, secure, and high-performance connectivity is driving innovation beyond the reach of traditional terrestrial networks. Private 5G networks have emerged as a critical enabler for enterprises seeking robust wireless infrastructure; however, their potential remains constrained by physical and geographic limitations. This white paper explores how integrating Non-Terrestrial Networks (NTN), as standardized in 3GPP Release 17, with Cisco's Private 5G-as-a-Service (P5GaaS) offering can overcome these barriers. By leveraging satellite and airborne platforms, organizations can extend the reach of private mobile networks to remote, mobile, and underserved environments. We detail the architectural integration of Cisco P5GaaS with Cisco Identity Services Engine (ISE), enabling secure, policy-driven access and unified identity management across terrestrial and non-terrestrial domains. The paper examines the benefits of adopting 3GPP R17 NTN standards, such as seamless mobility, enhanced resilience, and global coverage, and outlines key deployment scenarios spanning industries from logistics and energy, to emergency response. Through practical use cases and technical insights, this session demonstrates how Cisco's holistic approach - blending P5GaaS, Cisco ISE, and NTN - can deliver a truly mobile, next-generation infrastructure, empowering organizations to unlock new operational efficiencies and business opportunities.

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242995158001vwDe

This session explores the evolving post-quantum security landscape, industry standards, and strategies to address these challenges. Learn how Cisco is leading the charge in Post-Quantum Security with cutting-edge standards, industry-first products, and solutions to protect your networks. Discover how IOS XE enhances transport encryption, secure boot, and authentication for Enterprise Routing Products in Routing VPN and SD-WAN use cases, ensuring your network remains quantum-safe.

### Multi-Agent Security Is Not Your Traditional Security - DEVNET-2648
Speakers: Peter Bosch (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1771608142333001r6sQ
While traditional security practices mostly revolve around assessing what attackers can potentially do to, or are doing to, deployed networked applications and then remediating those problems, multi-agent application security is of another order: it is about the intent of the multi-agent application. Securing multi-agent applications is about making sure that application attackers cannot and are not derailing those multi-agent applications through direct or indirect thwarting model instructions, triggering flawed tool calling, or falsifying agentic memory. One could argue that securing a multi-agent application is akin to securing a single agent application, but it is really of a different order. In a multi-agent application multiple models, prompts, and tool calling are mixed, and any derailment of one agent or its memory can derail an entire multi-agent application. One compromised agent may poison memory, from which another agent reads its instructions when engaging with its model. That output may then be forwarded to the next agent, etc … and eventually disrupting the entire application. Thus: to detect if a multi-agent application is derailed, it is not sufficient to only look for “known vulnerabilities”. It is essential to assess what the multi-agent application is doing and is still aligned with the application goals. The “discourse” of the application must align with the intent of the application. At CL-EMEA ’26, we present a set of techniques to secure multi-agent applications. In this talk, we first show through a set of examples, how easy some applications are derailed, and what techniques we use to automatically keep the application in line with its objectives. We use techniques to automatically assess the discourse of a multi-agent application through judging of application beliefs, and to compare that with the application’s goals, and, when the application derails, we can find out where the root of the problem likely originated with active in-line detective agents testing such beliefs. In short, we provide a system for multi-agent guardrails and a means to repair those apps should they derail.

### Multi-Cast with EVPN, Segment Routing, and Traffic Engineering - BRKMPL-1123

Segment Routing (SR) is an industry-standard approach aimed at simplifying transport network, including traffic engineering and management across multiple domains. Ethernet VPN (EVPN) serves as a widely adopted overlay solution for Layer-2 extensions when L3 VPN serves as an overlay solution for IPv4 and IPV6 private services. This session offers a comprehensive exploration of multicast service delivery within EVPN and L3VPN networks. It delves into transport and services multicast delivery methods, covering both distributed protocols Multicast tree computation and controller-based approaches. Additionally, the session will focus on deploying multicast over the SRv6 transport network, providing a clear path forward for integrating multicast in SRv6 environments.

### Overlay Multicast in VXLAN EVPN (Tenant Routed Multicast - TRM) - BRKDCN-3638
Speakers: John Weston (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243043979001veKN
Data centers host servers that host various multicast applications such as collaboration, multimedia, video streaming, and conferencing. As video traffic is the highest bandwidth-consuming application in the network, it is imperative to implement architectures that most optimally forward traffic for multicast within the data center for east-west traffic and exiting/entering the data center for north-south traffic to/from external networks. This unique advanced-level session introduces Tenant Routed Multicast (TRM) routing in a VXLAN BGP EVPN fabric. The session will address the following questions: What problem does Tenant Routed Multicast address, and what are its benefits? What is an IPv4/IPv6 network architecture enabled for TRM? How do the control and data planes work in a network with TRM configured? What are the latest TRM innovations from Cisco? It is assumed the audience has a strong background in VXLAN BGP EVPN technology, MP-BGP, and fundamentals in IP multicast routing concepts such as PIM, and IGMP. You will leave this session with an introductory-level understanding of the TRM feature in VXLAN fabrics, applications, and architecture.

### Post-Quantum Hybrid PQC+QKD for Crypto Agility - BRKETI-2667
Speakers: Simón Ovsyannikov (Cisco), Xavier Azemar (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242994338001vpmJ
As the world accelerates towards post-quantum cryptography (PQC), organizations face the complex challenge of securing their networks against future quantum threats. This session, designed at Cisco Innovation Labs, will demystify the leading approaches: the mathematical rigor of PQC and the quantum physics certainty of Quantum Key Distribution (QKD), including the Bring Your Own Keys (BYOK) paradigm. We will explore how these distinct methods can be combined into resilient hybrid solutions, emphasizing practical implementation and achieving true crypto agility. Attendees will gain a clear understanding of: The fundamental principles and practical applications of PQC (e.g., ML-KEM) and BYOK (e.g., QKD, SKIP Protocol) Strategies for designing and implementing hybrid post-quantum security architectures How to configure Cisco IOS-XE devices for external key injection and ensure crypto agility within your infrastructure The session will feature a compelling demo showcasing a hybrid QKD+PQC setup, illustrating real-world crypto agility. Security features, configuration awareness, and an intermediate background in network security are recommended for attendees.

### Post-Quantum Security: Preparing Security Teams for the Cryptographic Transition - DEVNET-2130
Speakers: Oleksii Borysenko (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242993341001veiu
Quantum computers are expected to break current encryption within 5-15 years, creating urgent security challenges. NIST mandates quantum-resistant systems by 2035, with 112-bit algorithm deprecation by 2030. RSA-2048 cracking time drops from 10 years to one day with quantum computers. Network protocols are adapting: IKEv2 supports quantum-resistant keys via RFC 8784, while ML-KEM, ML-DSA integrate into TLS 1.3, SSH, and IPsec. Cisco's SKIP automates post-quantum key distribution without hardware refresh. Organizations must inventory cryptographic assets, implement crypto-agile architectures, and train teams on lattice mathematics for incident response and compliance readiness.

### Preparation Guide to Emergency Comms and Networking using Amateur Radios (HAM) - BRKGEN-1012

What happens when you can't leave your family, there are no roads, no cell, no water, no power, and you need help from first responders in the area. We examine the critical role of emergency communications (EMCOMM) with the use of amateur radio to Cisco networking. Share use cases from recent hurricanes Helene/Milton 2024, emphasizing when a disaster hits foundational communication channels are needed to bring up Network Operations and services. We will share tactical deployments to real-world success stories, this section underscores the importance of integrating amateur radio with networking technologies for public safety. The technical innovations behind IP networking over packet radio are then explored, showcasing how amateur radio operators have embraced and contributed to the field of network engineering. Finally, we turn to modern amateur radio and Cisco networking, outlining how newcomers can start, and examine practical applications of Layer 2 and Layer 3 technologies based on the OSI Model. This segment offers use cases and other alternative network communications integrate with Meraki and Cisco route switch along side Starlink and linking other CLUS sessions eg. NERV Truck.

### Red Light, Green Light: Navigating AI Security Risks in Amsterdam - AI-1362
Speakers: Jan Heijdra (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242978622001vwYM
As generative AI and agentic AI systems become critical to enterprises, understanding their security challenges is no longer optional, it's essential. This beginner friendly session breaks down the threat landscape of Generative AI and Agentic AI, covering real-world vulnerabilities and risks. We will introduce industry AI security frameworks like MITRE ATLAS and the OWASP Top 10 for LLMs, demonstrate large language model (LLM) vulnerabilities, and explain foundational protocols and security requirements behind agentic systems. Attendees will learn about the emerging risks posed by autonomous agents and the importance of securing Agent protocols. Leave with a practical roadmap for identifying, understanding, and defending against AI Threats. No prior AI expertise required.

### Rethinking Identity, Policy, and Access Control (IPAC) for Agentic AI - BRKETI-2623
Speakers: Marcelo Yannuzzi (Cisco)

Unleashing the power of agentic AI requires a new Identity, Policy, and Access Control (IPAC) model. Why? Agentic AI is not automation as we know it. It's a different beast that is disrupting current Identity and Access Management (IAM) systems, including how we define and scope what these entities are allowed to do, and safely authorize and trust them to perform tasks at unprecedented levels of speed, scale, autonomy, and requirements in terms of access controls. In this session, you will learn the main technology gaps that exist today in IAM solutions as well as new IPAC architectures and innovative techniques enabling companies to: Identify and establish trust dynamically among users, agents, and MCP Servers, including Just-In-Time (JIT) identification and Multi-Factor Verification (MFV) across different enterprise environments and domains Define policy capturing what these entities can do and not do, especially, without a Human In The Loop (HITL) Apply and enforce granular authorization controls for autonomous entities that communicate in Natural Language (NL) and operate at machine speed The session will include a comprehensive demo, pointers to start your journey on IPAC for agentic AI, and you will learn about Cisco's innovations and open source efforts in this space.

### Revolutionize Your Data Analysis with Agentic AI Agents - AI-2722

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242942588001vVBh

Discover how agentic AI can transform your data analysis workflows by autonomously delivering faster, smarter, and more actionable insights. This session will focus on teaching practical best practices for implementing agentic AI effectively in real-world environments. Enterprise service organizations often struggle to extract answers from a tangled web of data sources - CRM databases, ticketing systems, application logs, and more. The real challenge lies not in analysis, but in the manual, error-prone process of gathering and integrating this scattered information. This session demystifies that process by exploring a two-stage, multi-agentic workflow. First, an Orchestrator Agent decomposes high-level business questions into manageable sub-tasks and automates the collection of relevant data. Next, a Business Context Agent brings meaning to the raw data by applying organizational knowledge - resolving ambiguities, mapping inconsistent identifiers, and transforming disparate sources into a unified, analysis-ready dataset. This approach not only accelerates data preparation, but also ensures that insights are contextually relevant and actionable. Join us to gain actionable insights and best practices that empower your organization to harness the full potential of agentic AI as your analytical copilot, enabling faster time-to-insight and improved decision-making.

### SD-Access: Intelligent Traffic Redirection Using Security Service Insertion - BRKENS-2857
Speakers: Mahesh Nagireddy (Cisco), Saravanan V A (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243013704001v26B
As part of the enterprise customer journey towards digitization, there are multiple transitions happening. Firstly, applications are moving to cloud and secondly, proliferation of IoT endpoints which are getting connected to the network which cant host any endpoint security stack and running vulnerable SW versions. Security and risk management leaders are looking for more efficient, cost effective solutions for security orchestration and automation to try to keep up with unprecedented pace of changes within their business models, IT and OT Networks. All of these changes are putting tremendous stress on current and antiquated practices for security insertion. There are disparate models throughout the network in many cases with stateless ACL controls within the network and manual stitching of network security services with VLAN stitching to redirect network traffic through security services. In almost all cases, all traffic is treated the same and steered to security gateways for processing. This overprovisioning leads to bottlenecks. Any adds, moves or changes involve high OpEx of reconfiguration and testing of many devices throughout the network. In addition to the high cost of OpEx, there are implications of reduced time-to-value (TTV) for implementing new business services, which necessitates coordination between multiple operations groups (NetOps, SecOps, etc.). The efforts take weeks to months in some cases, usually involving planning, reviews, POCs, change management, and eventual manual provisioning. Not ideal and can result in human error. As part of this session, lets see how Security Service Insertion using Cisco Catalyst Center help to move away from physical topology-based security insertion, to intent-based security services.

### SD-WAN OMP Unlocked: From Session Bring-Up to Advanced Routing, Multicast, and L2VPN - BRKENT-3115
Speakers: Waqas Ahmad Daar (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1771608136861001rCjV
This technical session is a comprehensive exploration of the intricate workings of OMP (Overlay Management Protocol) in SD-WAN (Software-Defined Wide Area Networking). Attendees will embark on a journey to understand the entire lifecycle of OMP sessions, beginning with the establishment process, exploring different fields, and gaining insights into reading logs and executing show commands. The session includes an in-depth examination of OMP's best path selection, where attendees will explore various use cases and delve into the algorithmic aspects that drive efficient path selection. Additionally, attendees will discover how SD-WAN policies are manipulated within the OMP framework. Throughout the session, live demos will be conducted to provide attendees with real-world demonstrations of OMP routing use cases. The session also covers the SD-WAN Multicast from the OMP perspective, how control packets are exchanged to build multicast tree in SD-WAN fabric. This session also covers SD-WAN Layer-2 VPN (L2VPN) from a control-plane perspective, including how OMP establishes L2VPN point-to-point (P2P) and point-to-multipoint (P2MP) tunnels, as well as how data traffic is forwarded across L2VPN tunnels within the SD-WAN fabric. By the end of this technical deep dive session, attendees will emerge equipped with a profound understanding of OMP's architecture, routing mechanisms, and policy manipulation capabilities within SD-WAN. The knowledge gained will empower attendees to optimize network performance, streamline data transmission, and overcome complex SD-WAN challenges. Join us in this enlightening session to elevate your expertise in SD-WAN OMP and pave the way for enhanced network efficiency.

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243087964001veS9

### Secure Firewall in ACI - BRKSEC-2992
Speakers: Fabien Gandola (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243084693001vXkb
In modern data centers, it is crucial to be able to insert, deploy, and operate next-generation firewalls (NGFWs) as easily as possible. This session won't make you a service insertion ACI expert nor a Cisco Secure Firewall expert in just 90 minutes. Instead, this session aims to explain and demonstrate how Cisco Firepower Threat Defense (FTD) can offer more than just any NGFW in ACI. We will first introduce very quickly the necessary key concepts on ACI to insert an NGFW, followed by the shortest Cisco Secure Firewall introduction ever. We will then cover the most common ways to insert NGFW nowadays, focusing mostly on service graphs leveraging L3 PBR. Finally, we will hit the key part of the session with few a key points that FTD brings to the table: The advantages of leveraging clustering, especially in multipod deployments The role of dynamic objects in FTD and how they can help accelerate enforcement, especially if leveraged by Cisco Secure Dynamic Attribute Connector (CSDAC) Integration with Cisco Secure Workload using FTD, for both policy and virtual patching The ability to automatically remediate with the remediation module in Firewall Management Center (FMC) The session is targeted at customers who already using FTD in ACI and wish to improve their integration, as well as those interested in implementing ACI and looking to learn about FTD insertion capabilities.

### Secure Operations for an IPv6 Network - BRKSEC-2044
Speakers: Eric Vyncke (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243086089001v44f
Nowadays, IPv6 is in all networks as all hosts have IPv6 enabled by default. While network operators are experienced with secure operations in the IPv4 world, what are the specific challenges and techniques to be used to securely operate an IPv6 network? This session, by one author of RFC 9099, will present the main differences between IPv4 and IPv6 with respect to security, how to prevent attacks, what about extension headers, inventory, user attribution, and event correlation in a dual-stack network (from Data Center to wireless network). A good knowledge of IPv6 will be assumed.

### Secure the Network Edge against the DDoS Attacks - BRKMSI-1001
Speakers: Raja Kolagatla (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243064620001vYbd
Latest DDoS trends indicate that L3-L4 attacks have increased, with 20% year on year in Q2 2024, and the average attack size of the L3-L4 attacks doubled since 2022. Traditional DDoS solutions can't scale with the above trends, making it prohibitively expensive for Service Providers to maintain large sized DDoS Scrubbers, in more distributed in tune with evolving Networks. Service Provider networks have also become the hotbed for the threat actors, and also stand second in the targeted industry too. This serves as a wakeup call for the Service Providers, to fortify the core networks to become threat free. Cisco Secure DDoS Edge Protection is an industry's first true on-box and CFO-friendly solution, that stops DDoS attacks at the Service Provider Network Edge, with projected TCO savings up-to 83%, in comparison with traditional scrubber based solutions. Cisco Secure DDoS Edge Protection on routers detects and mitigates attacks autonomously in real time, optimizing the performance of low-latency applications. The goal of the session, is to provide the high level architecture overview of the Cisco Secure Edge Protection solution, delve deeper into the use cases, showcase the TCO savings and provide customer success stories with this new innovative solution.

### Securing AI Agents and Agentic RAG Implementations - AI-2001

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243030822001viLX

These AI implementations offer powerful capabilities but introduces new security challenges. In this presentation, you will learn about the best practices for safeguarding these intelligent systems. We will explore the unique threats facing autonomous AI agents, including vulnerabilities related to privilege access, prompt injection, data poisoning, and unauthorized access to sensitive information. We will dissect the security implications of agentic RAG implementations, where AI agents orchestrate the retrieval and utilization of external knowledge. You will gain practical insights into implementing relevant security capabilities to build resilient and trustworthy AI agent and agentic RAG systems.

### Securing Industrial Operations: From OT Asset Visibility to Zero-Trust Network Access - BRKIOT-2910
Speakers: Abubakar Maaroof (Cisco), Kartik Kamra (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243085878001v7Zb
As industrial operations become increasingly connected, every new connection expands the attack surface. Cisco integrates security directly into the industrial network fabric embedding visibility, access control, and defense as foundational capabilities. Industrial operators face two major challenges: hidden asset drift and undocumented communications that create blind spots, and overly broad remote access that heightens the risk of lateral movement and insider threats. In this session, discover how Cisco Cyber Vision delivers actionable insights by identifying vulnerabilities, prioritizing risks, and accelerating investigations through a refreshed, intuitive interface. Learn how organizations enhance visibility, streamline compliance, and make smarter decisions with integrated asset intelligence. Explore how Secure Equipment Access (SEA) brings Zero Trust principles to OT environments, enabling session-based, least-privilege access for employees and vendors. When integrated with Cyber Vision, SEA helps reduce downtime, minimize exposure, and protect both plants and remote sites. Join us to learn how to begin and how to scale your journey toward transforming industrial networks into security platforms with built-in visibility and Zero Trust Network Access (ZTNA).

### Securing Your AI Frontier: Identifying, Mitigating, and Preventing Threats Across the AI Development Lifecycle - BRKAI-1495

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242943381001v6lZ
With AI rapidly reshaping industries and recent incidents exposing new attack vectors, securing the AI development lifecycle has become an important business and compliance priority. Securing the AI Development Lifecycle: AI systems face risks not just in code, but in every stage from data collection to inference, requiring controls for data integrity, model robustness, and continuous monitoring Model Supply Chain Vulnerabilities: The AI supply chain extends beyond code to include pre-trained models, datasets, and ML frameworks, making provenance verification, dependency auditing, and artifact integrity checks essential Generative AI applications are uniquely exposed to jailbreaks, prompt injections, and model extraction, demanding defenses like adversarial testing, input sanitization, and privacy-preserving techniques We'll demo prompt injections, jailbreaks, and tampered models in action - stark examples of why every stage of the AI development lifecycle must be secured. You'll learn how to interpret validation reports that reveal model behavior in real and adversarial conditions, and leave knowing how to apply those insights to implement policy controls and guardrails that keep generative AI safe and reliable.

IOS XE Enterprise Routing platforms have long supported SR-MPLS alongside IPsec, NAT, firewall, and DPI capabilities for edge deployments. IOS XE software is now being enhanced with Segment Routing IPv6. This session will introduce SRv6 capabilities in enterprise routing products, highlight the top SRv6 use cases and solutions, and discuss the latest innovations in Cisco SD-WAN (overlay) and SRv6 (underlay) integration.

### Segment Routing in a Nutshell - CISCOU-2348
Speakers: Peter Paluch (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243023973001v4kJ
Many of us have heard about Multi Protocol Label Switching (MPLS). For almost 30 years, it has provided the foundation for a myriad of services previously unfathomable in IP networking. Great technologies stay around, adapt, and evolve. So does MPLS. Its next generation is called Segment Routing (SR). Keeping the well known functionality from its MPLS predecessor and reviving the concept of source routing, SR brings along some fascinating innovations and approaches to the packet forwarding process, pushing the boundaries yet again. This session aims to provide an introduction into the world of Segment Routing, highlight the main similarities and differences to MPLS, and offer an insight into two implementations of SR: SR-MPLS and SR-IPv6.

### Simple Leaf/Spine with a Touch of ToR - Network Designs for the Modern Data Center - BRKDCN-2967

This session will step back and review the rationale behind classic 3-tier data center design and some of the deployment history in conjunction with virtual port channel (vPC) technology. We will review the challenges that have arisen with 3-tier data center designs and how those architectures are now evolving to overlay based fabric buildouts. We will outline how the data center requirements are transitioning to accommodate changing traffic patterns based on modern application development and how that's driving overlay fabric solutions. We will introduce VXLAN EVPN as the primary overlay fabric solution being deployed in the data center and how it's best suited to modern data center use cases. We will start with single fabric VXLAN EVPN designs and expand that to multi-site data center deployments where we are engineering for high availability, fault tolerance, and optimal performance characteristics.

### Simple VXLAN/EVPN Fabric Setup with Nexus Dashboard - BRKDCN-2929

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243040803001vAcN
VXLAN provides network capabilities and architecture scale that is not possible with traditional VLANs. NDFC provides a deep and rich set of features to tackle these problems, providing automation and analytics that can drive efficiency and scalability. In this session, we will provide an overview of how NDFC can alleviate operational hurdles with the deployment of NX-OS fabrics. We will start with initially standing up a single fabric, either as a new greenfield environment or integrating a preexisting brownfield setup, in a very condensed timeframe. We will review creating a multi-fabric design, enabling external connectivity and providing layer 4-layer 7 service node stitching. We will explore configuration change control, snapshot, and rollback, and how we address compliance use cases. Finally, we will outline how intelligent analytics can be leveraged to provide advanced visibility and insights for day-to-day operations.

### Simplifying Micro-Segmentation with Group Based Adaptive Policy - BRKSEC-2264

Adopting a microsegmentation strategy effectively limits exposure by restricting the lateral movement of malware. This session will guide you through the best practices for implementing a cloud-managed operating model and illustrate how Adaptive Policy can be used to implement a Zero Trust framework for your network. The session explores a case study with real-world use cases relevant to various industries featuring practical demonstrations, including initiating cloud management on Catalyst switches, and dynamically assigning policies through Meraki Dashboard. You will also learn how to implement Adaptive Policy in hybrid environments, providing a comprehensive understanding of the cutting-edge cloud-managed microsegmentation solution.

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243022601001vSDV
Simplify day-to-day network operations by syncing Cisco Catalyst and Meraki data directly into NetBox. In this Cisco U. Theater session, we'll demonstrate how NetBox automatically syncs critical network data - including sites, devices, IPs, interfaces, and wireless networks - from Cisco controllers. See how this integration streamlines network management, enhances visibility, and lays the foundation for automation in your environment. Through a live demo, you'll learn how engineers can leverage NetBox to unify data across Catalyst and Meraki deployments, reduce manual effort, and gain actionable insights that improve reliability.

### Simplifying Operations and Upgrades on Your Nexus Switch using Modular NXOS Software - BRKDCN-2639
Speakers: Samer Theodossy (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243040324001vAN6
This session will start with a discussion of the NX-OS overall system architecture and discuss upgrade procedures and best practices on Nexus switches for both the stand-alone and ACI product lines and what the best approaches are to take. Customers want ease of operations on their systems with Cisco software, and one of the biggest areas is upgrades and changing versions of software. Cisco's goal is to focus on best-in-class high availability. In-Service Software Upgrade (ISSU) is a major table-stake feature that requires coordination and patching. Customers want the ability to choose the software components based on deployment scenario and hardware they are using and there is a need to provide a more modular software to accommodate these goals and help bring lower memory footprint to Nexus switches. We will see how the Nexus architecture lends itself to the modular/optional design and will discuss how this helps with creating an NXOS software image that is more lean and provides a smaller memory footprint. In addition, this will lead to asynchronous delivery of features/fixes which can be deployed on the system. With the introduction of feature optionality, we will discuss how this provides the flexibility to remove unused RPM packages and how to install/uninstall an optional RPM package on the system.

We need a new approach to defense that doesn't require prior knowledge of the attack to function. In this session, you will learn how Talos security researchers have been building this new approach into a detection engine for Cisco Secure Firewall called SnortML.

### Solving Global WAN Challenges with Multi-Region Fabric - BRKENT-2609

Cisco SD-WAN's Multi-Region Fabric (MRF) introduces a transformative approach to building and operating large-scale WAN architectures. MRF enables organizations to simplify deployment and management of global SD-WAN fabrics by introducing a scalable, two-level customer overlay. This architecture provides enhanced regional segmentation, centralized policy control, and operational flexibility for both enterprises and managed service providers. In this session, we'll provide a concise technology overview of MRF and examine real-world scenarios where organizations benefit from simplified deployment, improved scalability, and granular control over their SD-WAN environment. Attendees will learn how MRF streamlines the rollout of global SD-WAN fabrics, connects disjointed WAN transports within regions, facilitates efficient inter-region connectivity, and optimizes the use of regional hubs and colocation sites. ### Solving the Segmentation Puzzle with Secure Workload - BRKSEC-2161
Speakers: Jorge Quintero (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243084496001vxZc
In a world of application workloads deployed anywhere, at any time, and with multi-cloud solutions, applying network security controls is no longer a trivial task. The policy control toolset just keeps growing, with multiple enforcement points in the network to protect our application workloads using different approaches such as the host firewalls, network firewalls and SDN controllers, or cloud-based in the form of security groups. With different teams managing each policy control, and usually working in organizational siloes, there is no wonder why it often leads to inconsistent islands of policy controls across the environment. Secure Workload has been solving this puzzle, by defining a common policy model across all of these enforcement points (host-based, network-based and cloud-based) harmonizing all policy controls into an effective Zero-Trust Segmentation policy. This session will navigate through the Network/NetSec team lenses on how you can leverage Secure Workload to define a common policy model for your applications using agent and agentless approaches to protect your application workloads regardless of their form factor (baremetal, VM or container) or location (on-prem or multi-cloud).
