### Taking Away the Network Pain from Cross-Hypervisor Migrations with Isovalent Network Bridge - BRKCLD-1713
Speakers: Dan Finneran (Cisco), Dean Lewis (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243000139001vJAT
Migrating virtual machines between hypervisor platforms and datacenter boundaries can be challenging when it comes to network accessibility, routing and security. Most hypervisors provide tooling to migrate the virtual machines configuration and storage to their platform, but they don't handle the change of networking at the datacenter level. Applications rely on static IPs and traditional topologies. Re-IP address increases risk and additional distruption during migrations. Full subnet migrations typically lead to downtime. All of these are hidden barriers to migrations of VMs between hypervisors. In this session, we introduce and dive into Cloud Native Hypervisors based on Kubernetes and KubeVirt, Cloud Native Networking and Storage based on Isovalent and other popular technologies. With a focus on Isovalent Network Bridge, eBPF powered tooling that allows seemless network migrations, enabling communication between VM and Kubernetes workloads in your datacenter, regardless of where on the network they are placed or migrated to. You will learn: the concepts and abstractions when it comes to running virtual machines and containers together understanding of cloud native networking married to your datacenter connectivity migration tooling, consdierations and risks best practices when migrating virtual machines from legacy hypervisor platforms technical deep dive walk through of Cisco's latest networking products that enable network migration for VMs

### Talk to Your Network: Bringing Conversational AI to Cisco Crosswork Network Controller - DEVNET-2534
Speakers: Sujay Murthy (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242972602001v1Qr
Modern IP transport networks are growing in complexity, making traditional operational models and tooling increasingly insufficient. The Cisco Crosswork Network Controller (CNC) provides a robust platform for WAN orchestration, health monitoring, and integration with external systems. However, today's interfaces often require deep expertise - operations staff must navigate complex UIs or craft precise API calls to extract actionable insights. In this talk, we present a prototype that infuses CNC with the power of Large Language Models (LLMs) and agent-based AI. Our goal is to demonstrate how conversational AI can revolutionize network operations by: Natural Language Queries : Operations teams can ask CNC questions like "Show me links with highest utilization" or "Which links are experiencing anomalies?" - no need to know APIs or payload structures Automated Insights and Debugging : The system not only retrieves information but also provides contextual analysis, such as anomaly detection (e.g., outliers in delay or packet drops) using AI models Proactive Remediation : When issues like link flaps are detected, the AI can suggest or even drive automated remediation workflows by coordinating with the MCP Server, reducing mean time to repair We'll showcase use cases including: Retrieving performance metrics and topology visualizations conversationally Detecting and surfacing anomalies in network health data Discussing and visualizing topology structures in plain language Creating and executing remediation payloads for events like link flaps Join us to see how integrating LLMs and agents with CNC's API can accelerate troubleshooting, empower operations teams, and pave the way for self-healing networks.

### The New, QUIC Based Encrypted Protocol Stack Taking over the Internet and How to Deal with It - BRKSPM-2024
Speakers: Andreas Enotiadis (Cisco), Bart Van de Velde (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243061205001vQoo
Internet traffic is transitioning from TCP to QUIC very fast, QUIC already being 50% of total traffic and doubling every 18 months. QUIC, together with HTTP/3, DoH, and eSNI/ECH, obfuscates the traffic between applications and changes it's behaviour with applications now managing flow control and disintermediating the network. Application visibility is eliminated by this stack through TLS1.3 encryption, congestion management transitions to the application and the key assumptions SP networks are built on are rapidly being obsoleted. In this session, we will show how this protocol stack is constructed and evolving, its behaviour in terms of both visibility and flow control, and the impact it has on infrastructure elements. We will also discuss the techniques SPs can use to keep pace with it, enabling better traffic visibility, QoE monitoring and management, and efficient use of 5G spectrum and other scarce resources.

### The Power of Cisco SD-Access LISP Fabric: Simplified Deployment to Advanced Use Cases - BRKENS-2804
Speakers: Mahesh Nagireddy (Cisco), Devi Bellamkonda (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1771608130895001r8sQ
Discover how the latest advancements in Cisco SD-Access (SDA) and LISP architecture are transforming enterprise networks making them smarter, more secure, and easier to manage. This demo-rich session takes you from building your SD-Access fabric to implementing adaptive, intent-based services that meet today's evolving digital requirements. We'll explore new capabilities such as migrating Fabric sites from traditional LISP BGP to the latest LISP Pub/Sub control plane , and how this evolution simplifies scalability and control across distributed environments. Through demonstrations and examples, we'll walk through Secure Access Point onboarding , showing how Catalyst Center automates secure AP provisioning into the fabric. The session also highlights Supplicant-based Extended Node deployments , enabling switches to securely join the fabric using 802.1X, ensuring end-to-end identity, segmentation, and automated trust establishment. You will also see how Multiple Catalyst Center to ISE delivers unified identity-based access between SDA and wireless domains, ensuring consistent policy enforcement for all users and devices. You'll leave with practical deployment strategies, proven best practices, and the confidence that your SD-Access network can deliver the flexible automation, scalable control, and unified identity-driven security your enterprise needs today and tomorrow. Prerequisites: Prior SD-Access knowledge recommended. Review BRKENS-2810, BRKENS-2811, and BRKENS-2814 in the Cisco Live On-Demand Library. For foundational concepts , see BRKENS-1804 (Part 1: Fabric Deployment Fundamentals) covering LAN automation, zero-touch onboarding, ISE integration, and policy-based segmentation. For deeper technical scenarios , refer to BRKENS-2804 from CLUS San Diego (Part 2: Advanced Scenarios), which covers the LISP Pub/Sub control plane, dynamic borders, LISP extranet deployments, and multi Catalyst Center / ISE integrations.

We will explore the key components of a successful threat emulation program, including: Defining the Attack Landscape: Understanding the threat actors, their tactics, techniques, and procedures (TTPs), and aligning emulation scenarios to real-world threats Building a Robust Emulation Environment: Establishing a secure and controlled environment to conduct realistic attack simulations Selecting the Right Tools and Techniques: Leveraging a variety of tools and techniques to emulate various attack vectors, from phishing and malware to advanced persistent threats Measuring and Analyzing Results: Utilizing metrics and analytics to assess the effectiveness of security controls and identify areas for improvement Continuous Improvement: Adapting to the ever-changing threat landscape and refining emulation strategies to stay ahead of adversaries By the end of this session, attendees will gain a comprehensive understanding of threat emulation, including how to design effective emulation scenarios, select appropriate tools, and interpret results. They will be equipped with the knowledge and skills to implement a robust threat emulation program and strengthen their organization's security posture.

The C9000 series continues to evolve, keeping pace with the rapidly changing landscape of networking driven by AI, automation, and digital transformation. Compared to models based on the UADP architecture, these platforms mark a significant leap forward in capability, performance, and visibility. In this session, we'll explore how Silicon One is redefining troubleshooting. Whether you're working in the field, supporting customers, or handling escalations in TAC, you'll gain actionable strategies to: Leverage new CLI commands and hardware-assisted tools built into the ASIC Perform fast, reliable network verification, even in large-scale or distributed topologies Utilize enhanced serviceability features such as persistent logging, advanced error correlation, and deeper hardware diagnostics If you're supporting or deploying the newest Catalyst platforms, this session will boost your confidence in troubleshooting and help you unlock the full potential of Silicon One powered switching.

### Troubleshooting the Session Initiation Protocol (SIP) - BRKCOL-2143
Speakers: Paul Giralt (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243025503001vQ3W
The Session Initiation Protocol (SIP) is the de-facto standard for real-time collaboration session establishment. It is core to Cisco Webex Calling and the Cisco on-premises collaboration portfolio on products such as Unified Communications Manager (UCM) and the Cisco Unified Border Element (CUBE) as well as many 3rd party products. Having a solid understanding of the protocol is essential to understanding how to troubleshoot signaling flows across a variety of products and systems. This session will primarily focus on understanding of the protocol itself and will try to remain relatively product agnostic. The session will start with the basic format of SIP messages and how they are used to set up and tear down calls, then will dive deeper into more complex call flows. We will cover session establishment, requests and responses, media negotiation including SDP and ICE, session refresh, invoking supplementary services (hold, transfer, conference, etc...), and securing SIP with TLS and SRTP. We will also briefly cover tools that can be used to more easily parse through SIP logging information to make troubleshooting easier. After completing this session, you should have a firm understanding of how to troubleshoot a SIP call flow on any platform that supports SIP.

### Trusting AI Agents: My Game, My Rules, My Limits - AI-2661
Speakers: Marcelo Yannuzzi (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242990067001vcxs
The explosive growth of AI Agents and MCP Servers is making evident the various technology gaps that exist today to identify these entities, establish trust, define policies, and enforce controls to securely delegate the execution of tasks to autonomous entities that operate at machine speed. Agentic AI requires structural changes to existing trust models, including rethinking Multi-Factor Authentication, (re)authentication frequency, transitive authorizations, the granularity of scoping techniques, access and task sequencing controls, micro-segmentation, and more. In this session, you will learn about: How we are addressing these problems and redesigning trust models for agentic AI The need for a new semantic-centric approach supported by a new breed of Auth Servers Our contributions and efforts to bring collective thinking around these new challenges through AGNTCY (AGNTCY.org) We will cover how to generate, assign, and provide verifiable identities to AI Agents and MCP Servers, how to build trust even before attempting to connect to these entities, showcase more granular access controls, along with transitive authorizations with and without a human in the loop.

### Under the Hood of IOS-XE EVPN/VXLAN on Catalyst 9000 - BRKENS-3840

This session covers in detail packet path in the Fabric in case BUM, bridged and routed flows with verification of the Fabric states and deep explanation of the CLI outputs of the show commands for relevant components. Focus of the session is to build deep knowledge of EVPN "mechanics" on Catalyst 9000 and a clear understanding of the process of packet forwarding and related verification methods.

Would you like a deeper understanding of RF communications and its impact on wireless network design? Are you curious about both the how and why? This session will demystify the fundamental principles of RF networking and explain its role as the physical layer in 802.11 WLANs. We'll also explore the latest 6 GHz wireless regulations and their implications for next-generation Wi-Fi 6E and Wi-Fi 7 technologies. Gain the knowledge to engineer robust wireless networks capable of supporting the most demanding real-time applications in our Wireless First World.

### Understanding Wireless Security and the Implications for Secure Wireless Network Design - BRKEWN-2104
Speakers: Mark Krischer (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243080568001vWCH
This session explores secure wireless network design with a key focus on the latest Wi-Fi and WPA standards. Mobility brings unique challenges to network security, such as the need for strong authentication and support for secure fast roaming. Participants will learn how 802.11 addresses these requirements and will examine the changes brought by version 3 of the Wireless Protected Access standard, and the implications for wireless deployments. We will also discuss how Catalyst Center and Meraki Dashboard expand on wireless security standards to provide visibility with rogue access point detection and location, and advanced wireless intrusion detection and prevention. The intent is to provide a deeper understanding of wireless security capabilities from the perspective of the attacks they defend against.

### Understanding and Enhancing Voice Channels in Webex Contact Center - BRKCCT-2723

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243038538001vZa3

This session will explore best practices and effective techniques for deploying voice channels within Webex Contact Center. Attendees will gain practical insights into designing and managing voice flows, as well as configuring voice entry points with support for regional media. We'll cover number routing and presentation - including E.164 formats and the new extension-based entry points. Furthermore, the session will showcase the latest enhancements in voice flows designed to enrich the customer and agent experience. This includes features like Agent Greeting, which allows for pre-recorded messages to be played at the start of an interaction, and Whisper Announcements, which provide agents with crucial context about the caller before the call connects. We will also cover other advanced voice-related flow and routing activities, such as skill-based routing and queue management, which ensure calls are directed to the most appropriate agent, improving resolution times and customer satisfaction. Whether you are new to Webex Contact Center, or an experienced administrator seeking to optimize your current deployment, this session offers actionable guidance and practical strategies to help you deliver smooth, engaging, and consistently high-quality voice interactions, ultimately enhancing your overall customer experience.

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243069139001v0Nu
Cisco Catalyst Center (formerly Cisco DNA Center) is the central management platform and the command center for the Enterprise Campus network. Troubleshooting the Catalyst Center has mainly been performed via the CLI (Command Line Interface) and has been perceived to be difficult. There have been many enhancements added to the GUI (Graphical User Interface) recently to simplify troubleshooting and provide an enhanced view of the connectivity and the internals. Please join this session as we cover the following major topics based on customer case studies: A review of the Catalyst Center System Architecture and Catalyst Center upgrade process and troubleshooting Troubleshooting the Catalyst Center using the new tools available in the GUI and the typical scenarios when TAC would use these tools (Grafana dashboards, Diagnostic Center, System 360, System Analyzer, Enhancements in the Inventory dashboard) Troubleshooting issues commonly seen in the field

### Understanding and Utilizing Recent Enhancements to UCS for Congestion Troubleshooting - CISCOU-2255
Speakers: Ed Mazurek (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243028279001vtFV
The UCS X-Series and B-Series are in reality large converged Ethernet networks. When they connect upstream to Fibre Channel fabrics such as MDS they can be a significant point of congestion. Congestion can spread from the UCS up to the FC SAN negatively affecting performance. Because these UCS systems converge lossless FC/FCoE, RoCEv2 with regular IP/Ethernet, traffic of one type may affect the other types. This congestion is from the typical 3 causes, (slowdrain, over-utilization and bit errors) identifying and resolving congestion requires a unique understanding of the UCS systems, the points of congestion and the command sets necessary to troubleshoot. This session will be an introduction including the diagnostics recently introduced.

### Understanding how Provision and Single Sign-On works for Collaboration - BRKCOL-2166
Speakers: Paulo Jorge Correia (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242960192001v9HN
We will review best practices for bridging identity between cloud and on-premises environments, and discuss when and how to leverage different protocols and mechanisms (SCIM, LDAP, REST API) for user provisioning. We will explore how our collaboration products implement single sign-on (SSO) and the available options for both cloud and on-premises products when using client applications such as Cisco Webex. We will discuss how protocols like OAuth, SAML, and OIDC can be used to deliver single sign-on. We will explain how the user's operating system interacts with the Webex App to deliver SSO, as well as the different authentication methods requested by the identity provider (IdP).

### Unified Access and Aggregation with Cisco Routed PON - BRKSP-1169
Speakers: Andy Moyer (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243066234001vNJo
Service providers are heavily investing in broadband deployments using passive optical network (PON) technology to cater unprecedented demands of end users. Billions of dollars have been funded by government organizations to bring broadband to everyone. With 10 gigabit symmetrical PON (XGS-PON), service providers are looking to converge business, mobile backhaul, and residential services onto a common platform and architecture. Cisco's routed PON solution reduces the footprint and power of the optical line terminal (OLT) to a small form-factor pluggable (SFP) that can be hosting in Cisco's NCS-based platforms running IOS-XR. Cisco NCS platforms are widely deployed as access and aggregation, and integrating the XGS-PON pluggable brings tremendous operations and cost advantages. In this session, you will learn the different components of the XGS-PON system and its integration into Cisco's converged SDN transport using segment routing and EVPN. The session will cover the shift in PON technology and deep dive into Cisco routed PON with both residential and business use cases. We will keep the session interactive with live demos showcasing the deployment and benefits of the solution.

### Unified CM Security Architecture: Technical Foundations for Deployment - BRKCOL-2226
Speakers: Matthew Jordy (Cisco), Scott Kiewert (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242959803001vL6g
Historically, securing Cisco Unified Communications Manager (Unified CM) deployments involved complex processes like Certificate Authority Proxy Function (CAPF) enrollment, managing client certificates (LSC), and explicit Certificate Trust Lists (CTL/ITL). While effective, this approach often led to significant administrative overhead, fragile trust models, and challenges in adapting to modern cloud-centric environments. This session dives into the evolution of Unified CM security, showcasing standards-based architecture designed for "always-on" security with reduced complexity. The session will also explore how the transition from traditional methods to a robust framework leveraging OAuth 2.0, Public Key Infrastructure (PKI) X.509 certificate chain validation, and streamlined certificate management is reshaping UC security. Key topics covered will include: API Protection with OAuth 2.0: Understand how OAuth has become the cornerstone for securing all client-facing APIs across soft clients and desk phones. Learn about authorization flows, access and refresh tokens, and the benefits of this optimized security model Client and Device Trust through PKI: Discover standard PKI X.509 certificate chain validation for trust establishment. Topics include managing certificate trust stores and trust lists (CTL/ITLs), incorporating enterprise CAs, Online Certificate Status Protocol (OCSP), and certificate onboarding with Enrollment over Secure Transport (EST) Simplified Device Onboarding and Provisioning: Explore phone onboarding methods, including Activation Code Onboarding (on-premises and Mobile Remote Access (MRA)) and Zero-Touch Onboarding (on-premises). Learn how these methods securely deliver OAuth tokens and trust anchors, enabling seamless mobility between on-premises and MRA Certificate Management and Encryption: Delve into certificate count reduction through multi-server (SAN) certificates and certificate reuse and TLS 1.3 support Attendees will leave with an understanding of how to implement a secure Unified CM environment, significantly reducing administrative overheads and enhancing security.

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242962947001vSgC

Explore practical lessons learned on deployment architectures, GPU utilization, advanced power and cooling solutions, and optimized networking configurations. This session will also cover planning for scalability and future expansion to support next-generation AI workloads and traditional applications alike. Attendees will gain expert guidance on accelerating their Cisco Unified Edge hardware deployments with real-world examples and engineering alliance insights, empowering them to build future-proof, high-performance edge infrastructures tailored for future-ready AI edge infrastructure.

Accelerate incident resolution using AI-ready, agentic workflows and correlated alerts across domains. Gain end-to-end visibility with out-of-the-box dashboards and the new ITSI Content Pack for unified network health. page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243013301001vdkt
Hybrid networks don't have to mean hybrid management headaches. ### Unlocking Circular Design: Cisco's Award-Winning Blueprint - PSOGRN-1934
Speakers: Ajay Ranjith Vempati (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242949465001vyea
The information and communications technology (ICT) industry faces escalating demands for sustainable practices amid increasingly complex global regulations. This session will explore Cisco's comprehensive blueprint for embedding circular design throughout its product and packaging development processes, demonstrating how this approach can support your sustainability goals and address regulatory needs. Learn how our bold goal,100% of new products and packaging incorporating Circular Design Principles by fiscal year 2025, is achieved through developing processes, tools, and a culture that drive environmental benefits, business value, and competitive advantage. You'll leave this session with ideas to help you: Understand Cisco's journey in embedding circular design across its entire supply chain, from material selection to end-of-life management. Learn about targeted initiatives to drive more sustainable material use, including sourcing recycled plastic and reducing foam in packaging. Discover how a robust circular supply chain strategy can address business challenges, enhance sustainability goals, and create long-term value, particularly in the context of evolving global compliance. This session is designed for business leaders, sellers, engineers, and anyone interested in the strategic implementation of circular economy principles.

Attendees will explore various processors, understand configuration best practices, and discover how telemetry data is processed and represented in the the OpenTelemetry Protocol (OTLP) before it's sent to a backend. This session will equip you with the knowledge and tools to harness the full potential of telemetry processing on the edge with OpenTelemetry. Don't miss the opportunity to transform your telemetry strategy and gain a robust framework for future-proof observability.

### Ushering in a New Era of 5G Innovation with Cisco's Multi-Use Case Mobility Services Platform (MSP) - BRKSPM-2341
Speakers: David Perez Gil (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243061804001vSmd
Today there are hundreds of mobile core networks around the world that are individually deployed, configured, integrated and operated with almost the same functionality, in hundreds of permutations. A radical new approach is required to drive adoption and monetization of new technologies such as 5G stand-alone (SA) with the flexibility required by both Service Providers and Enterprises. Cisco's multi-tenant Mobility Services Platform (MSP) has been designed to address this challenge and allow Service Providers and Enterprises alike to benefit from state-of-the-art tech practices to deliver growth and develop new business models. In this session, you will learn about Cisco's Mobility Services Platform (MSP) and how its "as-a-Service" approach differs from traditional on-prem and managed services models. You will also discover key real-life use cases across multiple domains including private 5G, IoT, B2B, MVNE/O, FWA, etc and the way these use cases are enabled through modern API-driven programmability in line with TM Forum specifications, combined with an AnyG Converged Core and simplified operations. Understanding of Mobile Packet Core technologies is recommended.

### Using Cisco SD-WAN Infrastructure as Code: a Fully Automated Approach to Migrate 500 Routers - CSSETI-1408
Speakers: Sebastian Lehminger (Aumovio SE), Leonardo Lopez (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242969536001vjvz
Continental Group is set to undergo its most far-reaching transformation in over 150 years of company history - splitting its business and IT Infrastructure into three independent group sectors in 2025. Among them, one introduced a fresh identity under the name Aumovio. For the IT project team this meant that in just a few months two new SD-WAN Fabrics had to be set up and configured followed by the fully automated migration of 500 routers into these new overlays. The total combined migration time window spanned just 15 hours for all routers. This fast-paced WAN split was only possible by leveraging Cisco SD-WAN Infrastructure as Code (IaC) technology, ensuring a consistent, scalable migration at minimum risk for the affected 24/7 production and development sites. This session will explore the journey of creating the required architecture, data models and pipelines required to make the fully automated migrations a success. These activities were carried out in partnership with Cisco CX services. Key takeaways from this session include in deep insights on how to enhance the Cisco SD-WAN experience by using the power of Infrastructure as Code to greatly speed up repetitive tasks and reduce human error to zero. This real-world scenario also shows the potential of reducing operational efforts and costs leveraging Automation and Standardization.

### Using Cisco's Integrated AI Security and Safety Framework to Navigate the Algorithmic Abyss - AI-2903
Speakers: Amy Chang (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242942395001vQC5
Rapid adoption of AI technologies has greatly expanded the attack surface and introduced novel safety and security risks to entities worldwide. This talk shares insight on the top threats across the AI lifecycle, mitigations with open-source tooling and frameworks, and key lessons going forward. This talk centers on Cisco's newly released Integrated AI Security and Safety Framework: the world's first lifecycle-aware, extensible, operationalizable AI framework and taxonomy. With over 150+ attack techniques catalogued, the AI Security Framework helps contextualize AI security and safety threats during model development and deployments, as well as in model context protocol (MCP), agentic, and model supply chain scenarios. This framework gives executives, security leaders, and engineers a shared language to understand AI risk and mitigate emerging threats as AI technology continues to scale and evolve.

### Using SRv6 to Unify Wireless Networks and eBPF to Extend SR to the Application - BRKSPG-2046
Speakers: Andreas Enotiadis (Cisco), Bart Van de Velde (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242952536001v3A1
Wireless networks, especially in field conditions such as defence and critical communication scenarios, are by nature denied, degraded and intermittent. In this session, we will discuss how we leverage segment routing v6 as a common unifying layer across multiple wireless infrastructures such as 5G, WiFi, CURWB and satellite. We will show how SRv6 can unify the various disparate technologies into a common IP layer that enables application intent and constraints to be dynamically reflected in the network path. We will also show how we can leverage technologies such as eBPF and Cilium to extend the SRv6 network all the way to the application through APIs, and how this is applied to the defence and critical communication space.

### Using Terraform to Automate your Meraki Deployment - DEVNET-2080
Speakers: Michael Lipsey (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243056386001vCCC

The challenge when you have many networks in one organization or multiple organizations, is that even with the ability to use templates and clone networks - to manage, configure you might end up with many templates that unintentionally introduces complexity and template sprawl. Using Terraform, you can leverage the best of both worlds, configure and deploy through the Dashboard API to get each network exactly the way you want it while also maintaining the ease of use and operational simplicity of the Meraki Dashboard. In this session, we will walk through how to do just that. We'll show benefits of automation via terraform vs the standard capabilities in the Meraki Dashboard.

### What is Cisco Doing in Quantum? An Introduction to Quantum Mechanics, Computing, and Networking - BRKETI-1401
Speakers: Tim Szigeti (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243060616001vbid
Cisco is making a major R&D investment in quantum networking, as future communication systems equipped with quantum networks will open new possibilities in several technology domains, including distributed quantum computing, secure communications, global clock synchronization, high-accuracy sensors, and more. This is an introductory session to the science and technologies that enable quantum networking. This session will begin with a brief introduction to quantum mechanics, with a focus on quantum bits (qubits) and some of their special properties, including superposition, entanglement, and no-cloning (Note: this discussion will include some mathematical concepts and proofs). This will be followed by an overview of quantum computing, to better understand the direct impact and demands this emerging technology is already having on network communications and security. Finally, we'll bring it all together to outline the building blocks, opportunities, and challenges of building quantum networks, as well as highlight what Cisco is already doing in this exciting space.

### What the Forensics. Exploring Cisco XDR Forensics and Cisco Orbital: One Incident, Two Stories - BRKSEC-2081

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243020082001vKxW
In the evolving landscape of cybersecurity, speed and precision are critical in responding to threats. Cisco offers two powerful investigative tools - XDR Forensics and Orbital - each serving distinct roles in digital forensics, incident response, and threat hunting. This session explores how these tools differ in scope, function, and outcome. While XDR Forensics provides rapid snapshots of endpoint activity tied directly to incidents, Orbital delivers deep, on-demand queries and scripts into system state using live telemetry. We'll follow the same incident through both tools, revealing how each tells its own story and uncovers unique insights. Real-world use cases will highlight where each tool shines, how they complement each other, and when to leverage one versus the other. Whether you're responding to an incident, or proactively hunting threats, understanding these tools- capabilities will empower more informed, efficient decision-making.

page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243101158001vL2p
In the AI era, ambition alone isn't enough--organizations win by turning AI into real, measurable outcomes. Cisco delivers the critical infrastructure that makes this possible, embedding security into the fabric of the network, enabling intelligent operations, and empowering teams to confidently adopt agentic AI at scale. In this keynote, Cisco leaders, customers, and industry experts will reveal the latest breakthroughs powering AI-ready data centers, future-proofed workplaces, and digital resilience--including solutions designed to meet Europe's growing need for data sovereignty. You'll see live demos of game-changing innovations, from full-stack AI infrastructure and security fused directly into the network to agentic operations that transform how you run your business. If you're shaping your organization's technology strategy, this is the keynote you can't afford to miss.

### When to Build (and Not Build) Your Own Agent: Deterministic vs. Probabilistic Automation with Cisco XDR and Meraki - DEVNET-2193
Speakers: Christopher Van Der Made (Cisco), Ryan Burton (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243053313001vLjV
Automation is evolving from static, rule-based logic to dynamic, agentic intelligence. But not every workflow needs an agent, and not every agent should act on its own. In this session, Engineering Product Managers from Cisco XDR and Meraki will show how Cisco's unified automation engine lets engineers design workflows that range from deterministic actions to probabilistic, reasoning-driven agents. This low-to no-code automation engine requires zero lines of code to build an agent and comes with native integrations for Azure OpenAI and OpenAI APIs. You'll learn when to use simple deterministic automation, without any AI, when to use Ask AI prompts, and when to safely build an Agentic workflow that can analyze, iterate, and tool-call across systems. If you've ever wondered where human intent should end and machine reasoning should begin, this session will show you how to find that line, and make it work for you. Expect live demos, practical design patterns, and real lessons from the field on how to balance control and autonomy in modern security and network automation across Cisco platforms. The session is aimed at SecOps and NetOps engineers, automation architects, and developers looking to apply AI-driven automation responsibly.

Speakers: Antonio Piepoli (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242945561001vxOu
Anyone that has approached infrastructure as code and Terraform at a certain point, has had issues with the Terraform state. ### Why Does the Enterprise Need the Cisco Secure AI Factory with NVIDIA? - PARAI-1803
Speakers: Danny Kiernan (NVIDIA)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242977822001vvjn
Enterprises are witnessing a rapid evolution of AI-from perception-based models to generative AI, now advancing to agentic AI capable of reasoning, extending into physical AI. The rise of agentic AI and reasoning models impacts all enterprise lines of business including IT, customer experience, product design, supply chain, etc. Implementing agentic AI in production requires a different approach to address the complexities of the data center infrastructure, software stack, and security, to be able to produce intelligence at scale. Such demands necessitate an "AI Factory" that offers superior TCO, is energy efficient, manifesting via a validated accelerated computing full stack architecture to deliver fastest time to business value, with security embedded at each layer. Join this session and hear how the Cisco Secure AI Factory with NVIDIA delivers the foundational AI platform for all enterprise AI needs. Don't miss seeing the Cisco Secure AI Factory with NVIDIA come to life with AI demos in the NVIDIA booth.

### Why You Shouldn't Fear Upgrading Your ACI Fabric: The Handbook - BRKDCN-2910
Speakers: Takuya Kishida (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243044636001vXKd
The Cisco Application Centric Infrastructure (ACI) architecture provides a single dashboard to manage hundreds of switches within your data center. This allows for a simple and seamless upgrade workflow, since everything can be automated or initiated from one place: the APIC controller. However, without knowing what happens "under the hood" when an upgrade is started, planning and executing a successful ACI upgrade may feel more like "shooting from the hip." This session aims at removing that doubt by providing a concise overview of what is happening to the APICs and switches as they undergo a software upgrade. Furthermore, this session will cover best practices and provide tools you can leverage to do "health checks" and audits of the pre-upgrade environment. This ensures that known issues are caught beforehand rather than during the upgrade, thus mitigating failure and enabling success. With this knowledge, you will have the utmost confidence in how to perform your ACI upgrade successfully, on time, and without heartburn.

### Wi-Fi 7 Deployment and Migration: What You Need to Know - BRKEWN-2025
Speakers: Anand Gurumurthy (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243079868001vZtm
Wi-Fi standards continuously evolve to cater to the needs of the latest applications and use cases. The next generation of Wi-Fi, i.e Wi-Fi 7, has created a lot of buzz, and is set to enhance wireless connectivity with unprecedented speeds, ultra-low latency and very high efficiency. As this next-generation standard emerges, organizations must be prepared to leverage its full potential. This session will explore the key advancements of Wi-Fi 7, that includes 4K QAM, 320 MHz channel width, multi-link operation, preamble puncturing, Multi Resource Units, and how these features will impact network performance, scalability and security. We'll also discuss the necessary infrastructure updates, security updates and strategies to ensure a smooth transition. Join us to discover how Wi-Fi 7 can elevate your network capabilities and learn about the steps you need to take to stay ahead.

### Your AI Agents Don't Talk to My AI Agents - Let's Fix That - BRKETI-1009
Speakers: Guillaume De Saint Marc (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243060005001veZp
Everyone is building AI agents - but almost no one is building the Internet they need to run on. To unlock the full potential of this new digital workforce, organizations are moving from single agents to networks of specialized agents. But fragmentation is becoming the biggest blocker: incompatible frameworks, closed runtimes, and no shared way for agents to discover, trust, and communicate across environments. In this session, Outshift by Cisco introduces the Internet of Agents and the role of AGNTCY.org , a Linux Foundation open-source project building the foundational infrastructure for agent-to-agent collaboration at scale. We'll cover the four core AGNTCY technologies - agent directory, agent identity, SLIM messaging, and observability - and demonstrate practical multi-agent use cases with demo and shared code .

### Your Kick-Start for the Sustainability Journey - PSOGRN-1087
Speakers: Colin Seward (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242949083001vSla
Sustainability is no longer a side conversation - it's central to how we design, deliver, and operate technology. In this session, join Cisco's Chief Sustainability Office for an introduction to Cisco's sustainability vision and strategy, and explore how we're driving meaningful impact across our business and with our customers. We'll break down the fundamentals of greenhouse gas (GHG) accounting and how Cisco measures and manages emissions across our entire value chain, from our own operations to the use of our products in the field. Learn how circular design principles are shaping the next generation of hardware, improving our packaging, and reducing waste. We'll also share how we're advancing energy management, lifecycle assessments, and product carbon footprints. Whether you're new to sustainability or looking to deepen your impact, this session will provide a clear view of where Cisco is headed - and how we can work together to build a more sustainable digital future.

### Your Network is Cloud-Managed. Why Isn't Your Physical Security? - PSOENS-1034
Speakers: Harshdeep Singh (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770243012309001vggw
Your Cisco Enterprise Network is designed for security, agility, and cloud management. However, the hundreds or thousands of video surveillance cameras connected to it can introduce significant challenges. Traditional video surveillance systems often rely on complex on-premise infrastructure, leading to management burdens and unquantified security risks that IT teams find difficult to control or observe. It's time to address this critical gap. Introducing Cisco MV cloud-managed physical security, a solution powered by your existing network. This approach provides seamless integration, enhanced visibility, and robust security by leveraging your network infrastructure. It simplifies management, mitigates potential vulnerabilities, and fosters a new level of collaboration between IT and physical security teams like never before. Join us to explore how you can transform your legacy infrastructure with a secure, agile, and intelligently managed solution, powered by Cisco.

### Zero Trust Provision and AVoIP Accessories of Collaboration Devices - BRKCOL-2165
Speakers: Paulo Jorge Correia (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242960001001vbpb
As collaboration devices, such as IP phones, video endpoints, and AVoIP accessories, proliferate across enterprise networks, secure and efficient provisioning becomes paramount. We will explore key considerations for AVoIP accessories and the types of topologies supported by Cisco. In the era of cloud-first strategies and hybrid work, organizations must deploy these devices rapidly, while rigorously enforcing security to prevent unauthorized access and data breaches. This session delves into best practices for implementing Zero Trust principles when provisioning collaboration devices, emphasizing automated, policy-driven workflows that minimize administrative overhead. We will review practical integrations with network access control solutions like Cisco ISE and discuss real-world scenarios where Zero Trust architectures protect against evolving threats. ### Zero Trust for the Connected Branch with Cisco Access Manager - BRKSEC-2352
Speakers: Daniel Alder (Cisco Systems, Inc.), Rossi Rosario Burgos (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242947530001vG1b
As branch environments rapidly evolve into interconnected and dynamic digital ecosystems, traditional security models struggle to keep pace with the complexity and dynamic nature of modern operations. This session demonstrates how Cisco Access Manager can transform branch security from a complex burden into a streamlined competitive advantage through best practices, real-world use cases, and hands-on demonstrations. In this session, you'll learn how to implement Zero Trust principles across diverse branch environments - from retail stores to remote offices - without compromising user experience or operational efficiency. Using a retail deployment as a practical use case, you will learn how to leverage the Meraki Dashboard for policy orchestration and contextual access controls, making enterprise-grade security accessible and manageable at scale.

### eBPF for Incident Response: Live Forensics Without Reboots - DEVNET-2135
Speakers: Kyle Winters (Cisco)
page: https://www.ciscolive.com/on-demand/on-demand-library.html#/session/1770242973201001vYqm
eBPF is transforming how security teams perform live incident response on Linux systems - without reboots, re-imaging, or intrusive agents. This session explores how to use eBPF to trace system activity at the kernel level, collect high-fidelity telemetry, and detect stealthy threats such as rootkits and unauthorized network activity in real time. Attendees will walk through hands-on use cases using tools like BCC, BPFTrace, and Tetragon to trace syscalls, inspect socket connections, identify anomalies that traditional tools might miss, and more. Designed for security engineers, DevSecOps practitioners, and platform teams with basic Linux and scripting experience, this session highlights the power of eBPF as a lightweight, real-time forensic sensor.
